# Parity contract: settings and accounts

Read from octo-player origin/main 0f11df0f. Every item cites the file and line at that commit; paths are relative to the repo root.

Parts, in order: settings front page, search and shared rows; Playback; Appearance, Library and Lyrics; Server and sync; Shared links and Radio stations; Streaming and downloads, Audio quality and Scrobbling; Backup, About and What's new; Family; Family join and hand-over; Octo admin and Import; system entries (launcher shortcuts, open with, deep links, media notification, Android Auto). Where two pages meet, an item can appear in both parts on purpose.

## Settings: how it is reached and how its pages stack

- [x] Settings is the fourth and last tab of the floating bar, labelled "Settings", with the icons `OctoIcons.Settings` (resting) and `OctoIcons.SettingsSelected` (selected) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/BottomBar.kt:84-87, app/src/main/res/values/strings.xml:6) (iPhone: SF Symbols gearshape and gearshape.fill)
- [x] The Settings tab keeps a back stack of its own that starts at the Settings front page (`SettingsRoute`); every page opened from Settings is pushed onto that same tab's stack (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:137-142, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:166)
- [x] Each tab keeps its pages' state and view models while another tab is in front, so going back to Settings finds it as it was left (search text, scroll, open page) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:151-165)
- [x] Tapping the Settings tab while it is already selected pops the tab back to its front page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:207-212)
- [x] Back on the Settings front page (tab stack of one) goes to the Home tab instead of leaving the app (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:280-281) (iPhone: a swipe in from the left edge on the front page goes to Home)
- [x] A tapped family notice, or an opened family link, switches to the Settings tab and pushes the Family screen (`FamilyOpen.Family`) or the family join form (`FamilyJoinRoute(familyAppLink(link))`) on top of the Settings stack (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:167-181) (iPhone: a notice pushes Family on the Settings stack; a family link opens the sign-in sheet in join mode)
- [x] A list shared to Octo from another app switches to the Settings tab and pushes Import, unless Import is already the top page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:182-189)
- [x] A settings page route carries the page and an optional `highlight` (the id of the row a search result pointed at) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/Routes.kt:13-14)
- [x] Opening a category: Sound opens its own page (`SoundRoute`, any highlight is dropped); every other page opens `SettingsPageRoute(page, highlight)` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:124-126)
- [x] `SettingsPageScreen` shows, per page: Playback page, Sound screen (stand-in should a stored route name Sound), Appearance, Library, Server (gets `onOpen` to push further pages), Streaming (gets `onOpen`), Audio quality, Lyrics, Scrobbling, Backup, About (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:333-350)
- [x] The pages in front page order with their exact titles: "Playback", "Sound", "Appearance", "Library", "Server and sync", "Streaming and downloads", "Audio quality", "Lyrics", "Scrobbling", "Backup and restore", "About" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:10-24)
- [x] The artwork glow area "Settings" covers: the Settings front page, every settings page, Sound, sign in, edit connection, the server form, Octo admin, Import, Family, Family join, Shared links and Radio stations; a page not in any area (for example History, Library health) gets no glow (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientSource.kt:48-63)
- [x] Pages are pushed with the push motion and popped with the pop motion; a tab change uses the tab motion; predictive back uses the gesture motion (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:269-278) (iPhone: the system navigation stack's push, pop and edge swipe; tabs fade into each other)

## Settings front page

### Layout

- [x] The front page is one vertical scrolling column with 20dp between its blocks; top padding is the status bar plus 8dp, bottom padding is the navigation bar plus 120dp (clears the floating bar) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:133-141, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:23-29)
- [x] A large page title "Settings" in the display style, 20dp side padding, 8dp above, no extra space below it on this page (`padding(bottom = 0.dp)` on top of the title's own 16dp) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:142, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:31-39)
- [x] Order under the title: the account card, the search field, then either the search results (when the typed text is not blank) or the four category groups (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:142-149)
- [x] The typed search text survives rotation and process death (`rememberSaveable`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:132) (iPhone: kept in the scene's storage)
- [x] A clock ticks every 30,000 ms so "Synced 5 min ago" style lines re-read the time on the front page while it is open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:87-88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:214-221)

### Account card

- [x] The account card is one raised group (no title) with hairlines inset 66dp (under the text, past the icon tile) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:166, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:362-363)
- [x] Each account row: whole row is a button, min height 56dp, padding 16dp sides and 12dp top and bottom, 16dp between icon tile, text and chevron; title in body style, primary colour, one line with ellipsis; each extra line in caption style, one line with ellipsis; a 20dp muted chevron at the end (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:223-243) (iPhone: a long server name wraps instead of being cut)
- [x] While the saved sign-in is still being read (`SessionState.Loading`) no server row is shown; the phone row still shows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:167-168) (iPhone: the kept servers are read before the first frame, so there is no loading state)
- [x] Signed out with no kept servers: cloud icon, title "Connect a music server", one muted line "Add the music on your own server"; tapping opens the sign-in form (`SignInRoute`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:169-175) (iPhone: the sign-in form opens as a sheet)
- [x] Signed out with at least one kept server: cloud icon, title "Your servers", one muted line "Signed out. Sign in to one, or add another."; tapping opens the Server and sync page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:176-184)
- [x] Signed in: cloud icon, title is the server's name (the label given to it trimmed, else the host of its main address, else the address without a trailing slash) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:185-196, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:79-80, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:26-27)
- [x] Signed in, first line: the username in the secondary text colour, or "Signed in with a key" when the username is empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:198)
- [x] Signed in, second line: the sync problem text in the error colour when the last sync failed; otherwise the server status in the muted colour (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:188-199)
- [x] The account card's server status shows "Connected at home" or "Connected away" only when the signed-in connection has a home address; with one address no place is said (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:188-193)
- [x] The "Synced ..." part uses the last sync only when it belongs to the signed-in server (`last.sourceId == session.sourceId`, the source id is from the main address); otherwise it reads "Not synced yet" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:187, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:72-74) (iPhone: matched by the kept server's id)
- [x] Tapping the signed-in row opens the Server and sync page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:201)
- [x] Always a second row for the phone: phone icon, title from the phone line (see summaries: "{n} songs on this phone" with access, "Allow access to the music on this phone" without), no extra lines; tapping opens the Library page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:205-210)
- [x] The phone row's song count is the catalogue's track count for the phone's own source (`DEVICE`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:113)
- [x] Icon tile used on account and category rows: 34dp square, 10dp corners, accent colour at 14% behind a 20dp accent-tinted icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:346-360)

### Search field

- [x] A glass text field with 16dp side padding and placeholder "Search settings" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:245-255)
- [x] The keyboard's action key is Search; pressing it only puts the keyboard away (results already show as you type) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:252-254)
- [x] With nothing typed the field's end shows a muted 20dp search icon with no accessibility label (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:257-258)
- [x] With text typed the end shows a 20dp close icon, accessibility label "Clear search", a button that empties the text (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:259-266)
- [x] Text that is only spaces counts as empty: the categories stay shown (`query.isNotBlank()`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:145)

### Search results

- [x] Results replace the four category groups and update on every keystroke (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:145-149)
- [x] Results sit in one untitled group; when nothing matches the group holds the note "No settings match." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:271-280)
- [x] Each result is an action row: title = the setting's title, helper line = where it lives ("{page title} · {section}" or just the page title when it has no section), with a chevron (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:276-278, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:37-38)
- [x] Tapping a result opens the setting's page with that setting's id as the highlight, so its row lights up (Sound results open the Sound page without a highlight) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:277, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:124-126)
- [x] Result rows themselves are never highlighted (they pass no entry) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:277)

### Category groups

- [x] Group "Listening" (rows with icons, 66dp hairline inset): Playback (icon Playback, line = playback summary), Sound (icon Sound, line = sound summary), Appearance (icon Appearance, line = appearance summary) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:305-313)
- [x] The Appearance line passes "live background" only when the setting is on and the phone can draw it (`liveBackground && LiveBackgroundSupported`, Android 13, API 33, and later) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:308-312, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/MeshBackground.kt:116)
- [x] Group "Your music": Library (icon Library, line = library summary), Server and sync (icon Cloud, line = server line), Streaming and downloads (icon Download, line = streaming summary), Audio quality (icon AudioQuality, fixed line "At home, away, on Wi-Fi and on mobile data") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:314-319)
- [x] The Server and sync line: "" (no line drawn) while the sign-in is loading; "Not connected" when signed out; when signed in, the server status with no home or away part ("Syncing…", or "Synced {time} ago", or "Not synced yet"), using the last sync only when it belongs to the signed-in server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:299-303) (iPhone: no loading state, as the kept servers are read before the first frame)
- [x] Group "Lyrics and history": Lyrics (icon Lyrics, line = lyrics summary from "Find lyrics online" and "Keep the screen on"), Scrobbling (icon Scrobbling, line = scrobbling summary) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:320-323)
- [x] Group "This app": Backup and restore (icon Backup, fixed line "Settings, playlists, likes and favorites"), About (icon Info, line "Version {version name}", or "Update ready: {version}" once an update is downloaded and ready) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:324-330, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:111-114) (iPhone: "Update ready" once a newer release has passed its signed manifest check)
- [x] A category row: icon tile, then title (body style) with the summary under it (caption, muted, one line, ellipsis), then a 20dp muted chevron; an empty summary draws no line; the whole row is a button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:331-344)
- [x] Category lines read live values while the front page is shown (each flow is held while subscribed, released 5,000 ms after the page stops watching) with these starting values before the first read: player prefs default, stream prefs default, offline prefs default, keep screen on true, ListenBrainz prefs default, song count 0, last sync none (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:103-121) (iPhone: the lines read observable stores, so they follow every change while shown)

## Front page summary lines (exact rules)

- [x] Every summary line joins its parts with " · " (space, middle dot, space), drops absent parts, and keeps at most the first 3 parts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:19-27)
- [x] Counts read "1 {one}" for exactly one, else the number with locale grouping (`%,d`, so "2,357") and the plural word (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:29)
- [x] A stream quality reads "{kbps} kbps" (320, 256, 192, 128) or "Original" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:31-32, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/StreamQuality.kt:5-11)
- [x] A cache size reads "Off" or "{whole GB} GB" (1, 2, 5, 10) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:34-35, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:20-26)
- [x] Playback line, part 1: "Crossfade {crossfadeSeconds} s" when crossfade is on, else "Gapless" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:37-38)
- [x] Playback line, part 2: the speed label (for example "1.25x", "2x", "0.75x", "1.15x") only when pace is not the default; pace is default only when speed is 1, keep pitch is on and the pitch shift is 0 semitones, so speed 1 with a pitch change shows "1x" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:39, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:100-101, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:60-71)
- [x] Speed label format: speed rounded to hundredths; whole numbers print with no decimals ("2x"), a tenth prints one decimal ("1.5x"), otherwise two decimals ("1.25x"), then "x" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:60-71)
- [x] Playback line, part 3 and 4: "Autoplay" when autoplay is on, "Skip silence" when skip silence is on; with the three-part cap "Skip silence" drops off when crossfade or gapless, a speed and Autoplay are all present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:40-42)
- [x] Playback line with fresh defaults (crossfade off, 8 s, speed 1, autoplay on, skip silence off) reads "Gapless · Autoplay" (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummariesTest.kt:24, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:49-64)
- [x] Sound line: "Equalizer off" when the equalizer is off; "Equalizer on, {preset}" otherwise, where preset is the graphic preset's name, or "Custom" for a curve changed by hand or free filters; with a headphone correction picked: "Equalizer on, {preset}, with {correction name}" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/sound/SoundEdits.kt:120-130, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/sound/SoundEdits.kt:32)
- [x] Appearance line, part 1 by glow strength: Off "No glow", Subtle "Subtle glow", Rich "Rich glow" (default strength is Subtle) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:44-49, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:18-20)
- [x] Appearance line, part 2: "Live background" when the live background is on and supported (default on) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:50, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:42)
- [x] Library line: "Music on this phone not allowed yet" whenever access is anything but granted (not asked, denied, denied for good) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:53-54, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:50)
- [x] Library line with access: "{n} songs" ("1 song"), then "{n} folders left out" ("1 folder left out") only when at least one phone music folder is excluded (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:55-56)
- [x] Phone line (account card): with access "{n} songs on this phone" ("1 song on this phone"), else "Allow access to the music on this phone" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:59-61)
- [x] Time ago: under 1 minute "Just now"; under 1 hour "{minutes} min ago"; under 24 hours "{hours} hr ago"; under 48 hours "Yesterday"; else "{days} days ago"; whole units rounded down; a clock that moved back counts as 0 ("Just now") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:63-73)
- [x] Synced line: "Synced " plus the time ago with its first letter lowercased ("Synced just now", "Synced 5 min ago", "Synced yesterday") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:75-76)
- [x] Server status: while syncing it is only "Syncing…" (with the single ellipsis character); otherwise "Connected at home" or "Connected away" when a place is given, then the synced line or "Not synced yet" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:78-91)
- [x] Streaming line: "Wi-Fi {quality}", "mobile {quality}" (lowercase m), then "no cache" when the cache is off or "{size} cache"; fresh defaults read "Wi-Fi Original · mobile Original · 2 GB cache" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:93-97, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummariesTest.kt:75)
- [x] Lyrics line: "Found online" when finding lyrics online is on (default on), else "From your music only"; then "Screen stays on" when keep the screen on is on (default on) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:99-102, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:55, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:220)
- [x] Scrobbling line, first match wins: switched off "Off"; on but no saved token user "Not connected yet"; token needs attention "Needs a new token"; else "ListenBrainz as {user}" (default is off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:104-109, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:42-50)
- [x] Backup line is always "Settings, playlists, likes and favorites" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:111)
- [x] About line: "Update ready: {version}" when an update is ready, else "Version {app version name}" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:113-114) (iPhone: "Update ready" once a newer release has passed its signed manifest check)
- [x] No summary line ever contains an em dash or en dash (tested) (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummariesTest.kt:102-113)

## Settings search: matching rules

- [x] Every setting is written once in the index with: id (marks its row), page, title, summary line, keywords, optional section; rows take their titles from the index so search finds what the page shows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:26-53)
- [x] Ids are unique and every title is non-blank; every page, Sound included, has at least one entry (tested) (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndexTest.kt:13-24)
- [x] Text is normalized before matching: trimmed, accents removed (Unicode NFD then combining marks dropped), lowercased, then everything but letters, digits and spaces removed, runs of spaces collapsed to one, trimmed; so "wifi" finds "Wi-Fi" and "Whät's" finds "What's new" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:534-536, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/catalog/Keys.kt:8-10)
- [x] Nothing typed (empty after normalizing, including only spaces or punctuation) finds nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:549-550)
- [x] The typed text is split into words on single spaces; every word must be found for a setting to match (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:551-561)
- [x] A word is found in a text when the text starts with it, or some word in the text starts with it (" " + word); a word of 3 or more characters is also found anywhere inside the text; an empty text finds nothing (so "ss" matches nothing, "eq" finds "Equalizer") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:538-544)
- [x] Rank 1 (best): the normalized title starts with the whole typed text (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:557)
- [x] Rank 2: every word is found in the title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:558)
- [x] Rank 3: every word is found in the title or in the "other words" (the keywords, the page title and the section, joined) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:554, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:559)
- [x] Rank 4: every word is found in the title, other words or the summary line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:560)
- [x] Results are ordered by rank; ties keep the order the settings are written in the index (stable sort) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:546-565)
- [x] Known results to match: "crossfade" gives "Crossfade" then "Longest blend" first; "CROSS" gives "Crossfade" first; "gapless" gives "Crossfade" first; "semitones" gives "Speed" first; "tempo" includes "Speed"; "replaygain" includes "Loudness"; "streaming mobile" gives exactly "Streaming on mobile data", "Fetch ahead on mobile data", "Download on Wi-Fi only"; "crossfade bluetooth" gives nothing; "playback resume" ranks by index order (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndexTest.kt:39-105)
- [x] No index title, summary, place or keyword contains an em dash (tested) (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndexTest.kt:26-31)

## Settings search: every indexed setting (id, page, section, title, line, keywords)

### Playback

- [x] `crossfade`, Playback, no section: "Crossfade", "Each song fades into the next"; keywords fade, blend, gapless, transition, mix (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:56-59)
- [x] `crossfade_length`, Playback: "Longest blend", "The most seconds two songs blend for"; keywords fade, seconds, duration, crossfade length (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:60-63)
- [x] `smart_transitions`, Playback: "Smart transitions", "Each blend starts where the songs meet best"; keywords automix, dj, mix, transition, beat, outro (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:64-67)
- [x] `filter_sweeps`, Playback: "Filter sweeps", "Songs thin out as they cross"; keywords filter, sweep, bass, eq, dj (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:68-71)
- [x] `match_tempo`, Playback: "Match tempo", "The next song nudges its speed to the beat"; keywords tempo, bpm, beat, beatmatch, speed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:72-75)

## Settings page frame and shared rows (used by every settings page)

### Page frame

- [x] A settings page is: a floating round back button (40dp, glazed, accessibility label "Back") at the top start, 16dp from the side and 8dp under the status bar, over a vertical scrolling column (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:25-52, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:54-66)
- [x] The page column starts 56dp lower than other pages (clears the back button) plus status bar plus 8dp, and ends navigation bar plus 120dp from the bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:44, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:51-52)
- [x] The page title is the large display title, then the page's groups one under another with 24dp between them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:46-47)
- [x] The highlighted row (from search) lights only once per visit: a saved "shown" flag is set when the highlight starts, so coming back to the page (or rotating) does not light it again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:34-36)

### Highlight of a searched-for row

- [x] When a row's index entry id equals the page's highlight id: mark shown, wait 350 ms for the page to settle, scroll the row into view, raise an accent wash (accent at 14% times the glow) over 250 ms, hold 900 ms, fade it out over 700 ms (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:62-71, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:152-169)

### Group

- [x] A group is rows inside one raised panel with 20dp corners, 16dp from the screen sides (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:60, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:77-105)
- [x] An optional group title sits above the panel: shown in capitals, label style with 0.6sp letter spacing, muted colour, 16dp sides and 8dp below; marked as a heading and read aloud in its original (not capitalised) words; led by an optional 15dp icon 7dp before the words (a brand mark keeps its own colours) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:88-104, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:246-254)
- [x] A row that draws nothing (zero height) takes no place and gets no hairline (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:105-123)
- [x] Hairlines go only between rows, one physical pixel tall, primary text colour at 10%, starting at the separator inset (16dp by default, 66dp for rows with icon tiles) and running to the end (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:130-141)
- [x] An optional footer sits under the panel in caption style, muted, 16dp sides, 8dp above (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:124-126)

### Row frame

- [x] Every row: full width, at least 56dp tall (notes have no minimum), padding 16dp sides and 12dp top and bottom, 12dp between parts, contents centred vertically (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:143-177)
- [x] Row label: title in body style (muted when disabled) with an optional line under it in caption style, 2dp gap, muted by default (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:179-186)

### Switch row

- [x] The whole row toggles the setting (role switch); the switch itself is hidden from accessibility so the row is read once; disabled rows do not toggle and show a muted title and a faded switch (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:188-202)
- [x] Switch look: track 36x22dp, thumb 18dp, 2dp inside padding; on is the accent colour with a faint accent glow under it (30%); off is white at 18% and looks recessed; the thumb slides over the card duration and stretches up to 1.18 times wider toward where it is going while pressed; disabled is drawn at the disabled alpha (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/OctoSwitch.kt:33-151) (iPhone: the system switch, tinted with the accent)

### Choice row and choice popup

- [x] A choice row: title on the left, the current choice on the right (body small, secondary colour, end aligned, at most 2 lines with ellipsis, up to 0.8 of the row's spare width), then an 18dp chevron; disabled: muted value and chevron at 50% (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:204-244) (iPhone: an up and down chevron, as the row opens a system menu)
- [x] Tapping a choice row asks its question through the app-wide choice popup, anchored to the row (the row is remembered as the asker when a finger lands on it) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:216-217, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:104-138) (iPhone: a system menu opened from the row)
- [x] A pressed control counts as the popup's anchor only if the question is asked within 2,000 ms of the press; otherwise the popup is centred (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:78-100) (iPhone: a system menu always opens from the row that asked)
- [x] The choice popup shows the question's title as a quiet heading, then the answers one per line; each answer can carry a detail line; the current answer sits in a darker pill with a white 18dp check; hairlines part answers except next to the chosen pill (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:160-177, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:51-157) (iPhone: a system menu headed by the question, each answer with its detail line, the current one checked)
- [x] An answer marked not enabled is shown dimmed (muted words) with its detail saying why, and cannot be picked (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:35-38, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:118-147)
- [x] Picking an answer runs the page's action with its index and closes the popup at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:167-173)
- [x] The choice list is 220dp to 320dp wide (as wide as its widest answer within that), padded 6dp, and scrolls when taller than the popup may be (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:163-176) (iPhone: the system menu sizes and scrolls itself)
- [x] Popup placement: 12dp clear of every screen edge and the system bars; under the anchor with a 6dp gap when it fits, else over it when that fits, else on the roomier side pushed back inside; lined up with the anchor's start edge, or its end edge when the anchor's centre is on the far half of the screen; with no anchor it is centred (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:64-115) (iPhone: the system menu places itself beside the row)
- [x] Popup size limits: at most 340dp wide (and the screen less margins), at most 60% of the screen height (and the room between the bars); keeps above the keyboard (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:117-121, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:240-246) (iPhone: the system menu keeps itself inside the screen)
- [x] Popup look and motion: frosted glass card with 20dp corners, a black film at 52%, frost blur 24, a halo; grows from the corner nearest the anchor (scale 0.92 to 1 with fade) over the swap duration on the spring easing; with Reduce motion or system animations off it only fades (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:112-132, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:175-228) (iPhone: the system menu's Liquid Glass and its own motion)
- [x] The popup closes on a tap outside the card (on finger up) or on back; its accessibility pane title is the question's title (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:187-239) (iPhone: the system menu closes on a tap outside; VoiceOver reads its heading)
- [x] The popup is drawn over everything, the floating bar included, from the app shell (one host for the whole app) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:59-62, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:345) (iPhone: system menus draw over everything, the bar included)

### Slider row

- [x] A slider row: title on the left and the current value on the right (body small, tabular digits, secondary colour) on one line, the settings slider under them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:256-280)
- [x] The settings slider is the "Jewel" look, 48dp tall touch area; a tap jumps to that point; a drag reports the value continuously while dragging (live) and again on release; its accessibility label is the row's title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:271-277, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/LineSlider.kt:34-131)

### Action, info and note rows

- [x] An action row: title (in the error colour when destructive), optional helper line, and at the end either a trailing word (shown like a choice value, no chevron by default) or a 20dp chevron; while busy a 18dp accent spinner replaces both and the row cannot be tapped; disabled rows cannot be tapped and fade the chevron to 50% (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:282-312)
- [x] An info row: title, optional helper, and its value on the right like a choice value; not tappable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:314-321)
- [x] A note row: a few words in caption style (muted by default, or a given colour such as the error colour), no minimum height, can still be highlighted by search when given an entry (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:323-329)

### Sound page cards (CardLines)

- [x] The Sound page draws titled cards instead of the rows: 20dp from the sides, raised panel with 20dp corners, 20dp inside padding, 10dp between lines, title in the section style (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/CardLines.kt:25-43)
- [x] A card switch line: label (body small, muted when disabled) over a muted detail, the switch at the end; only the switch toggles (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/CardLines.kt:45-60)
- [x] A card choice line: label over its current value (muted), a 20dp chevron; the whole line is a button that opens the options (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/CardLines.kt:62-78)

## Settings front page: the Playback entry

- [x] The Settings front page shows Playback as the first row of the group titled "Listening": a CategoryRow with the OctoIcons.Playback icon on a tinted tile, the title "Playback", a one-line summary under it, and a chevron; tapping it opens the Playback page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:305-306) (iPhone: first row of "Listening", SF Symbol play.circle on a tinted tile)
- [x] The page title string is "Playback", taken from SettingsPage.Playback, which is the first page in the settings page order (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:12-13)
- [x] The summary joins its parts with " · " (space, middle dot, space) and keeps at most 3 parts, most telling first (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:19-27)
- [x] Summary part 1: "Crossfade {crossfadeSeconds} s" (for example "Crossfade 8 s") when Crossfade is on, otherwise "Gapless" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:38)
- [x] Summary part 2: the speed label (for example "1.25x") only when speed and pitch are not at their defaults; "not default" means speed is not 1, or Keep pitch is off, or the pitch shift is not 0, so speed 1 with Keep pitch off shows "1x" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:39; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:100-101)
- [x] Summary part 3: "Autoplay" when Autoplay is on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:40)
- [x] Summary part 4: "Skip silence" when Skip silence is on, dropped whenever three parts come before it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:41)
- [x] Summary examples that must match: defaults give "Gapless · Autoplay"; Crossfade on at the default length gives "Crossfade 8 s · Autoplay"; Crossfade 4 s, speed 1.25, Autoplay off, Skip silence on gives "Crossfade 4 s · 1.25x · Skip silence"; speed 2 with Skip silence on and Autoplay on gives "Gapless · 2x · Autoplay" (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummariesTest.kt:23-33)
- [x] The summary line is one line, ellipsized when too long (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:338-340)
- [x] The route for SettingsPage.Playback renders the Playback page with the back action and the optional search highlight id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:337)

## Settings > Playback page

### Page frame and page-wide behavior

- [x] The page is a settings page frame: a back button, the large screen title "Playback", then the groups stacked one under another 24dp apart, the whole page scrolling vertically (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:27-52; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:78)
- [x] Group order, top to bottom: an untitled group (Crossfade, Longest blend, Smart transitions, Filter sweeps, Match tempo, Speed, Skip silence, Autoplay), then "Radio", then "Headphones", then "Casting" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:79-197)
- [x] The page's view model exposes the stored player settings as a state that starts at the defaults (PlayerPrefs()) and stays subscribed for 5 seconds after the page stops listening; until the stored values arrive the page draws the defaults (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:37-38) (iPhone: the values are read at start, so the page never shows the defaults first)
- [x] Every control on the page writes its new value straight to the settings store in a background task; there is no local copy, and the row redraws when the store emits the new value (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:40-59) (iPhone: each control writes straight to UserDefaults and the row redraws from it)
- [x] Opened from a search result, the row whose entry id matches is lit once: after 350 ms it scrolls into view, an accent wash at 14% alpha fades in over 250 ms, holds 900 ms, and fades out over 700 ms (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:62-70,145-177)
- [x] The search light shows once per visit (saved across configuration changes) and does not light again when coming back to the page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:34-36)
- [x] Rows hidden by a parent switch (Longest blend, Smart transitions, Filter sweeps, Match tempo, Always play on connect) are still found by search; opening such a result lights nothing while the row is hidden, and the light stays pending for that visit, so turning the parent switch on during the visit makes the row light as it appears (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:35-36; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:155-164; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:86-107,175-182)
- [x] Each group is one raised panel with 20dp corners and 16dp side padding; rows are separated by a one-physical-pixel hairline (primary text colour at 10%) inset 16dp; a hidden row takes no room and gets no hairline (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:60,72-141)
- [x] A titled group shows its title above the panel in capitals (label style, letter spacing 0.6sp, muted colour) led by a 15dp muted icon 7dp away; the title row is an accessibility heading whose spoken text is the title in its normal case (for example "Radio") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:88-103)
- [x] A group footer, when present, is a muted caption under the panel with 16dp side padding and 8dp top padding (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:124-126)
- [x] Each row is at least 56dp tall with 16dp side and 12dp vertical padding, items spaced 12dp (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:145-177)
- [x] A switch row is toggled by a tap anywhere on the row (accessibility role Switch); the title is body text, the helper line (when given) is a muted caption 2dp under it; the switch graphic itself is hidden from accessibility so the row reads once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:179-202)
- [x] The helper lines on this page are written in PlaybackPage.kt; the SettingsIndex summary of each setting is never drawn on the page (it is only used to match search words) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:80-196; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:277)
- [x] No row on this page is ever disabled or dimmed; rows are either shown or hidden (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:79-197)

### Group 1 (no title): blending, speed, silence and Autoplay

#### Crossfade

- [x] First row of the page: a switch row titled "Crossfade" (entry id "crossfade") with the helper line "Albums played in order stay gapless." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:80-85; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:56-59)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "crossfade" in the "player" settings store, default false (off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:49,122,124,195,241-243)
- [x] With Crossfade on, "Longest blend" and "Smart transitions" appear directly under it, and with Smart transitions also on, "Filter sweeps" and "Match tempo" appear under that; turning Crossfade off hides all four and keeps their stored values (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:86-108)
- [x] The player's longest blend is crossfadeSeconds × 1000 ms when Crossfade is on and 0 when off (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:90-91)
- [x] The player gets one transition settings object: longest blend (0 turns blending off), smart transitions, filter sweeps and tempo matching (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:93-95; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:62-71)
- [x] Changes apply at once: the playback service watches the transition settings, and only when they change hands them to the phone's player and records whether crossfade is on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:218-226)
- [x] Any change to the transition settings (Crossfade, Longest blend, Smart transitions, Filter sweeps, Match tempo) ends a blend in progress at once, drops the planned transition and unloads the preloaded next song, then plans again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfader.kt:60-66,166-173)
- [x] When the playback service starts, the stored Crossfade value is read before the saved queue is restored, because it decides how the sound path is built (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:203-207)
- [x] Turning Crossfade on while a song plays: that song was set up without crossfade, so it cannot be shaped and plays out with no blend into the next song (log "automix: this song started before crossfade was on, so it plays out"); blends start from the following song (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfader.kt:481-487) (iPhone: every deck's sound always runs through the shaping step, so the song already playing can blend once Crossfade is turned on)
- [x] No blend (songs play gaplessly) when: the longest blend is 0, nothing is next, repeat one is on, the player stops at the end of this song, either song's length is unknown or 0 (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfade.kt:24-25)
- [x] Albums played in order stay gapless: no blend when both songs have the same album id and the next song's album position is exactly the current one plus 1 (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfade.kt:26-32)
- [x] Blend length is the smaller of the longest blend and half of the shorter of the two songs; a length under 500 ms means no blend (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfade.kt:11-12,33-34)
- [x] The plain crossfade is equal power: outgoing volume cos(p × pi/2), incoming sin(p × pi/2), p from 0 to 1 (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfade.kt:37-41)
- [~] Crossfade belongs to the phone's own player only: while casting, the crossfade length in use is 0 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/output/OutputSwitch.kt:41-42; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:221) (not on iPhone: there is no casting; AirPlay carries the phone's own player, crossfade included)
- [x] With Crossfade on, Autoplay adds its songs earlier (see Autoplay below) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:112-114)
- [x] The sleep timer's "end of song" stop sets pause-at-end, which turns blending off for that song; a song change caused by a blend counts as the song ending for "after N songs" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/SleepTimer.kt:36-41,163-173; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfader.kt:219,222)

#### Longest blend

- [x] Shown only while Crossfade is on, as the row right under Crossfade (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:86-87)
- [x] A slider row titled "Longest blend" (entry id "crossfade_length") with no helper line; the current value sits at the right of the title as "{n} s" (for example "8 s") in small body text with tabular figures and secondary colour; the slider spans the row under the title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:203-217; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:256-280)
- [x] Range 2 to 16 whole seconds in 1 second steps (CrossfadeSecondsRange = 2..16) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:119-120)
- [x] Slider position = (seconds - 2) / 14; a touch or drag at position f picks 2 + round(f × 14) (half rounds up) and saves only when it differs from the current value (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:206-215)
- [x] Stored as Int key "crossfade_seconds", default 8; clamped into 2..16 when read, when written and when restored from a backup (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:50,125,196,245-247,369)
- [x] The slider is the settings ("Jewel") slider in live mode: values save continuously while dragging, a tap on the line jumps there, the drag sends the value again on release (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:271-277; design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/LineSlider.kt:76-103)
- [x] No value bubble and no step ticks on this slider; while dragging, the thumb follows the finger freely; after release (or when the value changes from elsewhere) the fill slides in 80 ms, straight, to the saved whole-second position (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/Sliders.kt:77-91,96-199; design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/LineSlider.kt:104-110)
- [x] Accessibility: the slider's description is "Longest blend"; the "{n} s" value is separate text (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:268-276)
- [x] The longest blend is real time: at a playback speed other than 1 the planner gets it multiplied by the speed (at 1.5x an 8 s blend covers 12 s of the songs and still lasts 8 s) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixTiming.kt:44-48; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfader.kt:479)
- [x] A blend is never longer than half of the shorter song, planned or plain (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfade.kt:33; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:189)

#### Smart transitions

- [x] Shown only while Crossfade is on, right under Longest blend: a switch row titled "Smart transitions" (entry id "smart_transitions") with the helper line "Each blend starts where the two songs meet best, on the beat when it can. Off blends at the end of each song." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:88-93)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "smart_transitions", default true (on) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:51,126,197,249-251)
- [x] With it on, "Filter sweeps" and "Match tempo" appear under it; turning it off hides both and keeps their stored values (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:94-107)
- [x] Off: every blend is the fixed equal-power crossfade at the end of the song (no curve weight, no filters, no headroom) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:181,411-429)
- [x] Off: the song's end and the next song's start are not analysed or fetched, and the plan is made at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfader.kt:329-331,445-449)
- [x] On: each blend is planned (where it starts, where the next song comes in, how long, and a cut, lift or blend curve, on the bar when the tempo allows) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:157-334)
- [x] On, but a song shorter than 35 s, or either song's genre containing classical, opera, spoken, audiobook, podcast or comedy (any case), gets the plain crossfade instead of a planned one (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:9-10,41-42,182-187)
- [x] On: a playback speed other than 1, or Skip silence on, turns off bar lock and tempo matching for planned blends (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:76-78,193)

#### Filter sweeps

- [x] Shown only while Crossfade and Smart transitions are both on, right under Smart transitions: a switch row titled "Filter sweeps" (entry id "filter_sweeps") with the helper line "The song ending loses its highs and bass as the next one comes in." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:94-100)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "filter_sweeps", default true (on) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:52,127,198,253-255)
- [x] Effect: planned blends run the filters at strength 0.7 (FILTER_STRENGTH) when on and 0 when off; the plain crossfade never sweeps (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:190; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixCurves.kt:20)

#### Match tempo

- [x] Shown only while Crossfade and Smart transitions are both on, right under Filter sweeps: a switch row titled "Match tempo" (entry id "match_tempo") with the helper line "Songs with a close tempo blend in step. The next song settles back to its own speed after." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:101-106)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "match_tempo", default false (off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:53,128,199,257-259)
- [x] Effect: only when both songs' tempos are confidently known, the blend can lock to the bar (speed 1, Skip silence off), and the tempo ratio (folded by halving or doubling into 0.707 to 1.414) is off 1 by at least 0.5% and at most 4%, the next song plays at that ratio (clamped 0.94 to 1.06) through the blend and enters on a downbeat (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutomixPlan.kt:297-304,336-344)
- [x] After the blend the next song eases back to its own speed in steps of 0.0025; the listener changing the speed ends the easing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Crossfader.kt:34-35,608-630)

#### Speed

- [x] Always shown, after the Crossfade rows: a choice row titled "Speed" (entry id "speed") with no helper line, the current pace on the right and a chevron; tapping it opens the Speed popup (not the choice list) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:109,199-200)
- [x] The value text starts with the speed label; when speed is not 1 it adds ", voices keep their pitch" if Keep pitch is on or ", pitch follows the speed" if off; when the pitch shift is not 0 it adds ", shifted {n} st" with the signed whole number and no plus sign (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:62-67)
- [x] Value examples: "1x"; "1.25x, voices keep their pitch"; "0.75x, pitch follows the speed, shifted -2 st"; "1x, shifted 3 st"; speed 1 with Keep pitch off shows just "1x" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:62-67)
- [x] Speed label format: the speed in hundredths, rounded; a whole number shows no decimals ("1x", "2x"), a tenth shows one ("1.5x", "1.1x"), otherwise two ("1.25x", "0.55x"), then "x" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:60-71)
- [x] The value text on a choice row is right-aligned small body text in secondary colour, at most 2 lines with an ellipsis, taking at most 0.8 of the title's share of the row; the chevron is 18dp, muted, with no accessibility label; the row has the Button role (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:204-244)
- [x] Whether the Speed popup is open survives configuration changes (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:75)

#### Skip silence

- [x] A switch row titled "Skip silence" (entry id "skip_silence") with no helper line (title only) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:110-114)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "skip_silence", default false (off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:62,133,205,286-288)

#### Autoplay

- [x] Last row of the first group: a switch row titled "Autoplay" (entry id "autoplay") with the helper line "When the queue ends, similar songs keep playing." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:115-120)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "autoplay", default true (on) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:64,134,206,290-292)
- [x] Autoplay watches the setting and checks again at once when it changes; turning it off stops any pending add (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:69-78,87-109)
- [x] It is due only when: Autoplay is on, the playing entry is the last in the queue, repeat is off, the entry is not an internet radio stream, Autoplay has not already tried after this entry, and the player is playing or has ended (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:87-103)
- [x] It checks every 1 s until the real time left in the song (at the current speed) is at most the lead: the larger of 20 s and (longest blend + 45 s when crossfade is on + 15 s) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:29-40,111-117,169-173)
- [x] It adds up to 10 songs, each marked as Autoplay's, from Octo's radio seeded by the last song plus up to 10 of the listener's own songs among the last 30 queue entries (most recent first, Autoplay's own left out); never a queued song, one of the last 50 played, or the seed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:26-27,118-124,144-151; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutoplayPicks.kt:6-7)
- [x] When the radio finds nothing it falls back to library songs by the same artist, then in the same genre, each shuffled, still excluding the above (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:151-167; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/AutoplayPicks.kt:9-23)
- [x] It tries once per queue entry whatever the result; songs are added only if the setting is still on and the entry is still the last; if the song already ended while waiting, it jumps to the first added song and plays (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:123-139)
- [x] The radio Autoplay uses follows the Radio settings on this page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:150; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/LibraryRadio.kt:49)
- [x] Songs Autoplay added carry an "Autoplay" caption in the queue (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/QueueSheet.kt:381)

- [x] Choice 3: label "Balanced", detail "About 1 song in 3 from outside your library", stored "Balanced", share 0.35 (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/radio/RadioTuning.kt:24)

#### When the radio settings take effect

- [x] All four radio settings are read fresh each time a radio is built, so a change applies to the next Start radio and the next Autoplay batch; songs already queued stay as they are (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/LibraryRadio.kt:39-79)
- [x] Start radio plays the seed then up to 50 radio songs as the queue, named "{song title} radio"; when nothing similar is found the toast reads "No similar songs found" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenu.kt:360-374; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/LibraryRadio.kt:23-24)
- [x] The radio works from the library alone with no server; server trouble only loses its suggestions (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/LibraryRadio.kt:26-31,50-55)
- [x] "Library songs only" (Library page, Boolean key "library_only", default false) also drops server suggestions found online from every radio whatever Discovery says; the Discovery row is not dimmed or changed by it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/LibraryRadio.kt:56,67; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:86-88,163; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:122-153)
- [x] A radio never picks: the first seed, an excluded song, a song rated 1 star, or filler (shorter than 45 s or longer than 45 min, "interview" or "podcast" in the title, a podcast or audiobook genre, or "intro", "introduction", "skit" or "interlude" in the title of a song 4 min or shorter); a length of 0 counts as unknown (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/radio/RadioMix.kt:116,124; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/radio/RadioFilter.kt:5-24)

### Headphones

- [x] Third group, titled "Headphones" with the OctoIcons.Headphones icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:154-156)
- [x] Footer "Only while Octo is open or paused in the background." shown only while "Resume when headphones connect" or "Resume when Bluetooth connects" is on; no footer when both are off (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:157-161)
- [x] Row 1: a switch row titled "Resume when headphones connect" (entry id "resume_wired") with the helper line "If unplugging paused it in the last 30 minutes." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:163-168)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "resume_wired", default false (off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:73,139,211,310-312)
- [x] Row 2: a switch row titled "Resume when Bluetooth connects" (entry id "resume_bluetooth") with the helper line "If losing them paused it in the last 30 minutes." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:169-174)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "resume_bluetooth", default false (off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:74,140,212,314-316)
- [x] Row 3, shown only while either of the first two is on: a switch row titled "Always play on connect" (entry id "resume_always") with the helper line "However or whenever the music was paused." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:175-182)
- [x] Stored as Boolean key "resume_always", default false (off); kept when hidden, and has no effect while both kinds are off (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:75,141,213,318-320; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:46-51)
- [x] Wired kinds: wired headphones, wired headset and USB headset (USB-C counts); Bluetooth kinds: A2DP, BLE headset and BLE speaker; any other output never resumes (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:18-32) (iPhone: iOS port types Headphones and USB audio for wired, Bluetooth A2DP and LE for Bluetooth)
- [x] Music starts again on connect only when: that kind's switch is on, nothing is playing (play not requested), the queue is not empty, and either "Always play on connect" is on or the music was paused by the output going away (cable pulled, Bluetooth lost) within the last 30 minutes (0 to 30 min inclusive, a clock going backwards does not count) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:14-16,34-53)
- [x] Only a pause caused by the output going away ("becoming noisy") is remembered; any other pause or pressing play forgets it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:113-121)
- [x] The check waits for the sound to move to the new output: 500 ms after a wired device connects, 1,500 ms after Bluetooth; a newer connection replaces a pending check, and a device gone before the wait ends is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:55-59,84-93)
- [x] Only outputs that were not already connected count as new; outputs present when watching starts are ignored, and a removed output counts as new when it returns (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:77-79,95-104)
- [x] On resume it forgets the remembered pause, prepares the player if it was idle, and plays (log "headphones: {wired|bluetooth} connected, playing again") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:123-137)
- [x] It is always the phone's own player that headphones bring back, never a cast device (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:235-239) (iPhone: there is one player; AirPlay is only a route of it)
- [x] While either resume switch is on, the playback service keeps running in the foreground for the full 30 minutes after a pause; with both off it uses the media library's default timeout (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:235-244) (iPhone: the output keeps running silent 1,800 s after a pause while a resume switch is on, else 600 s)
- [x] It works only while the playback service is alive; nothing starts the service for it, so once the system has stopped the service (long paused, or the app swiped away), connecting headphones does nothing, which is what the footer says (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:61-65) (iPhone: only while the app is alive in the background, during the silent wait; the footer says so)

- [x] Effect when casting ends by itself (the device went away or was stopped from elsewhere): the music carries on on the phone only if it was playing and this is on; otherwise it comes back paused (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/output/Outputs.kt:209-217; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/output/RemoteQueue.kt:199-204) (iPhone: when an AirPlay speaker or TV goes away (the route's AirPlay output is gone), the music carries on here only if it was playing and this is on)

- [~] Crossfade, speed, pitch and Skip silence belong to the phone's player and wait there while casting; the device list says "Sound settings apply on this phone only" while casting (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:221; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/output/OutputSheet.kt:169) (not on iPhone: there is no cast session; AirPlay plays the phone's own player, so these settings always apply)
