- [x] "radio_variety": title "Artist variety", summary "How soon an artist or album comes back", keywords "radio", "repeat", "spacing", "artist", "album", section "Radio" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:96-99)
- [x] "radio_favorites": title "Favorites more often", summary "Hearted and 4 or 5 star songs come around more", keywords "radio", "favorite", "hearted", "rating", "stars", section "Radio" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:100-103)
- [x] "resume_wired": title "Resume when headphones connect", summary "Plays again when a cable goes back in", keywords "headphones", "cable", "wired", "usb", "plug", section "Headphones", place "Playback · Headphones" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:104-107)
- [x] "resume_bluetooth": title "Resume when Bluetooth connects", summary "Plays again when Bluetooth reconnects", keywords "bluetooth", "speaker", "car", "headphones", section "Headphones" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:108-111)
- [x] "resume_always": title "Always play on connect", summary "Plays on connect however the music stopped", keywords "headphones", "bluetooth", "connect", section "Headphones" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:112-115)
- [~] "cast_renderers": title "Show TVs and speakers (DLNA)", summary "Smart TVs, receivers and streamers on the Wi-Fi", keywords "cast", "casting", "dlna", "upnp", "renderer", "tv", "speaker", "receiver", "streamer", "devices", section "Casting", place "Playback · Casting" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:117-120) (not on iPhone: DLNA was dropped by the owner)
- [x] "cast_keep_playing": title "Keep playing on the phone when casting ends", summary "Otherwise the music pauses", keywords "cast", "casting", "chromecast", "tv", "speaker", "disconnect", "resume", "phone", section "Casting" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:121-124) (iPhone: also found by "airplay")
- [x] The entries are registered in the order above (it breaks ties between equally good matches), and each row takes its title from its entry so search and the page always agree (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:41-53,546-565)
- [x] A search result row shows the setting's title with its place under it ("Playback", or "Playback · {section}" joined with " · ") and opens the Playback page with that row's id to light (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:37-38; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:277)
- [x] Search checks for this page: "crossfade" lists "Crossfade" then "Longest blend" first; "CROSS" finds "Crossfade" first; "gapless" finds "Crossfade" first; "semitones" finds "Speed" first; "tempo" includes "Speed"; "crossfade bluetooth" finds nothing (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndexTest.kt:39-68)

## Speed popup (opened from the Speed row)

- [x] A floating glass card whose accessibility pane title is "Speed", opening beside the point last pressed in the app within the last 2 s (the app shell records the finger's point everywhere), or in the middle of the screen when nothing was pressed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:88-101; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/PressSpot.kt:32-70; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:264) (iPhone: a system popover anchored to the Speed row)
- [x] It goes under the anchor 6dp away when it fits, over it when only that fits, else on the roomier side; it lines up with the anchor's end edge when the anchor is on the right half of the screen; it keeps 12dp from every edge and clear of the system bars and keyboard (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:64-115) (iPhone: the system popover places itself)
- [x] It is at most 340dp wide and 60% of the screen tall, the content scrolling inside; it grows in from the corner nearest the anchor (scale 0.92 to 1 with a fade); with Reduce motion it only fades, and with system animations off it appears at once (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:117-132,174-226) (iPhone: the system popover sizes and moves itself; the 320 wide card fits a phone in portrait)
- [x] It closes on a tap outside the card or on back (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:183-239) (iPhone: the system popover closes on a tap outside)
- [x] The card content is a 320dp wide scrolling column, the same speed controls as the player's speed page but without the "Sleep timer" back row that page shows when opened from the sleep timer (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:72-86; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SleepSheet.kt:53) (iPhone: the speed card, 320 wide, with no Sleep timer row)
- [x] Padding: 20dp at the sides, 8dp at the top, 18dp at the bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:105)
- [x] Header row, 48dp tall: "Speed" in body text on the left; a "Reset" button on the right only while speed, Keep pitch or pitch is not at its default (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:106-109)
- [x] "Reset" removes the stored speed, Keep pitch and pitch shift, so they read as 1x, on and 0 again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:277-284)
- [x] 8dp under the header, the speed label in the large display style with tabular figures (for example "1.25x") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:110-111)
- [x] Speed slider: Octo's glowing line slider in live mode (changes heard as the finger moves); position = (speed - 0.5) / 1.5; a position f picks snapSpeed(0.5 + f × 1.5) and saves only when it differs (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:112-118; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:33-39)
- [x] Speed slider accessibility: description "Playback speed", state the speed label (for example "1.25x") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:119-122)
- [x] Speed range 0.5 to 2.0; within 0.04 of 0.75, 1, 1.25 or 1.5 it settles on that speed; otherwise it moves in 0.05 steps, rounded to two decimals (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:7-31)
- [x] Under the slider the labels "0.75x", "1x", "1.25x" and "1.5x", each centred where its speed falls on the line and kept inside the width; caption size, tabular figures; the one matching the current speed in primary colour, the rest muted; tapping one sets that speed (no ripple, Button role, 6dp by 4dp padding) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:124,163-191; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:11-12)
- [x] 16dp under the labels, the Keep pitch line: "Keep pitch" in small body text with the detail "Voices sound as usual at any speed." when on or "Pitch rises and falls with the speed, like a record." when off, and an on/off switch at the right; only the switch toggles it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:126-137)
- [x] Keep pitch is stored as Boolean key "keep_pitch", default true (on) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:59,131,202,269-271)
- [x] 12dp under it, the Pitch line: "Pitch" in small body text on the left and the shift on the right as "+2 st", "0 st" or "-3 st" with tabular figures, muted at 0 and primary otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:139-147; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:73-74)
- [x] Pitch slider: glowing line slider in live mode; -6 to +6 whole semitones; position = (st + 6) / 12; a position f picks round(f × 12) - 6 and saves only when it differs; accessibility description "Pitch" with state the shift label (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:148-159; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:20-21,41-46)
- [x] Speed is stored as Float key "speed", default 1.0, snapped on read, write and restore; the pitch shift as Int key "pitch_semitones", default 0, kept within -6 to +6 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:58,60,130,132,201-204,265-275,374-376)
- [x] Effect, heard at once on the phone's player: speed is the snapped speed and pitch is (1 with Keep pitch on, else the speed) × 2^(semitones / 12) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/Pace.kt:48-58; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:228-230)
- [x] The speed is kept for every song and across restarts, and the Speed row's value updates live while the popup is open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/SpeedSheet.kt:72-75; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:74,109)

## Choice popup (Discovery, Adventure, Artist variety)

- [x] A choice row marks itself as the anchor as the finger lands on it; a list asked for within 2 s of that press opens beside the whole row's bounds, otherwise in the middle of the screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:78-106,119-138; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:216-217) (iPhone: a system menu opens from the row)
- [x] It is the same floating glass card as the Speed popup (placement, size limits, motion, tap outside or back to close), with the question as its pane title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:140-158) (iPhone: a system menu)
- [x] Inside, the question is repeated at the top as a quiet heading (label style, secondary colour, padding 14dp sides, 10dp top, 6dp bottom) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:165; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:87-96) (iPhone: the menu's section header carries the question)
- [x] The list is 220dp to 320dp wide (as wide as its widest line needs), padded 6dp, and scrolls when taller than the card may be (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:163-176) (iPhone: the system menu sizes itself)
- [x] Each answer: its label in small body text and its detail line under it as a muted caption, at least 40dp tall, padded 14dp by 10dp (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:118-147; design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/OctoTokens.kt:91-93) (iPhone: a menu item with its line under it)
- [x] The current answer sits in a darker pill (14dp corners) with a white 18dp check at the right; the other answers keep 18dp of room there so the width never changes (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:63-64,138-155) (iPhone: the system check mark on the current answer)
- [x] Hairlines (primary text colour at 8%, 14dp in from the sides) sit only between two answers that are both unselected; the selected pill never touches a line; the row height never shifts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:51-61,76-85,98-107) (iPhone: the system menu's separators)
- [x] Answers are radio buttons in a selectable group for accessibility; a finger on an unselected answer shows a quiet accent-tinted pill at once instead of a ripple (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:76-79,109-135) (iPhone: the system menu's accessibility and press feedback)
- [x] Picking an answer saves it and closes the list; picking the current answer saves the same value and closes (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:167-173; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlaybackPage.kt:126-128,134-136,142-144)

## Storage, defaults and backup

- [x] Every value on this page lives in one key-value settings store named "player" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:122)
- [x] Defaults for a new install: Crossfade off, Longest blend 8, Smart transitions on, Filter sweeps on, Match tempo off, speed 1, Keep pitch on, pitch 0, Skip silence off, Autoplay on, Discovery Balanced, Adventure Balanced, Artist variety Normal, Favorites more often off, all three headphone switches off, Show TVs and speakers on, Keep playing on the phone off (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:36-89)
- [x] A choice stored under a name the app does not know reads as that choice's default (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:165-167)
- [x] A settings backup writes every one of these values under its property name (crossfade, crossfadeSeconds, smartTransitions, filterSweeps, matchTempo, speed, keepPitch, pitchSemitones, skipSilence, autoplay, radioDiscovery, radioAdventure, radioVariety, radioFavorites, resumeWired, resumeBluetooth, resumeAlways, castRenderers, castKeepPlaying) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:38-89,360-361; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:54-61)
- [x] Restoring a backup writes them all in one go, clamping the blend length, snapping the speed, keeping the pitch in range and storing choices by name; values missing from the backup take their defaults (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:363-402; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:86-89)

## Related settings that are not on this page

- [x] "Start playing after" is not on Playback: it is on Streaming and downloads, section "Streaming", with the choices "As soon as possible" (250 ms), "1 second" (1,000 ms, default), "2.5 seconds", "5 seconds", "10 seconds", stored as String key "start_after" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:371-374; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/StartAfter.kt:3-14; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:115-116,152)
- [x] "Library songs only" is on the Library page, section "Songs found online" (its words: title "Library songs only", help "Shows only songs in your library: albums, search, artists and radio leave out outside songs."), yet it changes what radio and Autoplay may pick (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:232-235; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/discovery/LibraryOnly.kt:3-12)
- [x] Sound (equalizer, loudness, balance, headphone correction) has its own page and is not part of Playback (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:126-146)

## Settings > Appearance

### Page frame and order

- [x] The page is reached from the Settings front page row "Appearance" (group "Listening", icon OctoIcons.Appearance) and from any search result whose page is Appearance; the route opens `AppearancePage(onBack, highlight)` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:308-312, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:340) (iPhone: SF Symbol paintpalette)
- [x] The front page row's summary line is `appearanceSummary`: first part "No glow" (strength Off), "Subtle glow" (Subtle) or "Rich glow" (Rich); second part "Live background" only when the stored live background switch is on AND the phone can draw the live background (Android 13+); parts joined with " · " (space, middle dot, space), at most 3 parts. It does not look at the background mode, so Artwork or Colour mode with the switch on still says "Live background" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:27, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:44-51, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:311) (iPhone: every iPhone can draw the live background, so the switch alone decides)
- [x] The page uses SettingsPageFrame with the large title "Appearance", a back button, a vertically scrolling column, and 24dp spacing between each top-level item (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:79, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:28-52)
- [x] Top-level order on the page: (1) the ambient preview strip, (2) an untitled group holding the ambient strength row, (3) the group "Where it shows" (only while strength is not Off), (4) the group "Player" (player background section), (5) the group "Playlists", (6) the group "Motion" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:79-131)
- [x] Group titles render as a quiet uppercase label above the panel (e.g. "WHERE IT SHOWS") led by the group icon, while their accessibility text is the original-case title; rows inside a group are separated by hairlines (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:87-123)
- [x] Until the ambient settings have been read from storage, the page shows the defaults `AmbientPrefs()` (strength Subtle, every area on), not a blank state (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:74)
- [x] When opened from a search result with a row id, that row is scrolled into view and lit once (settle 350 ms, glow in 250 ms at 14% accent, hold 900 ms, fade 700 ms); coming back to the page does not light it again. A result that points at a row the page is not currently drawing (for example a "Where it shows" row while strength is Off, or "Drift speed" while the mode is not Immersive) lights nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:64-70, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:146-177, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:34-36)

### Ambient preview strip

- [x] First item on the page: a strip, full width inside 16dp horizontal padding, 72dp tall, corners rounded 14dp, filled with the app background colour (OctoColors.Background, ARGB 0xFF0C0C0D), with the ambient glow drawn over it at the currently chosen strength (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:80, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:202-212, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:61)
- [x] Over the strip, vertically centered at the start with 16dp horizontal padding: line 1 is the song on now's title, or "Nothing playing" when nothing is on (bodySmall, primary text colour, one line, ellipsis) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:213-220)
- [x] Line 2 is the song's artist (empty string when the artist is unknown) while a song is on, or "The glow takes its colours from the song on now." when nothing is on (caption, secondary text colour, one line, ellipsis) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:221-227)
- [x] The preview takes its colours from the song on now's artwork (the same palette cache the full player uses); with no artwork it uses the quiet default palette (PlayerColors.Quiet); it never drifts (moving = false) and redraws as the strength choice changes; at Off its alpha is 0 so it shows the plain background (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:203-204, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:212, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/ArtworkPalette.kt:44-51)

### Ambient strength (untitled group)

- [x] Row "Ambient colour from the artwork" is a ChoiceRow (title left, current answer right in the secondary text colour, chevron); the value shown is the label of the stored strength: "Off", "Subtle" or "Rich" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:81-82, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:208-227) (iPhone: a system menu row with the current value; title "Ambient color from the artwork")
- [x] Tapping it opens the choice popup beside the row titled "Ambient colour from the artwork" with three answers in this order, the current one marked: "Off" with detail "The plain dark background everywhere."; "Subtle" with detail "A faint glow of the artwork's colours behind the pages."; "Rich" with detail "A fuller glow, still kept dark enough for text." Picking one saves it and closes the popup (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:38-42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:83-88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:163-177) (iPhone: a system menu by the row, titled, with each answer's line and a check on the current one)
- [x] Stored in the "player" preferences store under string key "ambient_strength" as the enum name "Off", "Subtle" or "Rich"; default Subtle; an unknown stored value reads as the default (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:122, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:143, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:166-167, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:215, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:322-324, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:20)
- [x] Effect: the glow's opacity over the near-black page background is 0 for Off, 0.14 for Subtle, 0.24 for Rich; it takes effect at once on every page behind the app (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:41-45, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:176)
- [x] Effect: Off turns the glow off everywhere and hides the "Where it shows" group; the area switches keep their stored values and come back as they were when strength goes back to Subtle or Rich (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:91, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:30-37)

### Where it shows (group, only while strength is not Off)

- [x] Group title "Where it shows", icon OctoIcons.Layout; rendered only when strength != Off. Rows in order: Home, Library, Search, Settings, Mini player and bar, Album and artist pages use their own artwork (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:91-109) (iPhone: SF Symbol rectangle.3.group)
- [x] Row "Home": switch, no helper line; stored boolean "ambient_home", default true; on/off controls the glow on pages in the Home area (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:93, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:144, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:216, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:326-334, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:21)
- [x] Row "Library": switch, no helper line; stored boolean "ambient_library", default true; controls the glow on pages in the Library area (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:94-98, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:145, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:217, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:22)
- [x] Row "Search": switch, no helper line; stored boolean "ambient_search", default true; controls the glow on the Search page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:99, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:146, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:218, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:23)
- [x] Row "Settings": switch, no helper line; stored boolean "ambient_settings", default true; controls the glow on settings pages, including this page itself, so turning it off removes the glow behind the page immediately (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:100, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:147, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:219, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientSource.kt:59-61)
- [x] Row "Mini player and bar": switch with helper "A trace of the song's colour in its glass."; stored boolean "ambient_bar", default true (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:101, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:148, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:220, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:336-338, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:26)
- [x] Row "Album and artist pages use their own artwork": switch with helper "The glow follows the page's album or artist, not the song."; stored boolean "ambient_page_artwork", default true (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:102-107, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:149, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:221, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:340-342, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:28)
- [x] Every switch row flips when the whole row is tapped; the switch itself carries no separate accessibility node (the row is the toggle) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:190-202)

### What the ambient glow does (behavior the settings drive)

- [x] Area of a page: Home area = Home and Charts pages; Search area = Search page; Library area = Library, Albums, Artists, Songs, Genres, Folders, Playlists, Liked, Downloads, Favorites, every album page, artist page, online album page, online artist page, genre page and playlist page (wherever opened from); Settings area = Settings front page, Sound, Sign in, Edit connection, Server form, Octo admin, Import, Family, Family join, Shares, Radio stations, and every settings category page; any other page gets no glow at all (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientSource.kt:51-63)
- [x] What a page glows with, in order: settings not read yet means no glow (so a glow turned off never flashes at launch); a page with no area means no glow; an area switched off (or strength Off) means no glow; if "Album and artist pages use their own artwork" is on and the page is an album, artist, online album or online artist page that has registered its artwork, it glows with that page's artwork; otherwise it glows with the song on now's artwork; with no song loaded, no glow (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientSource.kt:66-83)
- [x] Album, artist, online album and online artist pages register their own artwork for the glow while shown and remove it when they leave (only if it is still theirs) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:144-152, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumScreen.kt:235, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/artist/ArtistScreen.kt:248, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/online/OnlineAlbumScreen.kt:158, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/online/OnlineArtistScreen.kt:72)
- [x] The glow is one layer above the plain background and under every page, for the page on top of the navigation stack (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:268, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:154-180)
- [x] Each artwork colour is made safe before it glows: same hue, saturation capped at 0.55 and lightness capped at 0.5 (HSL), so white or neon covers come through as a dark soft tint (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:57-58, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:65-68)
- [x] The glow is drawn from the artwork's four mesh colours: on Android 13+ as the same four-pool mesh shader the classic player background uses; on older phones as a still diagonal linear gradient of the four colours (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:281-309, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/MeshBackground.kt:31-64)
- [x] Over the glow a vertical fade runs from transparent at 35% height to the page background at 40% opacity at the bottom, so lists and the bar sit on the quietest part; nothing is drawn while the animated opacity is at or below 0.002 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:85-88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:262-272)
- [x] Colour and opacity changes (new song, new page artwork, strength change, glow turning off) ease over 800 ms; when the source turns to none the old colours are kept so they fade out as they were (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:75, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:168-176, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:237-242)
- [x] The glow drifts only while all hold: music is playing, the full player is not open, the app is in the foreground (started), Octo's Reduce motion is off, and the phone's animations are not off; it drifts at a fifth of the player mesh's pace (time advances 0.2 per second) and redraws only every 100 ms; on phones without the mesh shader it never moves (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:78-81, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:177-179, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:245-260, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:268) (iPhone: the system Reduce Motion switch stands for animations off)
- [x] Bar tint: when strength is not Off, "Mini player and bar" is on and a song is loaded, the floating bar's glass film becomes the song's main mesh colour (made safe as above) at opacity 0.06 (Subtle) or 0.10 (Rich), composited over the usual glass tint; otherwise the plain glass tint; changes ease over 800 ms (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:37, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:49-53, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:185-197, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:240) (iPhone: the film is the Liquid Glass tint)

### Player (group: player background section)

- [x] Group title "Player", icon OctoIcons.Playback. Row order: "Player background"; "Live background"; then, only when the mode is not Classic, "Brightness cap", "Saturation", "Contrast"; then, only when the mode is Immersive (Default) and the phone runs Android 13+, "Drift speed", "Move with the beat", "Frame rate" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:70-120, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:51)
- [x] Row "Player background": ChoiceRow whose value is the label of the stored mode ("Immersive", "Artwork", "Colour" or "Classic") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:71)
- [x] Tapping "Player background" opens the choice popup titled "Player background" with four answers in this order, the current one marked: "Immersive" (stored "Default") with detail "The artwork torn into drifting layers, blurred into a slow wash of its colours."; "Artwork" (stored "Artwork") with detail "The artwork, blurred and still."; "Colour" (stored "Colour") with detail "A flat fill of the artwork's main colour."; "Classic" (stored "Classic") with detail "Four of the artwork's colours, flowing into each other." Picking saves the mode (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:36-41, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:72-77, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:9-22) (iPhone: a system menu titled "Player background", with each answer's line, the current one checked)
- [x] Mode is stored in the "player" store under string key "immersive_background" as the enum name; default "Default" (Immersive); unknown values read as the default (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:153, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:187, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:28)
- [x] Every background change (mode or any slider, switch or frame rate) writes all seven background values at once after holding them to their ranges: mode, brightness cap, saturation, contrast, use BPM, fps, speed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:51-53, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:237-239, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:421-429)
- [x] Row "Live background": switch. It can move only when the mode is Immersive and the phone runs Android 13+ (SDK 33), or the mode is Classic and the phone can draw the live mesh (Android 13+); Artwork and Colour never move (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:63-68, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/MeshBackground.kt:116)
- [x] "Live background" shows checked only when the stored value is on AND the current mode can move; when the mode cannot move the row is disabled (title dimmed, switch disabled, row not tappable) and shows off, but the stored value is not changed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:79-83, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:181-186)
- [x] "Live background" helper line: "Needs Android 13 or newer." when the mode is Immersive on a phone below Android 13; "Needs Android 13 or newer." when the mode is Classic on a phone that cannot draw the mesh; "This background stays still." when the mode is Artwork or Colour; no helper line otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:84-89) (iPhone: every iPhone draws both moving backgrounds, so only "This background stays still." for Artwork and Color)
- [x] "Live background" is stored in the "player" store as boolean "live_background", default true (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:123, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:185, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:232-234)
- [x] Row "Brightness cap" (shown when mode is not Classic): slider, range 20 to 100, step 5, value label "<n>%" (for example "50%"), default 50; stored int "immersive_bg_brightness_cap" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:92-94, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:29, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashReading.kt:8, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:154)
- [x] Row "Saturation" (shown when mode is not Classic): slider, range 0 to 300, step 10, value label "<n>%", default 180; stored int "immersive_bg_saturation" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:95-97, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:30, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashReading.kt:9, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:155)
- [x] Row "Contrast" (shown when mode is not Classic): slider, range 0.50 to 2.00, step 0.05 (30 steps), value label formatted "%.2f" with a root locale (for example "1.30"), default 1.3; the saved value is rounded to two decimals; stored float "immersive_bg_contrast" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:98, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:139-153, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:31, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashReading.kt:10, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:156)
- [x] Row "Drift speed" (shown only for Immersive on Android 13+): slider, range 5 to 100, step 5, value label "<n>%", default 25; stored int "immersive_bg_speed" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:100-103, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:36, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashReading.kt:12, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:159)
- [x] Row "Move with the beat" (shown only for Immersive on Android 13+): switch with helper "Drifts with the song's tempo, when it's known."; default true; stored boolean "immersive_bg_use_bpm" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:104-109, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:32, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:157)
- [x] Row "Frame rate" (shown only for Immersive on Android 13+): ChoiceRow whose value reads "<fps> fps" (for example "60 fps"); tapping opens the popup titled "Frame rate" with answers in order "30 fps" (detail "Easiest on the battery."), "60 fps", "90 fps", "120 fps" (no detail for the last three); default 60; stored int "immersive_bg_fps" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:110-118, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashReading.kt:11, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:33, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:158) (iPhone: a system menu titled "Frame rate")
- [x] Percent sliders (Brightness cap, Saturation, Drift speed) map the drag position to range start + round(fraction x span / step) x step, held to the range, and save only when the picked value differs from the current one (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:124-136)
- [x] Contrast slider: position index = round((value - 0.5) / 0.05); a drag picks round(fraction x 30); saves (0.5 + picked x 0.05) rounded to two decimals only when the index changed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/PlayerBackgroundSection.kt:139-153)
- [x] All settings sliders are "live": a tap jumps and saves, and a drag saves continuously as it moves (not only when the finger lifts); drag fractions are clamped to 0..1; the slider's accessibility label is the row title; the value label uses tabular figures (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:258-280, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/LineSlider.kt:80-102, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/Sliders.kt:40-44)
- [x] Values read back from storage are held to the offered ranges: brightness cap 20..100, saturation 0..300, contrast 0.5..2.0, speed 5..100, and an fps not in [30, 60, 90, 120] reads as 60 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:39-45, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:186-194)

### What the player background settings do

- [x] Immersive (Default) on a phone below Android 13 is drawn as the Artwork background (still blurred artwork) instead of the moving wash; the settings still say Immersive (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:86-89, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/ImmersiveWash.kt:49-55) (iPhone: a phone whose graphics cannot build the wash shows the still artwork, the setting still saying Immersive)
- [x] Immersive: the moving wash from the prepared cover; it holds still (covers still cross-fade) when Live background is off, or Octo's Reduce motion is on, or the phone's animations are off (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:112-117)
- [x] Immersive pacing with "Move with the beat" on: the song's BPM (from any copy's tags or server details) held to 40..240; with it off, or no BPM known, the base 120 BPM; one frame's motion step is (BPM / 120) x (60 / frame limit) x frames elapsed (0.5 to 3) x drift speed / 100 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:69-73, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:116, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashMotion.kt:10, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashMotion.kt:21-27, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashClock.kt:25-33, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashMotion.kt:118-119)
- [x] Immersive frame rate: the chosen limit while the window is focused, capped at 30 while battery saver is on (tracked live), 10 fps while another window is in front, none while hidden; a change in the limit eases in by 8% of the gap per frame (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/ImmersiveWash.kt:76-80, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/ImmersiveWash.kt:139-160, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashMotion.kt:89-107) (iPhone: Low Power Mode caps it at 30; a card over the player or an inactive app counts as unfocused; in the background it pauses)
- [x] Artwork: the prepared cover, blurred 80dp and still, scaled 1.12 (overscan) times the open/close dolly scale; covers cross-fade (0 ms for the first, 300 ms when the last change was under 1.5 s ago, else 500 ms, ease in-out cubic); phones below Android 12 use a 64 px copy box-blurred three times (radius 3) instead of a live blur (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:118-121, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:136-161, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashFade.kt:5-15, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashArtwork.kt:53, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashArtwork.kt:93-105)
- [x] Colour: a flat fill of the artwork's dominant colour passed through the same contrast, saturation and brightness cap as covers (falls back to the palette's base colour), easing to a new colour over 500 ms (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:122, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:165-169)
- [x] Classic with Live background on and Android 13+: the four-pool moving mesh; it moves at full pace while playing and eases to 0.15 pace over 1.2 s when paused, colours ease over 520 ms, and frames stop while the app is out of sight. This branch does not check Reduce motion (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:123-126, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/MeshBackground.kt:66-113)
- [x] Classic with Live background off, or on a phone below Android 13: the song's own artwork (not the prepared cover) blurred 80dp at 60% opacity, cropped to fill (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:127-128, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:173-184)
- [x] Brightness cap, saturation and contrast become the cover preparation tuning (contrast factor, saturation / 100, brightness cap / 100): crop to the centre square at 512 px, contrast as (c - 0.5) x contrast + 0.5, then saturation away from luma grey (0.299, 0.587, 0.114), then any pixel whose channel mean is over the cap is darkened by (mean - cap) / cap; a song without artwork gets a 512 px diagonal gradient of its four mesh colours instead (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:47, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashColour.kt:10-78, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashArtwork.kt:45-89)
- [x] Prepared covers are cached by artwork plus contrast, saturation and cap (the last 4 kept), so a tuning change re-prepares the cover and the background cross-fades to it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashArtwork.kt:37, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashArtwork.kt:46, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:77-83)
- [x] The player's words and icons switch between white and dark ink (0xFF141416) from the range of what the chosen mode draws (Immersive: cover range scaled by the wash's final gain; Artwork: cover range; Colour: the prepared dominant colour; Classic: the four mesh colours, or each laid at 60% over the base when not live), keeping 4.5:1 contrast and dimming the background only as far as white words need (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerViewModel.kt:120-135, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:62-78, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/WashReading.kt:63-86)

### Motion (group)

- [x] Group title "Motion", icon OctoIcons.Sparkle, one row "Reduce motion": switch with helper "Backgrounds and lyrics hold still. On by itself when the phone's animations are off." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:123-130) (iPhone: SF Symbol sparkle)
- [x] "Reduce motion" is stored in the "player" store as boolean "reduce_motion", default false; the switch shows only Octo's own stored value (it does not turn on visually when the phone's animations are off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:80, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:160, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:223, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:344-346, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AppearancePage.kt:54-62)
- [x] Everywhere motion is checked, it counts as reduced when Octo's switch is on OR the phone's animator duration scale is 0 (re-read each time the app resumes) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ReduceMotion.kt:21-35) (iPhone: the system Reduce Motion switch, followed live, stands for animations off)
- [x] Effects of reduced motion: the ambient glow stops drifting; the Immersive player wash holds still; the player content calms; flowing lyrics get the engine's reduce motion; the shared LocalReduceMotion (glass popups, rows turning into library songs), the downloads pill and the add-to-library button calm (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientLayer.kt:177-179, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/PlayerBackground.kt:115, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerOverlay.kt:320, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/FlowingLyrics.kt:165, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/NotInLibrary.kt:102, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/downloads/DownloadsPill.kt:121-122, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/AddToLibraryButton.kt:133-134)

## Settings > Library

### Page frame and entry points

- [x] The page is reached from the front page row "Library" (group "Your music", icon OctoIcons.Library), from the account card's phone row, and from search results on the Library page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:205-210, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:315, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:341)
- [x] Front page summary for Library: "Music on this phone not allowed yet" when access is not granted; otherwise "<n> songs" ("1 song" for one, thousands grouped) followed, when any folder is switched off, by " · <n> folders left out" ("1 folder left out" for one) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:53-57)
- [x] Account card phone row text: "<n> songs on this phone" ("1 song on this phone") when access is granted, else "Allow access to the music on this phone"; tapping opens the Library page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:60-61, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:205-210)
- [x] The page uses SettingsPageFrame with large title "Library"; groups in order: "Music on this phone", "Songs found online", then "Music folders" only when at least one folder is known (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:70-104)

### Music on this phone (group)

- [x] Group title "Music on this phone", icon OctoIcons.Phone; rows in order: Music access, Songs, Rescan (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:71-86) (iPhone: SF Symbol iphone)
- [x] Access states: Granted, NotAsked, Denied, DeniedForever; at start the state is Granted if the permission is held, else NotAsked (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:50, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:73-74) (iPhone: iOS asks only once, so a phone that already refused starts at Open settings)
- [x] When access is Granted, "Music access" is an info row with the value "Allowed" on the right and no tap action (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:72-73, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:316-321)
- [x] When access is not Granted, "Music access" is an action row with helper line "Not allowed" and the word on the right "Allow access" (states NotAsked and Denied) or "Open settings" (state DeniedForever); no chevron (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:74-76, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/LibraryReach.kt:80, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:285-312)
- [x] Tapping "Music access" while not DeniedForever shows the system permission prompt for the phone's audio (READ_MEDIA_AUDIO on Android 13+, READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE before); while DeniedForever it opens the app's system settings page instead (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/LibraryReach.kt:60-77, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:69-71) (iPhone: the Music library permission prompt; Open settings opens Octo's page in iOS Settings)
- [x] The prompt's answer sets access: allowed becomes Granted and starts a scan; refused with the system still willing to ask becomes Denied; refused with the system no longer asking becomes DeniedForever (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:118-125, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/LibraryReach.kt:65-69) (iPhone: iOS never asks twice, so a refusal always becomes Open settings; a grant scans)
- [x] Each time the app resumes, access is checked again: newly held becomes Granted and starts a scan; lost while Granted becomes NotAsked (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:127-136, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:225-227)
- [x] Row "Songs": info row, value is the number of library tracks whose source is the phone (source id "device"), formatted with thousands grouping ("%,d", for example "1,234"); shows "0" until counted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:46-47, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:77, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:135-136, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceCatalogBuilder.kt:13)
- [x] Row "Rescan": action row with no chevron; enabled only when access is Granted (title dimmed otherwise); while a scan runs it shows helper "Looking for music…" (with a single ellipsis character), an 18dp accent spinner (stroke 2dp) on the right, and does not respond to taps (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:78-85, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:296-311) (iPhone: the system spinner, small, accent tinted)
- [x] Tapping "Rescan" runs one scan: nothing happens without permission; scans never overlap (a lock); "scanning" is on for the whole scan and off when it ends or fails (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:49-51, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:144-181)
- [x] A scan lists every file the phone's media library marks as music (IS_MUSIC != 0), re-reads tags only for files new or changed (modified time, size, or tag version 2), forgets tags of files gone, rebuilds the folder list from all files, leaves out files in switched-off folders, rebuilds the phone's catalog and merges it with any server's library (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceScanner.kt:44-50, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:150-168, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:187-208) (iPhone: playable, unprotected Music app songs plus files in Octo's own Files folder)
- [x] Scans also run on their own: once at app start, 2 s after the phone's media library stops reporting changes, right after a folder is switched on or off, after access is granted, and after Octo changes phone files (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:98-115, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:138-142)

### Songs found online (group)

- [x] Group title "Songs found online", icon OctoIcons.AddToLibrary; one switch row "Library songs only" with helper "Shows only songs in your library: albums, search, artists and radio leave out outside songs." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:87-89, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/discovery/LibraryOnly.kt:5-6) (iPhone: SF Symbol plus)
- [x] Stored in the "player" store as boolean "library_only", default false (shows off until read) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:163, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:226, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:356-358, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:39-40)
- [x] The same setting is toggled by the "Library songs only" filter chip on the Search page (shown only when signed in to a server); both stay in sync (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/search/SearchScreen.kt:140-142, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/NotInLibrary.kt:54-73)
- [x] Effect on album pages: songs found online (finds) are left out of the list and of play/shuffle; the album page's own "Only my songs" toggle is not offered while the setting is on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumScreen.kt:122-139, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumScreen.kt:273, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumExtras.kt:38-39)
- [x] Effect on artist pages: the artist's outside extras are reduced to library songs only (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/artist/ArtistScreen.kt:168-169)
- [x] Effect on Search: top songs and chart lists keep library songs only, and the server's online results are not shown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/search/SearchScreen.kt:103-107, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/search/SearchScreen.kt:146)
- [x] Effect on radio: the server's suggested songs that are finds are dropped before the radio mix (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/LibraryRadio.kt:56, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/LibraryRadio.kt:67, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/discovery/LibraryOnly.kt:11-12)

### Music folders (group, only when folders are known)

- [x] Group title "Music folders", icon OctoIcons.Folder; shown only when the folder list is not empty (it is empty until the first scan of this app run finishes) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:90-91, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:79-81, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:155) (iPhone: SF Symbol folder)
- [x] First row is a note "Switch a folder off to leave it out." (caption, muted) carrying the "music_folders" search id, so a search for it lights this note (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:92, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:325-329)
- [x] Then one switch row per folder, folders being the top level of each music file's relative path (for example "Music", "Download"), grouped across storages, sorted by song count, most first, including switched-off folders (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/Folders.kt:17-31) (iPhone: the folders are "Music app" and the top-level folders of Octo's Files folder ("Octo" for files at its top), most songs first, switched-off ones included)
- [x] Each folder row: title is the folder name, or "Phone storage" when the name is empty (files at the storage root); helper "<n> songs" with thousands grouping and always the plural (so a one-song folder reads "1 songs"); checked when included (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:93-101)
- [x] Folder switches have no search entry of their own (no highlight id) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LibraryPage.kt:94-95)
- [x] Switching a folder off adds its name to the string set "excluded_folders" in the "library" preferences store; switching on removes it; default empty, so new folders are always in (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/Folders.kt:60-79)
- [x] A change to the excluded set triggers a rescan straight away, after which the folder's songs leave (or rejoin) the library; tags are kept for every file so switching a folder back on needs no re-reading (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:103-104, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceLibrary.kt:151-156)
- [x] The excluded folders list is saved in a backup and replaced whole on restore (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:170, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/Folders.kt:75-78)

## Settings > Lyrics

### Page frame and entry

- [x] The page is reached from the front page row "Lyrics" (group "Lyrics and history", icon OctoIcons.Lyrics) whose summary is "Found online" (lyrics online on) or "From your music only" (off), then " · Screen stays on" when keep screen on is on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:321, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:99-102)
- [x] SettingsPageFrame with large title "Lyrics"; groups in order: an untitled group (Find lyrics online, Keep the screen on), "Synced lyrics", "Timing", "Look" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:121-185)
- [x] Before values are read the page shows defaults: lyrics online on, keep screen on on, the default look, and the output's automatic timing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:55-62)

### Untitled group

- [x] Row "Find lyrics online": switch with helper "From LRCLIB when your music has none. Sends only the title, artist, album and length."; stored in the "player" store as boolean "lyrics_online", default true; when off, no online lookup is made (lookups also need a non-blank title and artist) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:123-128, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:55, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:129, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:200, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsRepository.kt:177-178, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsRepository.kt:213-217)
- [x] Row "Keep the screen on": switch with no helper line; stored in the "lyrics" preferences store as boolean "keep_screen_on", default true; when on, the screen is held on while found lyrics show in the player (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:129-133, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:135-137, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:219-224, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/LyricsPane.kt:191-194)

### Synced lyrics (group)

- [x] Group title "Synced lyrics", icon OctoIcons.Lyrics; one ChoiceRow "Lyrics style" whose value is "Flowing" or "Classic" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:135-136)
- [x] Tapping "Lyrics style" opens the popup titled "Lyrics style" with answers in order: "Flowing" with detail "Lines ripple into place, words fill with light and long notes bloom."; "Classic" with detail "The line being sung grows and its words brighten in place." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:137-144, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLook.kt:7-10) (iPhone: a system menu beside the row, titled "Lyrics style", with each answer's line)
- [x] Stored in the "lyrics" store under string key "lyrics_style" as "Flowing" or "Classic"; default Flowing; an unknown value reads as Flowing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:20, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:32, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:39-41)

### Timing (group)

- [x] Group title "Timing", icon OctoIcons.Timer, footer under the panel "Words late? Tap Earlier. Early? Tap Later. One song is set from the player's lyrics menu." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:147-151)
- [x] First row: info row whose title is the output playing now's label ("Phone speaker", "Headphones" for a cable, the Bluetooth device's name or "Bluetooth" when it has none, the USB device's name or "USB audio"), not the index title; it follows output changes live (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:152-157, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sound/SoundEngine.kt:40, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sound/SoundEngine.kt:55-71) (iPhone: "iPhone speaker" in place of "Phone speaker"; AirPlay and CarPlay outputs have their own names; it follows route changes live)
- [x] The info row's value is the output's timing as a signed number of seconds: "0 s" at zero, "+<s> s" later, "-<s> s" earlier, trailing zeros stripped (for example "+0.75 s", "-0.3 s", "-0.5 s") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:154, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:126-132)
- [x] The info row's helper: "Every song on this output." when the listener set a timing (0 included); while on the automatic timing, "Every song on this output. Automatic: 0.5 s earlier, the usual for Bluetooth." for a Bluetooth output, or "Every song on this output. Automatic: 0.3 s earlier, the usual for a speaker or a cable." for any other output (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:156, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:89-95, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:119-124)
- [x] Automatic timing (never set by the listener): -500 ms for an output whose key starts "bluetooth:", -300 ms for every other output (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:66-74, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sound/SoundEngine.kt:43)
- [x] Under the info row (a separate row in the same panel, padded 12dp top and 8dp at each side): two equal buttons "Earlier" and "Later", 8dp apart; "Earlier" moves the timing 50 ms down and is disabled at -2000 ms; "Later" moves it 50 ms up and is disabled at +2000 ms (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:158-166, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/TimingAdjuster.kt:80-93, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:27-29) (iPhone: Liquid Glass Earlier and Later buttons, 8 apart, 50 ms a tap, stopping at 2,000 ms either way)
- [x] A step starts from the output's current timing (its automatic one at first, or one saved under a key the output had before), is held to plus or minus 2000 ms, and is saved under the output's current key only (0 included), removing any former keys (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:55-56, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:196-202)
- [x] Under the buttons, centered in a row at least 44dp tall: the text button "Use automatic"; tappable at full opacity only while the output has a timing the listener set; at 40% opacity and not tappable while on the automatic timing; tapping removes the output's saved timing under every key it may be saved under (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:164-165, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/TimingAdjuster.kt:94-107, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:204-207)
- [x] Output timings are stored in the "lyrics" store as long values under "lyrics_output_offset:<output key>" (keys like "speaker", "wired", "usb:<name>", "bluetooth:<name>") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:141, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:145, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sound/SoundEngine.kt:55-71)

### Look (group)

- [x] Group title "Look", icon OctoIcons.Appearance, footer "For the Flowing style."; the sliders and switches are always shown and enabled, whichever style is chosen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:168-184)
- [x] Seven sliders in this order, each a live slider with value label "<n>%": Emphasis, Glow, Lift, Motion speed, Inactive line size, Fill softness, Ripple (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:100-108, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:169-171, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLook.kt:13-21)

- [x] Each look slider snaps to steps of 5: picked = round((range start + fraction x span) / 5) x 5, held to the range, saved only when it differs; saving holds it to the range again; stored in the "lyrics" store (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:45-46, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:190-203, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:24, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:43-45)

- [x] Row "Curved lyrics": switch with helper "Lines bend round a drum."; stored boolean "lyrics_arc" in the "lyrics" store, default false (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/LyricsPage.kt:178-183, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:22, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:34)
- [x] The whole look (style, seven dials, keep sung lines, curved), keep screen on, and every output timing are saved in a backup and put back on restore (output timings restored 0 included, held to plus or minus 2000 ms) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:109-113, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:176-179, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:57-67, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:212-217)

## Settings > Server and sync: opening and frame

### How the page is reached

- [x] The page is SettingsPage.Server, titled "Server and sync"; it is the second row of the "Your music" group on the Settings front page (after Library), with the cloud icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:17, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:314-316)
- [x] Front page row summary under "Server and sync": "" (empty) while the saved sign-in is still loading, "Not connected" when signed out, else serverStatus with no place: "Syncing…" while a library copy runs, otherwise "Synced {time ago}" or "Not synced yet" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:299-303, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:76-91) (iPhone: the saved sign-in is read at launch before the page draws, so there is no loading moment)
- [x] "Synced {time ago}" is timeAgo with its first letter lowercased: "Synced just now", "Synced 5 min ago", "Synced 3 hr ago", "Synced yesterday", "Synced 4 days ago" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:64-76)
- [x] The front page re-reads the time every 30 seconds (CLOCK_MS 30,000 ms), so its "Synced ..." line ticks (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:214-221)
- [x] Settings account card, signed in: a row titled with the server's name (cloud icon tile), first line the username (or "Signed in with a key" when the username is empty) in secondary colour, second line the sync problem in error colour if the last copy failed, else serverStatus with place: "Connected at home" or "Connected away" (only when the connection has a home address) joined by " · " with "Synced ..."/"Not synced yet", or "Syncing…"; tapping opens this page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:185-202, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:81-91)
- [x] Settings account card, signed out but servers kept: row "Your servers" with line "Signed out. Sign in to one, or add another." opens this page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:176-184)
- [x] Settings account card, signed out with no kept servers: row "Connect a music server" with line "Add the music on your own server" opens the sign-in screen directly, not this page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:169-175)
- [x] A settings search result for one of this page's entries opens this page with that entry's id as the highlight (route SettingsPageRoute(page, highlight)) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:125-126, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:272-279)

### Frame

- [x] The page is a back button over a vertically scrolling column: the large screen title "Server and sync", then the groups one under another, 24dp apart (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:27-52, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:182)
- [x] Each group is a raised rounded panel (corner 20dp) inset 16dp from the screen sides; a group title, when present, sits above the panel as uppercase muted label text with 0.6sp letter spacing, led by the group's 15dp icon; accessibility reads the title as a heading in its normal case (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:77-103)
- [x] Rows inside a group are separated by a 1 physical pixel hairline (text primary at 10% alpha) inset 16dp from the start; a row that draws nothing takes no space and gets no hairline (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:105-141)
- [x] An optional group footer shows under the panel as a muted caption, 16dp side padding, 8dp above (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:124-126)
- [x] Rows show only their title (and an explicit helper when the page passes one); the summary and keywords written in SettingsIndex are used only by search and are never drawn under the row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:180-186, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:284-321)
- [x] Rows are at least 56dp tall with 16dp horizontal and 12dp vertical padding; a title is body text, a helper line is a muted caption 2dp under it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:145-186)
- [x] An info row with an empty value (Its folders, each Listening now row) still keeps the value's share of the width (title column weight 1 against value weight 0.8), so its title and helper wrap within about 55% of the row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:180-186, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:233-244, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:315-321)
- [x] Note rows (the music folder error, the sync problem) are a caption with no minimum height, in error colour on this page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:323-329, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:227, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:251)
- [x] Switch rows toggle from a tap anywhere on the row (role switch); the switch itself is hidden from accessibility so the row reads once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:188-202)
- [x] Action rows show a 20dp muted chevron unless told not to; a busy action row shows an 18dp accent spinner instead and ignores taps; a destructive action row has its title in error colour (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:282-312)
- [x] The row a search pointed at is lit once per visit: after 350 ms it is scrolled into view, an accent wash at 14% alpha fades in over 250 ms, holds 900 ms and fades out over 700 ms; coming back to the page does not light it again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:62-70, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:145-177, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:34-36)
- [x] A search entry whose row is not shown on the current server (for example Octo admin on a plain Navidrome server, Change password with an API key, Connection without a home address, Shared links on a server that refused sharing, Scan library now for a non-admin, Response time before it is known) still appears in search results; opening it lights nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:241-347, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:202-239, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:104-106)
- [x] The "Listening now" search entry is attached to no row (only the group title uses its title), so opening it lights nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:108-111)

### Search entries for this page (SettingsIndex)
