### What shows in each state, in order

- [x] Loading (the saved sign-in is still being read): only the title shows, no groups (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:184-186) (iPhone: the sign-in is read before the first frame, so there is no loading state to show)
- [x] Not loading and at least one server kept: the "Your servers" group leads the page, whether a server is in use or not (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:183-184)
- [x] Signed out with no kept servers: a single untitled group holding the action row "Connect a server" (chevron) with the footer "Your server's music joins your library here."; tapping the row opens the sign-in screen (SignInRoute) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:187-193, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:242-245)
- [x] Signed out with kept servers (none in use): only the "Your servers" group shows; no Connection, Server, Sync, On the server, Listening now or Sign out groups (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:187)
- [x] Signed in, in order: Your servers, Connection, Server, Sync, On the server, Listening now (only while someone else is listening), then an untitled group with "Sign out" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:184, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:194-268, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:103-112)

## Your servers list (ServersSection)

### Group

- [x] Group title "Your servers" with the Servers icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:111, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:246-250) (iPhone: SF Symbol server.rack)
- [x] One row per kept server in stored order (a new server is appended at the end of the list; editing keeps its place), then the "Add a server" row last (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:112-130, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:331, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:339)
- [x] The first server row carries the search entry "Your servers" (id your_servers), so searching it lights the first row; other server rows have no entry (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:120-121)
- [x] While the list is on screen every kept server is checked (one check per server, all at once) when the list first appears, again whenever the server in use changes, and again whenever the number of kept servers changes; earlier answers stay shown until a new answer replaces them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:79-81, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:108-116)

### Server row

- [x] Row minimum height 64dp, 16dp horizontal and 12dp vertical padding, 12dp between its parts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:161, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:165-176)
- [x] The server in use has a darker unlit pill (GlazeSelected) behind its row, inset 6dp horizontally and 4dp vertically, corner radius 14dp; the row is marked "selected" for accessibility (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:66, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:159-160)
- [x] Line 1: the server name in body style, one line with ellipsis, secondary colour when the server is signed out, primary otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:164-171)
- [x] Server name = the label the listener gave it, trimmed; when empty, the host of its address; when the address cannot be read, the address with a trailing "/" removed (e.g. label " Home " gives "Home"; "https://music.example.com:4533/" gives "music.example.com") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:25-27, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:48)
- [x] Line 1 also shows the caption "In use" in secondary colour after the name, only on the server in use (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:172)
- [x] Line 2: muted caption, one line with ellipsis: "{username} at {address without its scheme and trailing slash}", or only the address part when the username is blank (e.g. "winters at music.example.com", "music.example.com:4533") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:174-180, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:29-33)
- [x] Line 3: the status line caption (no line limit); in SignalOrange when the row is not in use and its last check was Unreachable or WrongPassword, muted otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:181-185, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:56-57)
- [x] Right side while this row is the one being switched to: a 16dp spinner in secondary colour and the caption "Switching", 8dp apart, in place of the button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:188-191)
- [x] Right side on a row not in use: a text button "Switch", or "Sign in" when the server is signed out; label style, accent colour, 4dp horizontal and 10dp vertical touch padding, role button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:192-199)
- [x] While any switch is running, every row's "Switch"/"Sign in" text turns muted and cannot be tapped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:118, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:195-197)
- [x] The row in use has no Switch button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:187-200)
- [x] Every row ends with a "more" icon (horizontal dots, 20dp, secondary tint, 8dp padding) whose accessibility label is "More for {name}"; its menu pops up beside that icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:201-210, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:104-106) (iPhone: a system menu from the ellipsis button, labeled "More for {name}")

### Status line words

- [x] The kind is the server type with its first letter uppercased, a space, and the version, blank parts left out; "Subsonic server" when neither is known (e.g. "Octo 0.9.3", "Navidrome 0.58.0") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:12-17)
- [x] While this row is being switched to: "Switching to it now" (checked first) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:46-47)
- [x] Signed out: "Signed out. Sign in again to use it." (whatever the last check said) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:48)
- [x] In use, or not checked yet, or check answer Unknown: the kind alone (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:49)
- [x] Answered: "{kind} · Answered in {ms} ms" under 1,000 ms, "{kind} · Answered in {seconds with one decimal} s" from 1,000 ms (e.g. 1,260 ms gives "Answered in 1.3 s", English number format); the kind alone if no time was measured (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:50, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:43-45)
- [x] Did not take the saved password: "{kind} · Didn't take the saved password" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:51)
- [x] Out of reach: "{kind} · Out of reach right now" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:52)

### Checking each server

- [x] A server no longer kept answers Unknown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:383-384)
- [x] The server in use is pinged with the session's own client (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:385-387)
- [x] A server not in use that is signed out or has no saved password is not asked: Unknown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:389)
- [x] A server whose saved secret or connection can no longer be opened: Unknown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:390-391)
- [x] A server whose saved address cannot be read: Unreachable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:392) (iPhone: addresses are kept as checked http(s) addresses, so an unreadable one cannot be kept)
- [x] Other servers are pinged with a client of their own (their own custom headers, trusted certificates and client certificate, User-Agent "Octo/{version} (Android)") with a 6 second connect and call timeout, never touching the shared client of the server in use (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:57, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:393, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ServerClients.kt:21-37)
- [x] A ping that answers gives Answers with the elapsed milliseconds; wrong credentials give WrongPassword; any other failure gives Unreachable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:395-401)

### Switch

- [x] Tapping "Sign in" on a signed-out server opens the server form in Sign in again mode for it, without contacting the server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:84-87)
- [x] Tapping "Switch" asks the switch to move to that server and waits for its outcome (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:88-89)
- [x] Outcome Done with a notice: a toast with a check icon shows the notice; Done without a notice shows nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:90, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Feedback.kt:86)
- [x] Outcome NeedsPassword: opens the server form in Sign in again mode for that server, with the note (if any) shown as the form's error line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:91, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:381)
- [x] Outcome Failed: a plain toast (no icon) with the message (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:92)
- [x] Toasts stay 3.5 seconds (6 seconds when they carry an action), sit above the bottom bar, and tapping one without an action dismisses it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Feedback.kt:55-57, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Feedback.kt:102-147)
- [x] Only one switch runs at a time: asking while one runs answers Failed "Still switching. Try again in a moment." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:141-143)
- [x] The switch runs in an app-wide scope, so leaving the page does not cut it in half; the switching id clears when it ends however it ends (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:139-154)
- [x] Switching to a server no longer in the list: Failed "That server isn't in your list any more." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:351)
- [x] Switching to the server already in use: Done with no notice (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:353, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:61-62)
- [x] Switching to a server that is signed out or has no saved password: NeedsPassword with no note (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:354)
- [x] Switching when the saved secret or the saved connection can no longer be opened: NeedsPassword with no note (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:355-356)
- [x] Switching to a server whose address cannot be read: Failed "That server's address can't be read. Edit it and try again." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:357-358) (iPhone: addresses are kept as checked http(s) addresses, so the "can't be read" case cannot happen)
- [x] The server is first pinged with a client of its own (6 s timeout) at its main address; if the main address cannot connect (and the failure was not a certificate problem) and a home address is set, the home address is pinged instead; everything stays as it was if both fail (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:360-366, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:587-593)
- [x] The server refusing the saved password: NeedsPassword with the note "{name} didn't take the saved password. It may have been changed." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:364, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:69)
- [x] Any other failure: Failed "Couldn't switch to {name}. {plain words for the error}" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:365, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:67, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:6-19)
- [x] On success the server's saved type, version and extension list are refreshed from what it said (old values kept for what it did not say), the server in use puts its things away, the new one is saved as active and becomes the session (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:367-378)
- [x] Switch notice: none when no server was in use before; "Now on {new name}." normally; "Now on {new name}. The music from {old name} stopped, and its queue is kept for when you come back." when music was playing and the switch stopped it (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:59-65, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:145-147)
- [x] Putting the old server's things away: the player stops (the last song's play counts for the old server, casting ends, the player is emptied), the play is noted, the old queue is saved on the old server (waiting at most 3 seconds), and the queue is handed over so it waits on the phone for when that server comes back (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:202-217, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:55, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:152-164)
- [x] After the change the app waits for the new server's library to be in place (at most 30 seconds, then carries on anyway), brings the new server's own queue back paused, and refreshes a car's browse tree (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:41-43, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:230-240)

### Row menu

- [x] The "more" icon opens a choice list titled with the server's name, with no current choice (options act as plain buttons, not radio choices), placed beside the icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:97-109, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:76-85, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:80-86)
- [x] Options, in order: "Edit" (always); "Change password" (only on the server in use, and only when it is not signed in with an API key); "Sign in" when the server is signed out, otherwise "Sign out"; "Remove" (always) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:98-107)
- [x] Picking an option closes the list, then runs it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:167-173) (iPhone: the system menu closes, then runs the action)
- [x] "Edit" opens the server form in Edit mode for that server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:99, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/Routes.kt:62-75)
- [x] "Change password" opens the Change password popup (the same one as the Connection row) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:100, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:184)
- [x] "Sign in" opens the server form in Sign in again mode for that server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:102)
- [x] "Sign out" acts at once with no question and no toast: for the server in use it stops a running library copy and then signs out; for another server it signs only that one out (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:104, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:172-177, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:191-198)
- [x] Signing out of a kept server keeps it in the list with signedOut true and its saved password or key cleared; its custom header values and certificate stay sealed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:408-419)
- [x] Signing out of the server in use also puts its things away (with no next server), leaves no server active, clears the home/away chooser and the connection security, and the state becomes signed out; its copy of the library stays for signing in again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:414-420, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:438-443, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:191-198)
- [x] "Remove" opens the Remove popup for that server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:106)

### Remove popup

- [x] A glass popup in the middle of the screen (no anchor), accessibility pane title "Remove" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:219-225, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:226) (iPhone: a system confirmation dialog)
- [x] Question, in body style, up to 3 lines, read as a heading: "Remove {name}?" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:228, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/PopupForms.kt:64-71) (iPhone: the dialog's title "Remove {name}?")
- [x] Detail line: "It leaves your list and Octo forgets its password." followed by " You'll be signed out of it now." only when it is the server in use, followed by " Your music and playlists stay on the server." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:229-231)
- [x] Under the detail, a switch row titled "Also delete what's kept here for it", off each time the popup opens for a server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:218, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:235-246) (iPhone: a second destructive button "Remove, and also delete what's kept here for it" in place of the switch; plain "Remove" is the off choice)
- [x] Its helper while off: "Off keeps its queue, live lists and downloads on this phone, in case you add it again." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:243) (iPhone: the dialog's message line)
- [x] Its helper while on: "Its queue, live lists and downloads on this phone go too." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:241) (iPhone: the dialog's message line)
- [x] Buttons side by side, 10dp apart, 18dp under the content: accent "Remove", glaze "Cancel" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:232-234, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/PopupForms.kt:76-79) (iPhone: the dialog's buttons plus the system Cancel)
- [x] "Cancel", a tap outside the card or back closes it with nothing changed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:141, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:222, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:187-239)
- [x] "Remove" closes the popup, starts the removal in the background, and at once shows a toast with a check: "Removed {name}." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:136-140)

### What removing does

- [x] Removing the server in use first stops a running library copy (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:203-206)
- [x] The server leaves the list with its sealed password; if it was in use its things are put away, no server is active and the state becomes signed out (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:423-433)
- [x] When the list becomes empty, the vault's key is deleted from the keystore (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:433-434, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/CredentialVault.kt:37-39) (iPhone: each server's secrets are its own Keychain items, deleted with it; there is no shared vault key)
- [x] Its copy of the library is deleted unless another kept server has the same address (source id); its last-copy record (synced_at, songs, albums, rows_version, source_id under its id) is removed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:200-211, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:296-306)
- [x] Playlists linked with a server no longer kept stay on the phone as phone-only playlists (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:304-305)
- [x] With "Also delete what's kept here for it" on, the phone also forgets for that server's id: its saved queue, plays waiting to be sent, favourite-star sync state, playlist deletions and its claim on the address, recent playlists, live lists, server queue save and offer marks, and its Octo admin state (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:179-196)
- [x] With the switch on, its offline downloads are forgotten too, unless another kept server shares the address (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:196-198)
- [x] With the switch off, those per-server stores stay; adding the same account again gives it the same id (the account key), so they come back (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:179-187, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:71-76)
- [x] After removing the server in use the page shows only the Your servers list when other servers are kept, or the "Connect a server" group when none are left (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:184-193)

("this one" in place of the name when none is in use) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServersSection.kt:126-130)

## Connection group (signed in only)

- [x] Group title "Connection" with the globe icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:199)
- [x] "Address": an info row whose value is the server's main address with any trailing "/" removed; it is never the home address, whichever address calls go to (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:200, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:256-259)
- [x] Info values sit at the row's end in secondary colour, small body text, right aligned, at most 2 lines with ellipsis, taking up to 0.8 of the title's share of the width (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:233-244)
- [x] "Connection": an info row shown only when the connection has a home address; value "Connected at home" when the home address is in use, else "Connected away"; it follows changes live (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:201-204, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:260-263)
- [x] Home is used only when a home address is set, the phone's active network is Wi-Fi or Ethernet, and the home address answered a ping just now; anything else uses the main address (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/HomeAddress.kt:13-17, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:105-117)
- [x] The home ping has a 2 second time limit; an error from the server itself still counts as answering, only "cannot connect" and "not a music server" count as no (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:53-54, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:184, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:595-602)
- [x] Each new session starts as Away and checks at once; it checks again on a new network or a change between local and not local (signal changes alone do not count), when the network is lost, and after a request to the server failed to connect (at most one such check every 5 seconds); a home address equal to the main address counts as none (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:20-22, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:46-103)
- [x] Failures that count as "could not connect": connection refused, no route to host, unknown host, socket timeout, or an interrupted call whose message is "timeout" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/HomeAddress.kt:19-25, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionSecurity.kt:96-107)
- [x] While Home, every call (API, covers, streams) goes to the home address; a change applies to the next call (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:82-86, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:536)
- [x] "User": an info row with the signed-in username, or "API key" when the username is empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:205, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:264-267)
- [x] "Music folder": a choice row (title, value at the end, 18dp chevron) whose value is the chosen folder's name, or "All" when none is chosen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:206, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:207-227) (iPhone: a system menu row with an up and down chevron)
- [x] Tapping "Music folder" reads the server's folders (getMusicFolders); if that fails an error-coloured note row "Couldn't read the server's music folders." appears under the row and nothing opens (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:207-212, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:227) (iPhone: a system menu opens only from a tap, so after a read that failed the row is a retry button showing the note, and a retry that works opens the folders on the next tap)
- [x] When the folders are read, the note is cleared and a choice list titled "Music folder" opens beside the row: first "All" with the detail line "Every folder on the server", then each folder by name with no detail (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:213-219) (iPhone: a system menu beside the row: "All" with "Every folder on the server", then the folders' names)
- [x] In that list the current folder is the selected choice; "All" is selected when no folder is chosen or the chosen one is no longer listed; a server listing no folders shows only "All" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:214, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:218)
- [x] Picking the choice already in use (same folder id, or All when none) does nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:220-222)
- [x] Picking another choice saves it in this server's connection, rebuilds the session so every library call carries that musicFolderId (none for All), then copies the library again, stopping a copy already running (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:139-145, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:480-490, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:182-189)
- [x] The folder is kept per server; editing the connection keeps it only when the main address did not change (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:267-268)
- [x] "Edit connection": action row with chevron; opens the sign-in screen in edit mode for the server in use (EditConnectionRoute) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:228, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:400)
- [x] "Change password": action row with chevron, shown unless the server in use is signed in with an API key; opens the Change password popup (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:229)
- [x] "Octo admin": action row with chevron, shown only when the server runs Octo; opens the Octo admin screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:230)
- [x] "Import": action row with chevron, shown only when the server runs Octo; opens the Import screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:231)
- [x] A server runs Octo when its status page answered at sign-in, or it calls itself "octo" (any case), or any of its extensions starts with "octo" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/OctoProbe.kt:13-19)
- [x] The status page check at sign-in: GET {address}/api/admin/status with a 5 second limit; Octo when it answers with success and a JSON object that has an "octo" key; it only answers on the home network, which is why the extensions also count (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/OctoProbe.kt:21-34, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:306-307)
- [x] "Family": action row with chevron, shown only while the server lists the extension octoFamily at version 1 (the text "octoFamily:1" among its extensions); opens the Family screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:232-233, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:41-42)
- [x] "Sign in on another device": action row with chevron, same condition as Family; tapping opens the "Sign in on another device" part of the Family screen (the hand-over) and then opens the Family screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:234-239, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:151-152)
- [x] Order inside the group: Address, Connection (if home), User, Music folder, folder error note (if any), Edit connection, Change password (if not API key), Octo admin (if Octo), Import (if Octo), Family (if family), Sign in on another device (if family) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:199-240)
- [x] At every app start the server in use is asked for its extensions again; when it answers with a list that is not empty and differs from the saved one, the saved list and the session are updated (only if the same session is still in use), so rows that depend on extensions appear without signing in again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:192-217)

## Server group (signed in only)

- [x] Group title "Server" with the info icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:289)
- [x] "Server": an info row whose value is the kind ("Navidrome 0.58.0", "Octo 0.9.3", "Subsonic server") and whose helper line says what it offers (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:290, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:12-17)
- [x] Offers helper: "Offers synced lyrics and adding songs you find online to your library." when both; "Offers synced lyrics." when only lyrics; "Offers adding songs you find online to your library." when only adding; no helper when neither (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:19-23, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:157-161)
- [x] Lyrics counts when an extension starts with "songLyrics:" or "octoLyrics:"; adding counts when one starts with "octoAcquisitions:" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:158-161)
- [x] For each new session object the page takes one quick look: it times a ping and asks getScanStatus; nothing is shown for these until the look ends (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:286-288, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:463-472)
- [x] "Response time": an info row with value "{ms} ms" (always milliseconds, even past a second), shown only once the look ended and the ping answered (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:291, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:284-287)
- [x] "Its folders": an info row with an empty value and the scan words as its helper line, shown only when there are scan words (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:292, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:288-291)
- [x] Scan words while the server scans: "Reading its folders now, {count with commas} files so far." when the count is above 0, else "Reading its folders now." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:47-53)
- [x] Scan words when idle with a readable last scan time (ISO instant or offset date-time): "Last read its folders {ago}." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:54-55, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerQueues.kt:63-68)
- [x] No scan words (row hidden) when the server gave no scan status, or is idle and its last scan time is missing or unreadable (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:50, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:54)
- [x] {ago}: "just now" (under 1 minute), "a minute ago", "{n} minutes ago" (under 60), "an hour ago", "{n} hours ago" (under 24), "yesterday" (under 2 days), "{n} days ago" (under 7), otherwise "on {d MMMM}" (e.g. "on 12 September"), with the year ("on 12 September 2025") when it was another year, in English month names and the phone's time zone; future times count as now (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerStats.kt:58-76)
- [x] The scan words are worked out against the time at the moment the row is drawn; they do not tick on their own (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:292)

## Sync group (signed in only)

- [x] Group title "Sync" with the sync icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:244)
- [x] "Last synced": an info row whose value is how long ago this server's library was last copied: "Just now" (under 1 minute), "{n} min ago" (under 1 hour), "{n} hr ago" (under 24 hours), "Yesterday" (under 48 hours), else "{n} days ago"; "Not yet" when there is no copy record for this server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:245, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:63-73)
- [x] The Last synced value is worked out when the page redraws; it does not tick on its own (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:245)
- [x] The copy record is kept per server id (source_id@{id}, synced_at@{id}, songs@{id}, albums@{id}) and only counts when its source id matches the session's (host plus port when not the default) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:115-124, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:197, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerCatalog.kt:26-32)
- [x] When a copy record exists, two info rows follow: "Songs" and "Albums", values with the phone locale's grouping separators ("%,d"), with no search entry (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:246-249)
- [x] "Sync now": action row with no chevron; tapping starts a copy unless one is already running (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:250, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:166-172)
- [x] While a copy runs, the "Sync now" row shows an 18dp accent spinner (2dp stroke) at its end and cannot be tapped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:250, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:296-310) (iPhone: the system spinner, small, accent tinted; the row cannot be tapped while busy)
- [x] When the last copy failed, an error-coloured note row with the failure words shows under "Sync now"; it clears when a copy works and when another server (or none) comes into use (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:251, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:106-108, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:143-146, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:278, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:288-290)
- [x] Failure words: cannot connect "Couldn't reach the server. Check the address, and that you're on the right network."; server busy "The server isn't answering right now. It may be restarting, so try again in a moment."; not a music server "That address answered, but not like a music server. Check the port."; wrong credentials "Wrong username or password."; token sign-in refused with code 41 "This account can't sign in with a token. Turn on Legacy sign-in under Advanced."; other sign-in refusal "The server doesn't take this way of signing in."; not found "That isn't on the server any more."; any other server error "The server said: {message}"; anything else "Something went wrong." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:5-19)
- [x] A copy reads the whole library, replaces the server's rows, rebuilds the merged library, then in order brings likes and stars together (sending plays still waiting), favourite albums and artists, downloads that arrived (joining as liked), playlists, and offline downloads; then records source id, time, song and album counts, rows version and which account the address's rows belong to (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:250-277)
- [x] When a server comes into use (sign in, switch, app start), a running copy stops, the library becomes that server's (rebuilt when the merged server changed), and a copy starts if: never copied, copied by an older rows version (current is 6), the address's rows belong to another account, or the copy is older than 6 hours (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:56-71, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerSync.kt:126-164)
- [x] "Sync play queue with the server": a switch row (the whole row toggles) with the helper "Keeps what's playing on the server, so you can pick up where another device left off." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:76-86, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:320-323)
- [x] Its value is stored as Boolean key "sync_on" in the "server_queue" store, one value for every server, default on; the switch shows on until the stored value is read (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:83, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:105-106, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:302, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:43)
- [x] Turning it off cancels a queue save waiting to go and takes away an offer to carry on from another device; while off, nothing is saved and no offer is looked for (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:111-119, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:218-222, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:250-251)
- [x] While on: the queue is saved on the server 10 seconds after the queue or the current song last changed (skipped when only the position moved), at once with the exact position when playback pauses, and when leaving a server (waiting at most 3 seconds); the queue brought back at launch is not itself a change (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:48-55, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:121-164, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:248-276)
- [x] A saved queue holds at most 400 songs, cut around the current song keeping 50 before it; songs the server lacks are left out; with shuffle on the songs go in playing order; a current song not on the server hands over to the next one that is, from its start (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerQueues.kt:12-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:57-75)
- [x] Servers listing the "indexBasedQueue" extension are saved and read by index; others by song id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:229-231, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:257-261)
- [x] "Save new playlists to the server": a switch row with the helper "New playlists are made on your server too.", shown while a server is signed in (it appears once the signed-in state is read, hidden before) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:128-130, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:253-260, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playlists/PlaylistSync.kt:65-66)
- [x] Its value is stored as Boolean key "new_on_server" in the "playlist_sync" store, one value for every server, default off ("nothing goes to the server unasked") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playlists/PlaylistSyncStore.kt:25-26, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playlists/PlaylistSyncStore.kt:48-53)
- [x] While on, a playlist made on the phone is also made on the server in use and kept in step with it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playlists/PlaylistSync.kt:97-106)
- [x] Order inside the group: Last synced, Songs, Albums, Sync now, sync problem note, Sync play queue with the server, Save new playlists to the server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:244-261)

- [x] Changing the username of a kept server gives it a new id (its own plays and queue); the first kept account on an address gets the account key, later ones "{key}-2", "{key}-3" and so on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:308-314, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:71-76, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/livelists/LiveList.kt:94-100)
- [x] When signing in with an API key the server is asked whose key it is (tokenInfo) and that name becomes the username (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:299-303)
- [x] On success the password and key fields are emptied; secrets are kept only sealed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:508-513)
- [x] A token sign-in refused with code 41 (the server cannot check tokens) is tried again at once with Legacy sign-in turned on, when the address is https or a home-network host; over plain http elsewhere it stops with "This server needs the password sent as it is, which isn't safe over http. Use https, or turn on Legacy sign-in under Advanced." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:515-530, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/LegacySignIn.kt:22-35)
- [x] Other failures show their plain words (the failure words list in the Sync group) as the error line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:528, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:21-29)
- [x] A failed try puts the security settings of the server in use back, so a bad edit never disturbs it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:273-285)

### Certificate trust question

- [x] When the main or home address shows a certificate the phone does not trust, a centred popup (at most 380dp wide, pane title "Trust this certificate", scrolling) asks "Trust this certificate?" with the detail "This phone doesn't trust the certificate the server showed. If it's your own server's, check the fingerprint matches before you trust it. Octo will trust this one certificate, and only for this host." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:718-743, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:286-296, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:604-613)
- [x] Under it: caption "Host" and the host, then caption "SHA-256 fingerprint" and the fingerprint as uppercase hex pairs joined by colons, in monospace (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:744-751, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/Certificates.kt:17-28)
- [x] Buttons: accent "Trust this certificate" and glaze "Cancel" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:738-740)
- [x] Trusting pins exactly that certificate (by SHA-256) for that host only (host in lowercase) and tries the save again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:560-566, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/Certificates.kt:30-36)
- [x] Cancel, outside or back: error line "Not connected. The server's certificate wasn't trusted." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:568-571)
- [x] A pinned certificate is accepted for its host even when the name on it does not match; every certificate is first checked the normal way, and a pin is used only when that fails (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/Certificates.kt:33-108)

## Stored state behind the page

- [x] Kept servers live in the "session" store under key "servers" as a JSON list; each has serverUrl, username, passwordSealed, serverType, serverVersion, isOcto, octoAdminReachable, extensions, connection (JSON with sealed secrets), id, label and signedOut (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:23-54, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:66-69, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:141-142) (iPhone: the servers list as JSON in UserDefaults "servers", with secrets in the Keychain)
- [x] The server in use is also written under the older single-server keys (server_url, username, password_sealed, server_type, server_version, is_octo, octo_admin_reachable, extensions, connection); with none in use, or the active one signed out, those keys are removed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:71-124, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:144-154) (iPhone: the first iPhone builds' single keys are moved into the list once)
- [x] Reading back, the older keys have the last word: a store from before the list becomes a list of one still in use; a server an older version signed in to since is added or updated and made the one in use (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:94-106, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:78-99) (iPhone: a single-key store becomes a list of one, in use)
- [x] Each kept server's id is fixed when first kept (the account key: first 8 bytes of SHA-256 of "username@address" in lowercase hex) and kept when the address is edited (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionStore.kt:39-51, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/livelists/LiveList.kt:94-100)
- [x] Per-account values in other stores are kept under "{name}@{server id}"; forgetting an account removes every key ending "@{server id}" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/AccountPrefs.kt:11-40)
- [x] The connection JSON holds homeUrl, authMode, pins (host to fingerprint), folder, and sealed headers and client certificate name; header values and the certificate name never reach disk in plain (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionSettings.kt:10-78) (iPhone: StoredServer fields plus Keychain items for header values and the certificate, per-host trust in ServerTrust, the folder in the account's prefs)
- [x] Passwords, keys, header lists and certificate names are sealed with AES-256-GCM (12-byte IV first, Base64 without wrapping) using a key that never leaves the phone's keystore (alias "octo.server.password") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/CredentialVault.kt:14-63) (iPhone: the Keychain, after first unlock, this device only)
- [x] At app start, when the server in use's secret, connection or address can no longer be opened (keystore key lost, as after restoring a backup on a new phone), it stays in the list signed out with no password and nothing is in use (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:513-528)
- [x] Custom headers, this install's id ("X-Octo-Device-Id") and the phone's name ("X-Octo-Device-Name", percent-encoded, at most 64 characters) go only to the server's own addresses (main and home, by scheme, host and port), never along a redirect to another host (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionSecurity.kt:21-94, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/ServerHeaders.kt:9-90, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/DeviceIds.kt:21-75)
- [x] The device id is made once (random UUID) and kept in its own "device" store, outside backups; the phone's name is its device name setting, else "{Maker} {model}" (model alone when it starts with the maker, "Android phone" when nothing is known) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/DeviceIds.kt:21-75)

## Where shares and radio stations are reached

### Settings, Server and sync page, "On the server" group

- [x] The "On the server" group (icon Cloud) is drawn only while a server is signed in (SessionState.SignedIn); it sits after the "Sync" group and before the "Sign out" group on the "Server and sync" page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:194, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:263)
- [x] Group order, top to bottom: "Shared links" (only while sharing is available), "Radio stations" (always), then the scan row (admins only) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:103-107)
- [x] "Shared links" is an action row with a trailing chevron, title "Shared links", no detail line on the row; tapping it opens the Shared links screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:104, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:285-312)
- [x] "Radio stations" is an action row with a trailing chevron, title "Radio stations", no detail line on the row; it is shown for every signed-in server (no capability check); tapping it opens the Radio stations screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:105)
- [x] The "Shared links" row shows only while `sharing` is true: a server is signed in and that kept server has not refused sharing during this app run (starts true right after sign-in) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:93, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:100-102)
- [x] Opening the Server and sync page probes sharing once: its view model calls getShares on creation and swallows any SubsonicException; a "sharing off" answer is remembered by the controls, so the "Shared links" row disappears after the probe (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerExtras.kt:51-61)
- [x] Settings search entry for the shares row: id "shares", title "Shared links", summary "Links to songs and albums you shared", keywords "share" and "links", section "On the server", page "Server and sync" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:328-331)
- [x] Settings search entry for the radio row: id "radio_stations", title "Radio stations", summary "The server's internet radio stations", keywords "radio", "internet radio", "streams", section "On the server", page "Server and sync" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:332-335)
- [x] Both screens are their own navigation routes (SharesRoute, RadioStationsRoute), pushed in the current tab's stack; back pops them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/Routes.kt:89-91, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:408-409)
- [x] Both screens belong to the Settings ambient area, so they get the Settings page glow (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientSource.kt:59-61)

### Share entry points that open the share sheet

- [x] Song menu: action "Share link" (icon Share, chevron because it opens a page) is offered only for a library song (never a song found online) when sharing is available and the song has a copy on the signed-in server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:102-103, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:168, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:268, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenu.kt:321)
- [x] The song's server id for sharing is the smallest native id (string order) among the song's copies whose source is the signed-in server; none means no "Share link" action (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:140-143)
- [x] In the song menu the "Share link" action is listed in the fourth group, first in that group: Share link, Share file, Set as ringtone, Song info, Find songs, Select (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenu.kt:185, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenu.kt:208)
- [x] "Share link" (server link) is a separate action from "Share file" (sends the file on the phone); this part covers only "Share link" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:264-268)
- [x] Tapping "Share link" turns the song menu card to its Share page (same card, page with a back chevron), with request ids = [the song's server id] and title = the song's title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:114-117) (iPhone: "Share link" is a submenu of the song menu holding the expiry choices; the id is the lowest server copy, the title the song's)
- [x] The song menu is the same card everywhere it opens (song rows, the full player), so "Share link" is reachable from any song menu (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:66-98)
- [x] Album page: a quiet action "Share" (icon Share, text "Share", label style, TextSecondary tint, no border or fill) sits first in the quiet actions row under the album header, before the album download action (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumScreen.kt:260-264, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumShare.kt:28-35) (iPhone: a quiet "Share" menu, first in the quiet actions row before the download action)
- [x] The album "Share" action shows only when sharing is available and the library album maps to a server album; it is hidden while that lookup runs and when it finds none (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumShare.kt:30-33)
- [x] The server album for a library album is the server album id that most of its songs' copies on the signed-in server belong to (majority count; on a tie the first one counted wins) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:158-167)
- [x] A copy's album row counts only when it is "<sourceId>:<albumId>" for the signed-in source and the album id does not start with "album:" (albums the app made up from loose songs) and is not empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:55-59)
- [x] Tapping the album "Share" opens the floating share card with request ids = [server album id] and title = the album's title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumShare.kt:34) (iPhone: the album's Share menu lists the expiry choices; the id is the server album's, the title the album's)
- [x] The album share action is not on the online album page (only the library album page uses it) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumScreen.kt:262)
- [x] There is no share entry for playlists, artists or multi-select; only songs (song menu) and library albums (album page) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:115, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/album/AlbumShare.kt:34)
- [x] When sharing is turned off for the server during the run (a refusal was remembered), every share entry point disappears: song menu "Share link", album "Share", and the Settings "Shared links" row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:96-102, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:187-192)

## Share card (make a shared link)

### Host and state

- [x] The share card host is a single app-wide state object (open request, last request, showing counter) provided to every page; the card is drawn over everything in the main shell, above the song menu, playlist sheets, disconnect and choice sheets, and below the feedback (undo) host (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:59-82, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:235, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:256, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:342-350) (iPhone: the expiry choices are a system submenu in the song menu and the album's Share menu, in place of a floating card)
- [x] A request carries the server ids to share (songs, or exactly one album id) and a title used for the card heading and the link's description (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:53-55)
- [x] Showing a request sets it as open and as last, and increments the showing counter so each showing starts fresh (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:71-75) (iPhone: the menu is built fresh each time it opens)
- [x] Closing clears only the open request; the last request is kept so the card can fade away with its content (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:63-65, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:77-79) (iPhone: the system menu closes itself)
- [x] The card is a glass pop-up (accessibility pane title "Share") that opens beside the control pressed in the last 2 seconds (the album "Share" action), or centred on screen when nothing was pressed recently (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:128-137, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/PressSpot.kt:46-51, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/PressSpot.kt:63-70) (iPhone: the system menu opens from the control tapped)
- [x] The card frosts the page behind it, closes on a tap outside the card or on system back, grows in from the corner nearest the pressed control, is at most 340dp wide and at most 60% of the screen tall (content scrolls inside) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:117-121, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:143-255) (iPhone: a system menu; a tap outside closes it)
- [x] The card content is the share page with no back chevron (heading form), keyed by the showing counter (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:138-139) (iPhone: menu content, with no back chevron of its own)

### Share page layout

- [x] Each time the share page appears it resets: nothing being made and no problem shown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:149-150, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:92-95) (iPhone: the menu holds no state, so each opening starts clean)
- [x] The page is a glass menu page 288dp wide with 6dp padding; the header stays put and the body scrolls when taller than the card allows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:151-155, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:70, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:162-172) (iPhone: the system menu's layout)
- [x] Header when opened from the album page (no way back): heading text "Share \"<title>\"" (the request title in straight double quotes), label style, TextPrimary, at most 2 lines with ellipsis, marked as a heading for accessibility (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:153, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:310-319)
- [x] Header when opened from the song menu: a back row with a white back chevron and the title "Share link" (label style, 1 line, heading semantics, click label "Back"), then a hairline; tapping it returns to the song's actions page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:153, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:280-306, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:104) (iPhone: the submenu's header reads "Share link" and goes back to the song menu)
- [x] In the song menu, system back on the Share page goes back to the actions page rather than closing the menu (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:97) (iPhone: the submenu's header goes back to the menu it came from)
- [x] First body line: note "Anyone with the link can listen." in caption style, TextMuted, padded 14dp horizontal and 8dp vertical (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:156, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:323-325)
- [x] Then a quiet menu title "Link expires" in label style, TextSecondary (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:157, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/GlassMenu.kt:89-96)
- [x] Then four expiry choices in this order, each a tappable line: "Never", "1 day", "1 week", "1 month" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:158-165, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:35-40)
- [x] No expiry is preselected; the choices are actions, not a selection (there is no default and no check mark) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:158-165)
- [x] Expiry line look: at least 46dp tall, rounded 14dp, 14dp horizontal padding, label in bodySmall; role Button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:171-191) (iPhone: menu buttons)
- [x] After the choices, a problem line (when there is one) in caption style and the Error colour; then a 4dp spacer at the bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:166-167) (iPhone: the menu closes on a pick, so a problem shows as a message)

### Making the link

- [x] Tapping an expiry line makes the link at once with that expiry; there is no separate confirm button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:159-164)
- [x] Only one link is made at a time: a tap while one is being made does nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:98-99)
- [x] While a link is being made, all four lines are disabled; the line being made keeps TextPrimary text and shows an 18dp accent spinner (stroke 2dp) at its end; the other three turn TextMuted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:159, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:183-189) (iPhone: while a link is made the four choices are disabled and the one being made shows an hourglass; the album's Share shows a spinner)
- [x] Starting a make clears any earlier problem line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:101) (iPhone: no line inside the menu; messages replace each other)
- [x] Expiry sent: "Never" sends no expiry; "1 day" = now + 86,400,000 ms; "1 week" = now + 7 days; "1 month" = now + 30 days, where now is the phone clock at the moment of the call, in milliseconds since 1970 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:32-44, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:174)
- [x] Server call: GET /rest/createShare with one "id" parameter per id (repeated name), "description" = the request title only when not blank, "expires" = the millisecond value only when there is an expiry, plus the usual sign-in, v, c and f=json parameters (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:171-176, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:72-81, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:753-755, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:794-804, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Models.kt:646-654)
- [x] The answer is read from "shares.share"; the first share is the new link; an answer with no share fails with server message "The server made no share" (shown as "The server said: The server made no share") (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:753-755, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:17)
- [x] With no server signed in, making fails with "The server said: No server signed in" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:172, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:17)
- [x] When the new share has a blank url, the problem line reads "The server made the link but did not say where it is." and the card stays open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:105)
- [x] When the server does not share (error code 50, error code 70, or an HTTP 501 answer), the problem line reads "Sharing is switched off on this server." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:109, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:46-53)
- [x] Any other failure shows the general request message on the problem line (for example "Couldn't reach the server. Check the address, and that you're on the right network." when unreachable, "The server said: <message>" for a server error) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:109, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:6-19)
- [x] A "server does not share" failure on create is remembered for that kept server for the rest of the app run, which hides every share entry point (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:173-175, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:187-192)
- [x] As built, in the song menu path a remembered "sharing off" refusal also removes the song's share id, so the Share page goes blank instead of showing the problem line; the album card keeps showing "Sharing is switched off on this server." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:103, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:114) (iPhone: the menu closes on a pick, so both ways show "Sharing is switched off on this server." as a message)
- [x] Whatever the outcome, the busy state ends when the call returns, re-enabling all four lines (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:110-112) (iPhone: the busy marks end with the making, success or not)
- [x] On success: the card closes first (song menu path: the whole song menu closes; album path: the share card closes), then the phone's system share sheet opens with the link (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:160-163, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:116)
- [x] Closing the card while a link is being made does not cancel the request; if it then succeeds, the system share sheet still opens (the view model outlives the card) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:84-115)

### System share sheet

- [x] The system share sheet shares plain text: the text is only the link URL; the subject is the request title (song title or album title, or the share's title when re-shared from the Shared links screen) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:117-123)
- [x] The chooser has no custom heading, and a failure to open it is ignored silently (no message) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ShareSheet.kt:124)

## Shared links screen

### Loading and states

- [x] Opening the screen loads the signed-in account's shares at once (GET /rest/getShares, answer key "shares", missing key means no shares) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:66-74, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:757-759)
- [x] With no server signed in, the list is empty (no call made) and the empty state shows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:178-181)
- [x] The screen title is "Shared links" in the large display style, padded 20dp sides, 8dp top, 16dp bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:115, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:31-39)
- [x] The list starts below the status bar plus 8dp plus a 56dp gap that clears the floating back button, and ends 120dp above the navigation bar inset (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:114, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:25-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:52)
- [x] A round glazed back button (40dp, content description "Back") floats at the top left, 16dp from the start and 8dp below the status bar; it pops the screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:140, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:55-66)
- [x] Loading state: a 22dp accent spinner (stroke 2dp), centred horizontally, 24dp below the title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:118, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerParts.kt:24-29)
- [x] Failed state: the message (bodySmall, TextMuted) with 20dp side padding, then 12dp below it a glaze button "Try again" that loads the list again (shows the spinner again) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:119-124)
- [x] Failed message when the server does not share (code 50, code 70, or HTTP 501): "Sharing is switched off on this server."; otherwise the general request message (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:78)
- [x] A "server does not share" failure while listing is remembered for that server for the run, which hides the Settings "Shared links" row and every share entry point (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:178-181, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:187-192)
- [x] Empty state: the note "No shared links yet. Share a song or an album from its menu." in bodySmall, TextMuted, padded 20dp sides and 4dp top and bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:126-128, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerParts.kt:17-21)
- [x] There is no pull to refresh and no automatic refresh; the list is read once when the screen opens (and again only from "Try again") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:66-81)
- [x] List order is the server's order exactly as answered; no sorting (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:129)
- [x] Order of items on the screen: title, then the delete problem note (when there is one), then the loading, failed, empty or list content (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:115-138)

### Share row

- [x] Row: full width, at least 60dp tall, padded 20dp start, 8dp end, 6dp top and bottom, content vertically centred (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:162-171)
- [x] Row line 1: the share's title in bodySmall, TextPrimary, one line with ellipsis (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:173)
- [x] Share title rule: the share's description when not blank; otherwise the first song's title when not blank; otherwise "Shared link" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:18-20)
- [x] Row line 2: the details line in caption style, TextMuted, one line with ellipsis (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:174)
- [x] Details line parts, in order, joined by " • " (space, bullet U+2022, space): song count, expiry words, visit count (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:22-35)
- [x] Song count part: left out when the share lists no songs; "1 song" for one; "<n> songs" otherwise (no thousands separator) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:24-28)
- [x] Visit count part: "No visits" for 0; "1 visit" for 1; otherwise "<n> visits" with thousands separators in the phone's locale (for example "1,234 visits") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:30-34)
- [x] The share's expiry text is read as an ISO instant (for example "2026-09-29T10:00:00Z") or an ISO date time with offset; unreadable or missing counts as never expiring (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:29, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerQueues.kt:63-68)
- [x] Expiry words: no expiry, or an expiry at or before 0 ms: "Never expires" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:39)
- [x] Expiry words: expiry at or before now: "Expired" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:42)
- [x] Expiry words: less than 1 hour left: "Expires within the hour" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:43)
- [x] Expiry words: 1 hour to under 24 hours left: whole hours rounded down; "Expires in 1 hour" for 1, "Expires in <n> hours" otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:44)
- [x] Expiry words: 24 hours or more left: days rounded to the nearest whole day (half a day rounds up); "Expires in 1 day" for 1, "Expires in <n> days" otherwise (for example 7 days minus 1 hour reads "Expires in 7 days") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:45, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormatTest.kt:24-33)
- [x] "Now" for the expiry words is the phone clock at the time the screen draws; the words do not tick on their own (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:112)
- [x] The share's created date, last visited date and owner username are not shown anywhere (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:172-175, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Models.kt:575-585)
- [x] Example: a share with no description, songs "Nightcall" and "Odd Look", 3 days left and 12 visits reads "Nightcall" over "2 songs • Expires in 3 days • 12 visits" (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormatTest.kt:35-48)
- [x] Example: a share with nothing but an id reads "Shared link" over "Never expires • No visits" (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormatTest.kt:47-48)
- [x] Tapping the row (role Button) shares that link again through the system share sheet (text = the share's url, subject = the row's title); no server call (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:133, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:167)
- [x] A row whose url is blank is not tappable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:167)
- [x] Trailing delete icon button (icon Delete, 20dp, TextMuted tint, standard 48dp touch target) with content description "Delete link"; tapping it asks for confirmation (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:176-178, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:134) (iPhone: SF Symbol trash in a 48 point target, labeled "Delete link")

### Delete confirmation

- [x] The delete confirmation is a glass pop-up (accessibility pane title "Delete link") that opens beside the delete button just pressed, frosting the page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:141-148) (iPhone: a confirmation dialog from the delete button)

- [x] The card keeps showing the same share's words while it fades out after an answer (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/SharesScreen.kt:149-150, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/PopupForms.kt:36-43)
