## Radio stations screen

### Loading and states

- [x] Opening the screen loads the server's stations at once: GET /rest/getInternetRadioStations, answer key "internetRadioStations.internetRadioStation", missing key means none (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:774-783, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:111-126)
- [x] The station list call allows up to 3 minutes to read the answer (Octo builds its stations the first time they are asked for after it starts) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:66-68, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:782)
- [x] Each station carries id, name (default ""), streamUrl (default ""), homePageUrl (optional) and coverArt (optional) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Models.kt:618-627)
- [x] With no server signed in, the list is empty (no call) and the empty state shows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:239-240)
- [x] On an Octo server, every station whose coverArt equals its own id is treated as made by the server and marked read-only for this account, for the rest of the app run (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:74-77, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:241-244)
- [x] Read-only marks are kept per kept server for the app run; switching servers and back finds them still marked; a reload never clears them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:108-116)
- [x] The screen title is "Radio stations" in the large display style (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:217)
- [x] Same page padding (status bar + 8dp + 56dp top gap, 120dp above the navigation bar) and the same floating round "Back" button as the Shared links screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:216, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:246)
- [x] Admins only: a glaze button "Add station" under the title (20dp from the start, 8dp below it), shown in every state (loading, failed, empty, list); it opens the station form empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:218-222, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:128-131)
- [x] Admin rights: on each server sign-in the app asks GET /rest/getUser with "username" = the signed-in username and uses its adminRole (default false); with a blank username (API key sign-in) or any error it counts as not admin; it reads false until the answer arrives (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:118-135, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:254-255, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Models.kt:35-38)
- [x] Loading state: the same 22dp accent spinner 24dp below the title (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:224)
- [x] Failed state: the general request message (bodySmall, TextMuted, 20dp sides) and 12dp below it a glaze button "Try again" that loads again; there is no special "not supported" wording for radio (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:122-124, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:225-230)
- [x] Empty state: the note "No stations on this server yet." (bodySmall, TextMuted, 20dp sides, 4dp top and bottom) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:232)
- [x] List order is the server's order exactly as answered; no sorting (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:233)
- [x] There is no pull to refresh; the list is read on open, after every successful save or delete, after "Try again", and when starting a server-made station finds it gone (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:111-126, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:163, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:197-199)

### Station row

- [x] Row: full width, at least 60dp tall, padded 20dp start, 8dp end, 6dp top and bottom, 14dp between parts, vertically centred; role Button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:263-275)
- [x] Leading Radio icon, 22dp, TextSecondary tint, no content description (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:276) (iPhone: SF Symbol dot.radiowaves.left.and.right, 22 points, hidden from VoiceOver)
- [x] Line 1: the station name in bodySmall, TextPrimary, one line with ellipsis (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:278)
- [x] Line 2 (caption, TextMuted, one line with ellipsis): "Made by the server" for a read-only station; otherwise the host of the stream address (for example "stream.example"); when the address cannot be read as an http or https URL, the raw stream address as typed (empty when there is none) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:279-280)
- [x] Admins see a trailing edit icon button (icon Rename, 20dp, TextMuted, content description "Edit <station name>") on every station that is not read-only; non-admins never see it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:240, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:282-286) (iPhone: SF Symbol pencil, labeled "Edit <name>")
- [x] Tapping the edit icon opens the station form filled with the station's id, name, stream address and home page (empty when none), clearing any earlier problem (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:133-136)
- [x] Tapping a row plays the station; it is tappable for everyone, admin or not (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:239, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:270)
- [x] While a server-made station's songs are being fetched, that row fades to 50% opacity (animated) and comes back when done; there is no spinner (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:265, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:269)

### Playing a station

- [x] While any station is starting, taps on any station are ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:181)
- [x] A station that is not read-only plays its stream at once: the queue is replaced by one radio item made from the stream address and name; a station with a blank stream address does nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:182-185)
- [x] A read-only (server-made) station asks the server for its lined-up songs: GET /rest/getPlaylist with "id" = the station id, resolved to the app's songs (library songs or online finds) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:186-189, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/discovery/Discovery.kt:164-168, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:413-414)
- [x] When that answer has songs, the queue is replaced by those songs in order, starting at the first, shuffle off, playing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:190-191, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:140-152)
- [x] When that answer has no songs (or no server is signed in), it falls back to the station's stream when its address is not blank, else nothing plays (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:192-194, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/discovery/Discovery.kt:166)
- [x] When the server answers "not found" (error 70), the station list is read again (Octo remade it) and nothing plays (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:197-199)
- [x] Any other failure while starting is only logged ("station failed to start: <error kind>"); no message is shown and nothing plays (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:200-202)
- [x] As built, a station marked read-only after a refused edit (not made by Octo) also goes the playlist route when tapped, so on a non-Octo server it usually plays nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:182-202, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:259-266)
- [x] Playing replaces the whole queue: shuffle mode off, start at index 0 at position 0, prepare and play; the queue's source name is empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:142-152)

### Station form (add or edit)

- [x] The form is a glass pop-up centred on screen (not beside the button, since it holds a form), accessibility pane title "Station", at most 400dp wide and 60% of the screen tall; it rises above the keyboard while typing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:247-256, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:196-199) (iPhone: a sheet sized to the form, which the system keeps above the keyboard)

- [x] Field 2: single-line glass text field with placeholder "Stream address", URL keyboard, keyboard action Next (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:308-313)

- [x] Glass text field look: 48dp tall, rounded 12dp, sunk with inner shadows, accent fill at 10% rising to 15% and a 1.5dp accent ring fading in while focused, body text, accent cursor, placeholder in TextMuted (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassInput.kt:34-112)
- [x] Validation, Save enabled only when: the name is not blank, the stream address (trimmed) is a valid http or https URL with a host, and the home page is blank or (trimmed) a valid http or https URL with a host (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:73-82)
- [x] Validation examples: "https://stream.example/fip.mp3" and " http://192.168.50.21:8000/live " are valid stream addresses; "stream.example/fip.mp3" (no scheme) and "" are not; a home page "not a page" blocks Save; a name of only spaces blocks Save (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormatTest.kt:60-69)
- [x] No error words are shown for an invalid field; Save simply stays disabled (dimmed) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:329)
- [x] Under the fields, a problem line (caption, Error colour) when the last save or delete failed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:320)
- [x] Buttons row (12dp apart): accent "Save" (disabled unless valid, shows a spinner in place of its words while saving); for an existing station a glaze "Delete" beside it, disabled while saving (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:327-331, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/Buttons.kt:140-160)
- [x] Add station: Save calls GET /rest/createInternetRadioStation with "streamUrl" (trimmed), "name" (trimmed), and "homepageUrl" (trimmed) only when not empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:146-148, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:785-786, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Models.kt:656-662)
- [x] Edit station: Save calls GET /rest/updateInternetRadioStation with "id" first, then "streamUrl", "name" (both trimmed), and "homepageUrl" only when not empty, so clearing the home page sends no home page at all (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:147, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:788-789, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Models.kt:656-662)
- [x] Tapping glaze "Delete" does not delete; it swaps the buttons row for an inline confirmation inside the same form (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:330, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:321-326) (iPhone: a confirmation dialog in place of the swap inside the form)
- [x] Inline confirmation text: "Delete \"<name as currently typed in the Name field>\" from the server?" in bodySmall, TextSecondary (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:322)
- [x] Inline confirmation buttons (12dp apart): accent "Delete" (spinner while working) that deletes, and glaze "Cancel" that returns to the Save row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:323-326) (iPhone: a destructive Delete plus the system Cancel; the spinner shows on Save)
- [x] Confirmed delete calls GET /rest/deleteInternetRadioStation with "id" = the station id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:150, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:791)
- [x] The inline confirmation starts closed every time the form is opened anew (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:293)
- [x] Only one save or delete runs at a time; a second tap while one runs is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:154-158)
- [x] Starting a save or delete clears the problem line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:158)
- [x] On success the form closes and the station list is read again (spinner, then the new list) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:161-163)
- [x] With no server signed in, add, update and delete make no call and count as success (the form closes and the list reloads empty) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:247-255)
- [x] When an update or delete is refused with "not found" (error 70, Octo's answer for its read-only stations) or error 50 (not authorized), that station is marked read-only for the account for the run (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:257-266)
- [x] A failed save or delete of an existing station that is (now) read-only shows "The server keeps this station as it is." in the form; any other failure shows the general request message; the form stays open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:166-171)
- [x] After a station is marked read-only, its row loses the edit icon and its second line reads "Made by the server" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:234, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:240, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:279)
- [x] An add (create) failure never marks anything read-only (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/ServerControls.kt:247-249)
- [x] Tap outside the card or system back closes the form, except while a save or delete is running (then it stays open) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:142-144, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:252)
- [x] Typing does not clear the problem line; opening Add or an edit does (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/RadioStationsScreen.kt:128-140)

### A radio stream in the queue

- [x] A stream's queue id is "radio:" + URL-encoded trimmed stream address + ":" + URL-encoded trimmed name (UTF-8 form encoding, so ":" becomes "%3A" and spaces become "+"); the name may be empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItems.kt:8-21)
- [x] Reading a radio id back: split after "radio:" on ":", decode both parts; an id whose address part is blank or undecodable is not playable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItems.kt:23-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItems.kt:51)
- [x] The player opens the stream address as is; the now-playing title is the station name, or the stream address when the name is empty; the artist line is "Radio"; it is a playable, non-browsable radio-station media item (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItems.kt:31-47, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:62-63) (iPhone: the lock screen marks it as a live stream)
- [x] A live stream has no length: it never crossfades and never counts as a play (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItems.kt:14-15)
- [x] A radio item is never scrobbled to ListenBrainz (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:202-207)
- [x] A radio item has no server id, so it is left out of the play queue saved to the server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/server/QueueSync.kt:278-281)
- [x] Autoplay never starts after a radio item ends (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Autoplay.kt:91)
- [x] "Save queue as playlist" leaves radio items out (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerViewModel.kt:174-175)
- [x] Lyrics prefetch skips radio items (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerViewModel.kt:276)
- [x] The media notification shows no heart (no like or download) for a radio item (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeart.kt:15-16) (iPhone: no like button on the lock screen or in CarPlay for a station)
- [~] Casting a radio item sends its http or https stream address straight to the device as a live web stream; any other scheme (for example rtsp) is skipped as unservable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/output/CastSources.kt:40-42, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/output/CastSourcesTest.kt:26-36) (not on iPhone: casting was dropped; AirPlay plays the stream)

## Server format helpers also used by the Server page

- [x] Scan row words: idle "Scan library now"; scanning with a count above 0 "Scanning, <count> songs" with thousands separators (for example "Scanning, 1,234 songs"); scanning with 0 "Scanning"; done "Scan finished"; failed shows the failure message (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:10-16, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormatTest.kt:15-22)
- [x] Listening now times, from the server's minutes: 0 or less "Just now"; 1 "1 minute ago"; 2 to 59 "<n> minutes ago"; 60 to 119 "1 hour ago"; 120 and up "<minutes / 60, rounded down> hours ago" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormat.kt:49-56, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/server/ServerFormatTest.kt:51-58)

## Settings > Streaming and downloads

### How the page is reached, and its frame

- [x] The Settings front page lists this page in the "Your music" group as a category row with the `OctoIcons.Download` icon, the title "Streaming and downloads", and a one-line summary under it; tapping it opens this page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:314, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:317, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:343) (iPhone: SF Symbol arrow.down.circle)
- [x] The front page summary is built from up to 3 parts joined by " · ": "Wi-Fi <Wi-Fi size>", "mobile <mobile size>", then "no cache" when Cache size is Off or "<N> GB cache" otherwise, for example "Wi-Fi Original · mobile 128 kbps · 2 GB cache" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:19, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:24-27, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:93-97)
- [x] A stream size reads "Original" for Original and "<kbps> kbps" otherwise ("320 kbps", "256 kbps", "192 kbps", "128 kbps") wherever settings show it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:31-32)
- [x] A cache size reads "Off" for Off and "<bytes shifted right by 30> GB" otherwise ("1 GB", "2 GB", "5 GB", "10 GB") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:34-35)
- [x] The page title is "Streaming and downloads" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:18, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:134)
- [x] The page frame is the back button, the large screen title, then the groups stacked with 24 dp between them; the whole page scrolls vertically (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:25-52)
- [x] Opened from a settings search result, the pointed-at row waits 350 ms, is scrolled into view, then glows with the accent colour at 14% alpha (fade in 250 ms, hold 900 ms, fade out 700 ms); it lights once per visit and not again when coming back to the page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:34-36, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:62-70, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:146-177)
- [x] Group titles show in upper case, muted, label style with 0.6 sp letter spacing, led by the group icon at 15 dp; the title row is an accessibility heading whose content description is the title in its original case (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:88-104)
- [x] A group's rows sit in one raised panel with 20 dp corners, a one physical pixel hairline (text colour at 10% alpha) between rows inset 16 dp; a row that draws nothing takes no space and gets no line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:60, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:72-141)
- [x] Rows show only their title (and a helper line where the page gives one); the summary lines in SettingsIndex are for search results and are never drawn under a row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:180-227, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:136-197)
- [x] A choice row shows its title on the left, the current answer on the right (secondary colour, right aligned, at most 2 lines with ellipsis, at most 0.8 of the spare width) and an 18 dp chevron; tapping it opens the choice popup beside the row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:204-244) (iPhone: the row opens a system menu headed by the question, the current answer checked; an up and down chevron)
- [x] Before the stored values have loaded the rows show the defaults (`StreamPrefs()` and `OfflinePrefs()`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:43-44, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:68-69) (iPhone: the stored values are read at start, before any page shows, so the defaults never show first)
- [x] Group order on the page: "Streaming", "Cache", "Downloads" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:135, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:153, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:172)
- [x] Picking an answer in any choice popup on this page saves it at once and closes the popup; there is no confirmation and no message (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:160-176, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:46-60, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:78-88)
- [x] This page has no conditions on the server: every row shows the same with an Octo server, a plain Subsonic server, or no server at all (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:119-200)

### Streaming group

- [x] Group title "Streaming" with the `OctoIcons.Wifi` icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:135)
- [x] Row 1 is a choice row titled "When a song is on the phone and the server", showing "Phone copy first" or "Best quality" on the right (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:136, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:350-353)
- [x] Its popup is titled "When a song is on the phone and the server" and lists, in order: "Phone copy first" with the line "Plays the phone's file, and streams only songs the phone lacks." (stored `PhoneFirst`), then "Best quality" with the line "Plays whichever copy sounds better, even when that means streaming." (stored `BestQuality`); the current one is marked selected (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:95-98, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:137-142, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:7-8)
- [x] Default "Phone copy first"; stored in the "player" preferences as the string enum name under key "copies"; a missing or unknown value reads as the default (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:107-113, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:122, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:142, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:165-178, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:404-406)
- [x] Copies that can play now: a server copy only when the server is reachable, a phone copy only while its file is still there; when none can play, all copies are the pool so one is still handed over, fails and is skipped like any song that cannot play (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:13-26)
- [x] With "Phone copy first" the first phone copy in the pool plays; with no phone copy in the pool the best copy plays (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:27-31)
- [x] With "Best quality" the best copy in the pool plays; phone copies are ordered first and a later copy wins only when strictly better, so a tie goes to the phone (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:23, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:27-30)
- [x] "Better" is decided in this order: lossless beats lossy (mime types audio/flac, audio/x-flac, audio/alac, audio/wav, audio/x-wav, audio/wave, audio/vnd.wave, audio/aiff, audio/x-aiff, audio/ape, audio/x-ape, audio/wavpack, audio/x-wavpack, compared lower case), then the higher sample rate, then the deeper bit depth, then the higher bitrate; a fact unknown (null or 0) on either side is skipped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:69-87)
- [x] A copy's bitrate is its listed bitrate (a value under 10000 is read as kbps and multiplied by 1000), else file size x 8 / length in seconds, else unknown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:89-96)
- [x] The server counts as reachable only when some song in the list has a server copy, the phone is online, and a server is signed in (waiting up to 3 s for the saved sign-in at app start) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:82-84, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:51-52, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:82-86) (iPhone: the saved sign-in is restored during start, so no 3 s wait is needed)
- [x] When the server is reachable, the phone copies of songs that also have a server copy are checked against the phone's media store in one query; a file that is gone counts as unable to play, so the stream is used (without music access, every such phone file counts as gone) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:85-86, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:108-130)
- [x] The copy is chosen when songs are turned into queue items (played or queued); a change never switches the song already playing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:36-40, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:76-106)
- [x] Row 2 is an action row titled "Audio quality" with the helper line "On Wi-Fi <Wi-Fi size>, on mobile data <mobile size>" (for example "On Wi-Fi Original, on mobile data 128 kbps") and a chevron; tapping it opens the Audio quality page; it has no search entry and is never highlighted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:144-146)
- [x] That helper always shows this app's own Wi-Fi and mobile data choices, even when a family account's own choice is what applies (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:146)
- [x] Row 3 is a choice row titled "Start playing after" showing the current answer, with the helper line under its title: "Waits for this much audio before a song starts. Shorter starts faster on a slow connection; longer stutters less." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:147-148, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/StartAfter.kt:13-14)
- [x] Its popup is titled "Start playing after" and lists, with no detail lines: "As soon as possible" (`Instant`, 250 ms), "1 second" (`Short`, 1000 ms), "2.5 seconds" (`Steady`, 2500 ms), "5 seconds" (`Safe`, 5000 ms), "10 seconds" (`Long`, 10000 ms) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/StartAfter.kt:3-11, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:147-149)
- [x] Default "1 second" (`Short`); stored under key "start_after" as the enum name in the "player" preferences (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:115-116, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:152, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:176, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:416-418)
- [x] Effect: a stream starts playing once the buffered audio, counted in playing time (buffered divided by the playback speed when the speed is not 1), reaches the chosen amount (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoLoadControl.kt:11-22, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoLoadControl.kt:38-45)
- [x] After running dry mid-song it waits for the larger of the chosen amount and 2000 ms (the player's own after-rebuffer wait) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoLoadControl.kt:43, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoLoadControlTest.kt:25-32)
- [x] Files on the phone (local schemes such as file and content, matched in any case) keep the player's default start rule and ignore this setting (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoLoadControl.kt:20, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoLoadControl.kt:24-36) (iPhone: phone files (file, Music library, data) start on a fixed 250 ms and ignore the setting)
- [x] A change applies at once to every deck, with no restart (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:232-234)

### Cache group

- [x] Group title "Cache" with the `OctoIcons.Storage` icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:153)
- [x] Row 1 is a choice row titled "Cache size"; its value reads "Off" when off, otherwise "<size>, <used> used", for example "2 GB, 1.3 GB used" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:154-157)
- [x] The used amount is written as "%.1f GB" from 1 GiB up, "%.1f MB" from 1 MiB up (1024-based units, dot decimal), else "<bytes rounded up to whole KiB> KB" ("0 KB" when empty) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/offline/DownloadsScreen.kt:111-116)
- [x] The used amount is measured again in the background each time the page opens, and updates after each fetch ahead, each cache size change and each clear; it does not count up while a song streams with the page open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:71-76, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:43-45, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:63, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:81-88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:156-159)
- [x] The "Cache size" popup is titled "Cache size" and lists: "Off" with the line "Songs stream every time, and nothing is fetched ahead", then "1 GB", "2 GB", "5 GB", "10 GB" with no lines (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:100-101, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:158)
- [x] Stored values: `Off` (0 bytes), `Gb1` (1 GiB, 1 shl 30), `Gb2` (2 GiB), `Gb5` (5 GiB), `Gb10` (10 GiB); default `Gb2`; key "cache_size" (enum name) in the "offline" preferences (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:18-26, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:31-34, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:48-49, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:66, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:77-79)
- [x] With Cache size Off, the rows "Fetch ahead on Wi-Fi", "Fetch ahead on mobile data" and "Clear cache" are hidden; their stored counts are kept (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:161-169)
- [x] Row 2 is a choice row titled "Fetch ahead on Wi-Fi" whose value reads "Off" for 0, "1 song" for 1, "<n> songs" otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:106-110, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:162-164)
- [x] Row 3 is a choice row titled "Fetch ahead on mobile data" with the same value wording (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:165-167)
- [x] Each fetch ahead popup is titled with its row's title and lists 0, 1, 2, 3, 5, 10 as "Off", "1 song", "2 songs", "3 songs", "5 songs", "10 songs"; only "Off" has a line: "Only the playing song is saved" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:103-104, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:202-209)
- [x] A stored count not in that list (any of 4, 6, 7, 8, 9, for example from a backup) is added to the popup in sorted order so it shows selected (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:203)
- [x] Defaults: 3 songs on Wi-Fi, 1 song on mobile data; keys "prefetch_wifi" and "prefetch_mobile" (ints); every read and write is clamped to 0..10 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:28-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:35-37, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:50-51, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:67-68, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:81-87)
- [x] Row 4 is an action row titled "Clear cache" with no chevron and no helper; it is disabled (muted title, not tappable) while the used amount is 0 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:168, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:282-312)
- [x] Tapping "Clear cache" removes every saved song at once with no confirmation and no message, then the used amount is measured again (the row then disables) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:90-92, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:81-92)

### Cache rules (what Cache size drives)

- [x] The cache keeps server songs played lately on the phone so they play again without a connection, letting the least recently played go once full; downloads are real files and never live in it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:27-29)
- [x] The cache sits in the app's cache folder under "media" and is opened on first use (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:47-51)
- [x] Until the saved size has been read, songs are neither read from nor written to the cache, and nothing is evicted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:39-41, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:102-103)
- [x] Switching to Off stops caching and deletes everything saved at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:55-59)
- [x] Switching to a size applies it at once: the least recently played pieces are let go until the cache fits; afterwards the least recently played pieces go whenever it is over the size (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:60-62, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:95-112) (iPhone: each song is saved as one file, so it is let go whole, least recently played first)
- [x] A song is saved under "<server id>|<song id on the server>|<mime type or unknown>|<max kbps or raw>", never under its signed address, so each stream size of a song is its own saved copy (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/StreamRef.kt:63-67, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:215-218)
- [x] While the cache is on, server songs play through it: what is saved is read from the phone and the rest streams from the server and is saved as it plays; while Off, server songs stream straight from the server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:263-289)
- [x] A saved copy that cannot be read is passed over for the server's (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:206-213)
- [x] Which size loads: the size this connection asks for when it is saved whole; else any other size saved whole (no data used, works offline); else the wanted size when it is partly saved; else another size that is partly saved (so a seek after moving between Wi-Fi and mobile data reads the same file); else the wanted size (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/StreamRef.kt:76-97)
- [x] Other sizes are tried in this order: the file as it is first, then smaller MP3 sizes largest first (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/StreamRef.kt:69-74)
- [x] With the cache Off nothing counts as saved, so every load and seek asks for the size the connection wants right then (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/StreamCache.kt:68-79) (iPhone: with the cache off each load asks for the size the connection wants then; a seek reads on in the file the song opened with)

### Fetch ahead rules

- [x] While the phone itself plays (not while casting), the next server songs in the queue are saved into the cache ahead of time (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:31-35, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:182-183)
- [x] How many: 0 while the cache is off; the Wi-Fi count when on Wi-Fi or a cable; the mobile data count when online any other way; 0 when offline (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:114-119, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:88-103)
- [x] Fetching starts 5 seconds after the last change so the playing song gets the connection first (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:27-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:98-104)
- [x] It looks at the next <count> songs in play order (following shuffle; repeat one is treated as repeat off; it stops if it wraps back to the playing song); songs found online and anything that is not a server song are skipped but still use up a place in the count (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:121-139)
- [x] It starts over on a song change, a queue change, play or pause, a shuffle or repeat change, and any change to the offline settings; while paused nothing is fetched and a running fetch is stopped mid-song; the same list already being fetched is not restarted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:55-68, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:70-112)
- [x] Each song is fetched whole at the size it will play (the same size rule as playback); songs already saved whole are skipped; fetching stops at the first connection error (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/Prefetcher.kt:141-160)

### Downloads group

- [x] Group title "Downloads" with the `OctoIcons.Download` icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:172)
- [x] Row 1 is a choice row titled "Download quality" whose value reads "Original" or "<kbps> kbps MP3" (for example "320 kbps MP3") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:112, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:173)
- [x] Its popup is titled "Download quality" and lists, in order: "Original (as the file is, FLAC stays FLAC)" with no line; "320 kbps MP3" with "About 10 MB for a 4 minute song"; "256 kbps MP3" with "About 8 MB for a 4 minute song"; "192 kbps MP3" with "About 6 MB for a 4 minute song"; "128 kbps MP3" with "About 4 MB for a 4 minute song" (the MB figure is kbps x 0.03 rounded) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:114-115, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:174-178, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:26-29)
- [x] Stored values `Original`, `Kbps320`, `Kbps256`, `Kbps192`, `Kbps128`; default `Original`; key "download_quality" (enum name) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/StreamQuality.kt:3-11, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:38-39, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:52, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:69, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:89-91)
- [x] Effect: each download reads the setting as it starts and asks for the server's file as it is ("format=raw") for Original, or for a lossy file whose bitrate in kbps is at or under the chosen size; otherwise it asks for an MP3 with "format=mp3", "maxBitRate=<kbps>" and "estimateContentLength=true"; files already downloaded are not changed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:428-433, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:98-119)
- [x] Row 2 is a switch row titled "Download on Wi-Fi only" with no helper line; tapping anywhere on the row flips it (role switch) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:180-184, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:188-202)
- [x] Default on; key "wifi_only" (boolean) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:40, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:53, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:70, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:93-95)
- [x] With it on, downloads start only while a server is signed in and the phone is on Wi-Fi or a cable; with it off, whenever a server is signed in and the phone is online (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:416-419, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:92-100)
- [x] With it on, a running download checks every 500 ms and, once the phone has left Wi-Fi, stops and goes back to waiting (Queued) without counting as a failed try (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:487-493, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:455-460) (iPhone: the running download stops the moment the connection leaves Wi-Fi instead of a check every 500 ms; it goes back to Queued and the try is not counted)
- [x] Changing it wakes the download queue at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:157-162)
- [x] Row 3 is a switch row titled "Keep Liked songs downloaded" with the helper "Liked songs from your server, kept in step with your likes." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:185-190)
- [x] Default off; key "keep_liked" (boolean) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:43, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:55, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:72, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:101-103)
- [x] With it on, every liked song that is on a server and not on the phone is queued for download with the reason "liked"; a song liked later is downloaded; an unliked song's download and file are deleted unless it was asked for by hand or a kept playlist also keeps it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:306-337, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadPlan.kt:32-66, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadDao.kt:152-159)
- [x] Switching it off deletes the downloads (and files) kept only because they are liked; ones asked for by hand or kept by a playlist stay, minus the "liked" reason (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadPlan.kt:44-66)
- [x] Rule changes settle for 1.5 s before downloads follow them, are never applied while the library is being rebuilt, and are applied again after each sync of the server's library (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:65-66, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:283-288, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:316, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:324-326)
- [x] Rules only add, change or remove downloads of the server in use; another kept server's downloads stay as they are until it is in use again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:339-345)
- [x] A song a rule keeps is not deleted just because it is briefly missing from the library during a sync (a reason goes only when the rule no longer wants the song) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadPlan.kt:44-49)
- [x] Row 4 is a switch row titled "Prefer streaming on Wi-Fi" with the helper "Even songs that are downloaded." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:191-196)
- [x] Default off; key "stream_on_wifi" (boolean) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:41-42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:54, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:71, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:97-99)
- [x] Without it (or off Wi-Fi, or with the server unreachable), a downloaded song plays its file like a phone copy, ahead of any stream; with it on, while the phone is on Wi-Fi or a cable and the server is reachable, downloads are ignored and the song streams by the "When a song is on the phone and the server" rule; decided when the songs are queued (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:88-97, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:34-47)
- [x] Row 5 is an action row titled "Downloaded music" with a chevron and no helper; tapping it opens the Downloads screen (titled "Downloads") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:197, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/offline/DownloadsScreen.kt:136-151)

### Download rules (what the Downloads settings drive)

- [x] Why a download is kept is saved as reasons: "manual" (asked for by hand), "liked", "playlist:<playlist id>"; stored sorted and comma separated in one column; a song stays while any reason does (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadPlan.kt:3-19)
- [x] A song can be downloaded only when it is on a server and not on the phone (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadDao.kt:152-159, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:166-179)
- [x] Each new download comes from the best copy on the server by the quality order (lossless, sample rate, bit depth, bitrate) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:353-376)
- [x] For a family account whose plan has offline copies off on the server in use, new downloads from that server are refused and the message "Offline copies are off for this account" shows; files already on the phone stay; without a family, or when the plan cannot be read, downloads are allowed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:101-103, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:377-388, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:40, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:38-39)
- [x] Downloads run one at a time, oldest added first (ties by title), only for the server in use, and only while the app is alive (no foreground service); a download cut short when the app closed waits again next start (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:85-89, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:138-149, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:400-414, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadDao.kt:89-91, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadDao.kt:113-115) (iPhone: one at a time, oldest first then by title, server in use only; a system background transfer carries on while the app is away, and one cut short waits again at the next start)
- [x] The queue wakes when a network becomes available or changes, when "Download on Wi-Fi only" changes, and when the sign-in changes (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:150-163)
- [x] Progress is written every 500 ms and held at 99% at most until the file is saved (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:62-63, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:488-494)
- [x] A failed try: with no connection (or the phone offline) it goes back to waiting however long that takes; when the server answers with an error status it is marked failed at once; any other error waits 5 s and tries again, marked failed after 3 tries (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:68-71, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:450-468)
- [x] The file is written as "<name>.part" and renamed when finished; if the download was removed meanwhile the part file is deleted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:433-446)
- [x] File name: "<Artist> - <Title> [<8 hex>].<ext>"; the characters \ / : * ? " < > | and control characters become spaces, runs of spaces collapse, spaces and dots are trimmed; the readable part is at most 80 characters with trailing spaces, dots and dashes trimmed, "Song" when empty; the hex is the first 4 bytes of SHA-1 of "<server id>|<song id>"; the ending comes from the mime type (mp3, flac, m4a, aac, ogg, opus, wav, aiff, ape, wv, wma, webm, else "audio") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/DownloadFiles.kt:5-44)
- [x] Files go in the app's own folder on the phone's music drive under "Octo" (or inside the app when there is no such drive), with a ".nomedia" file so other apps and the phone's music library do not list them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:503-512) (iPhone: Application Support/Octo, left out of device backups; the Files app and the Music library never list it, so no .nomedia is needed)
- [x] A download reads from the cache when the song is saved there (without adding to the cache), otherwise from the server with every request marked as an offline copy (X-Octo-Purpose), so an Octo server does not count it as playing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:159-165, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:198-203)
- [x] A finished download whose file has gone is put back to waiting the next time its song is played (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineDownloads.kt:290-300)

### Backup of these settings

- [x] A backup keeps and restores every setting on this page: copies, start after, Wi-Fi and mobile data size (player preferences), cache size, both fetch ahead counts, download quality, Wi-Fi only, prefer streaming on Wi-Fi and keep Liked (offline preferences); restoring writes them all in one go with counts clamped to 0..10 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:92-130, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:360-401, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:120-133)

## Settings > Audio quality

### How the page is reached, and its frame

- [x] The Settings front page lists this page in the "Your music" group, right under Streaming and downloads, as a category row with the `OctoIcons.AudioQuality` icon, the title "Audio quality" and the fixed summary "At home, away, on Wi-Fi and on mobile data" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:314-318)
- [x] It is also opened from the "Audio quality" row on Streaming and downloads (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/StreamingPage.kt:146)
- [x] The page title is "Audio quality" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:19, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:87, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:7)
- [x] "Family on" means the signed-in server lists the extension "octoFamily" at version 1 ("octoFamily:1" in its extensions); a plain Subsonic or Navidrome server, an Octo server with Family off, or no server signed in all count as off; it is read from the current session when the page draws (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:59-61, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:41-42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:57-58, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:12-15)
- [x] With Family on, opening the page reads the account (getFamily, then this account's devices with getFamilyDevices) unless it was already read; without Family nothing is asked of the server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:60-62, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:83, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:174-185)
- [x] The account is also read at app start and whenever the account in use changes, so the page usually opens with it already known (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:126-138)
- [x] Without Family the page shows one group titled "Streaming" with the two rows "Streaming on Wi-Fi" and "Streaming on mobile data" and no note (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:116-128)
- [x] With Family on, the group order is "Your account, on every app", then "Quality on this device" (only once this phone's entry is known), then "This app's quality" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:88-128)
- [x] With Family on but the account not read yet (or unreadable), only "This app's quality" shows, with its note, and its popups apply no family limit (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:116-121)
- [x] There is no loading spinner, empty state or error text on this page; the family groups simply appear once the account is read (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:78-130)

### Your account, on every app (Family on only)

- [x] Group title "Your account, on every app" with the `OctoIcons.Listeners` icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:90)
- [x] The family's limits come first as note rows in orange (`OctoColors.SignalOrange`): "Your family plan limits listening to <home> kbps" when the home limit is above 0, and "Your family plan limits away listening to <away> kbps" when the away limit is above 0 and there is no home limit or the away limit is lower; no note rows when there are no limits (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:91, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:66-72)
- [x] Row "At home" is a choice row showing the account's home quality as "Original", "High", "Standard" or "Data saver" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:92, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:12-17)
- [x] The "At home" popup is titled "At home" and lists four choices in order: Original, High, Standard, Data saver (stored as `Original`, `High`, `Standard`, `DataSaver`), the account's current one selected (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:93-94, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:8, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:45-54)
- [x] With no limit, the Original choice reads "Original (as the file is, FLAC stays FLAC)" with no line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:137, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:29)

### How the chosen quality is applied to streams

- [x] When this app picks (no Family, or this phone set to "Let this app decide"): on mobile data the mobile data choice applies, otherwise (Wi-Fi, a cable, or no connection at all) the Wi-Fi choice; when it does not pick, every stream asks for the file as it is and the server applies the account's own quality (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:105-109, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:328-335)
- [x] Mobile data is any active connection with neither a Wi-Fi nor an Ethernet transport; online means the active connection has internet capability (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:88-103)
- [x] The request: Original asks "format=raw"; a lossy file whose bitrate in kbps is at or under the chosen size also asks "format=raw" (making it MP3 would only lose quality); otherwise "format=mp3", "maxBitRate=<kbps>", "estimateContentLength=true" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:98-119) (iPhone: the same file or MP3 rules; files the phone cannot open (Ogg, Opus, WMA, APE, WavPack, DSD) are asked for as MP3 at the cap, 320 kbps for Original)
- [x] The size is chosen each time a stream opens (load or seek), from the setting as it is then, so a change applies from the next song that loads; with the cache on, a seek keeps reading the size the song started in (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:76-78, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:167-174, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:230-249, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/StreamRef.kt:76-97)
- [x] The file type the quality badge reads is "audio/mpeg" when the stream will be made into an MP3, else the server file's type; it is worked out when the songs are queued (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:121-125, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:102, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:109-110)
- [x] A made-on-the-way MP3 seeks by its steady bitrate (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:142-143) (iPhone: Apple's file reader works out MP3 places from the bitrate when there is no seek table)
- [x] With no connection, no server signed in, or a song from another kept server, a server song fails at once and is skipped, with no retry wait (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:236-260)
- [~] Casting to a TV or speaker on the Wi-Fi sends the song's pinned request, or else the Wi-Fi choice (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:176-179) (not on iPhone: casting was dropped; AirPlay plays the phone's own stream)
- [x] With Family on and this phone's mode not yet read, streams ask for the file as it is (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:106-109, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:88)
- [x] The shared helper `streamParams` (Opus at the family bitrate) is not used by the Android stream path, which always asks for raw or MP3 as above (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:90-94, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Copies.kt:101-111)

### Home and away (which server address is used)

- [x] This app's Wi-Fi and mobile data choices follow the connection type only, never the home or away place (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:105-109)
- [x] The place starts as Away; it is Home only when the server has a home address different from its main one, the phone is on Wi-Fi or a cable, and the home address answered just now (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:43-44, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:88-94, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:105-117, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/HomeAddress.kt:13-17)
- [x] It checks again when the server is set, when the phone moves to a new network or between local and not local (signal changes do not count), when the network is lost, and after a request fails to connect (at most once every 5 s); one check runs at a time and asks meanwhile fold into one more (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:20-22, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:46-79, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:101-103)
- [x] Calls go to the home address only while the place is Home; a change applies to the next call (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:24-27, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ConnectionChooser.kt:82-86)

## Settings > Scrobbling

### How the page is reached, and its frame

- [x] The Settings front page lists this page in the "Lyrics and history" group as a category row with the `OctoIcons.Scrobbling` icon and the title "Scrobbling" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:320-322) (iPhone: SF Symbol chart.line.uptrend.xyaxis on the "Lyrics and history" row)
- [x] Its summary reads "Off" when ListenBrainz is switched off, "Not connected yet" when on with no token, "Needs a new token" when the saved token stopped working, else "ListenBrainz as <user name>" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:104-109)
- [x] The page title is "Scrobbling" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:21, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:115)
- [x] The page offers ListenBrainz only (no Last.fm on the phone); it sends plays straight from the phone, with or without a server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:107-164)
- [x] Before the stored values load the page shows the defaults (off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:57-58) (iPhone: the values are read from UserDefaults at once, so there is no loading moment)
- [x] Group order: "ListenBrainz"; then "Connect" when needed; then the untitled "Send plays of" group and the untitled Disconnect group when connected (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:115-163)

### ListenBrainz group

- [x] Group title "ListenBrainz" led by the ListenBrainz brand mark drawn in its own colours (not tinted) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:116, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:96, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:246-254)
