- [x] Default off; key "enabled" (boolean) in the "listenbrainz" preferences (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:28-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:42-43, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:165-166)
- [x] Switching on saves it and, when connected, sends the waiting plays at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:123-128)
- [x] Switched off, nothing else shows on the page; plays made while off are not kept, plays already waiting stay but are not sent, and the token stays saved (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:135, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:51-55, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:79-89)
- [x] On, connected and the token working: an info row titled "Connected as" with the ListenBrainz user name on the right (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:123, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:129-131)
- [x] On, connected and the token no longer working: in place of "Connected as", a note in error red: "The saved token stopped working. Paste a new one; plays wait here until then." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:124-128)
- [x] On and connected with plays waiting: an info row titled "Waiting to send" with the count, grouped by thousands (for example "1,234"); hidden at 0 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:132)
- [x] The waiting count is read from the queue file once at app start and follows every change to the queue (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:67-71, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:134-163, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:62-68)

### Connect group

- [x] Shown when switched on and either no token is saved or the saved token stopped working (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:137-139)
- [x] Group title "Connect" with the `OctoIcons.Key` icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:138) (iPhone: SF Symbol key leads the group title)
- [x] First a muted note: "Paste the user token from your ListenBrainz settings. It stays encrypted on this phone." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:171-173)
- [x] Then an action row "Open ListenBrainz settings" with a chevron that opens https://listenbrainz.org/settings/ in the browser; if nothing can open it, nothing happens (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:174-178, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:36-37)
- [x] Then a row with a single-line text field (placeholder "User token") taking the spare width and the accent button "Connect" 10 dp to its right, padded 16 dp sides and 12 dp top and bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:179-193)
- [x] The field hides what is typed like a password, uses the password keyboard with autocorrect off, and its keyboard action is Done, which runs Connect (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:188-190) (iPhone: a secure field with the password content type, no autocorrect, its Done key running Connect)
- [x] Whatever is typed or pasted is trimmed of spaces and line breaks at both ends as it goes in (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:186)
- [x] "Connect" is disabled while the field is blank and shows a spinner while the token is being checked (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:193)
- [x] Connect does nothing while a check is running or the field is blank; it clears any earlier message as it starts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:68-71)
- [x] The typed token is held only on the page while connecting, never saved as typed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:61-62)
- [x] The token is first checked for shape on the phone: 8 to 128 characters, only ASCII letters, digits and dashes; anything else is refused as not accepted without asking ListenBrainz (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:244-246, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:322-324)
- [x] Then it asks GET https://api.listenbrainz.org/1/validate-token with the header "Authorization: Token <token>"; a 200 answer with "valid": true and a non-blank "user_name" is accepted; any other 200 answer, or 400 or 401, is not accepted; any other status, or no answer, is unreachable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:33-34, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:244-259)
- [x] Not accepted shows, under the row in small error red text: "ListenBrainz did not accept that token. Copy it again from your settings page." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:76, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:195-197)
- [x] Unreachable, or any unexpected error, shows: "Couldn't reach ListenBrainz. Check the connection and try again." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:73, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:77)
- [x] On a failed check the typed token stays in the field; the message stays until the next Connect or Disconnect (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:68-88)
- [x] Accepted: the field and message clear; the token is encrypted with a key held in the phone's keystore under its own alias "octo.listenbrainz.token" (separate from the server password, so signing out of a server keeps ListenBrainz connected) and saved with the user name; "needs a new token" is cleared; the switch is turned on; failure counters reset; waiting plays are sent at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:75, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:79, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:36-38, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:81-94, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:105-117, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/CredentialVault.kt:14-28) (iPhone: the token is its own Keychain item "octo.listenbrainz.token" (this device only), apart from server passwords)
- [x] Connecting as a different ListenBrainz user than the one saved before deletes the plays waiting for the earlier user (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:81-94)
- [x] Once accepted the page shows "Connected as <user>" and the Connect group goes away (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:123-139)

### Send plays group (connected only)

- [x] Shown whenever a token is saved, including while it needs replacing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:141)
- [x] An untitled group with the footer under it: "If your server already sends its plays, choose Only songs on this phone so none count twice." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:142-144, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:124-126)
- [x] Row 1 is a choice row titled "Send plays of" showing "All songs" or "Only songs on this phone" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:95-99, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:145)
- [x] Its popup is titled "Send plays of" and lists "All songs" with "Every song you play here" (stored `All`), then "Only songs on this phone" with "Songs your server also has are left out" (stored `PhoneOnly`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:101-105, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:145-151) (iPhone: a system menu headed "Send plays of", each choice with its line under it)
- [x] Default "All songs"; key "send_plays" (enum name) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:33, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:46, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:169)
- [x] With "Only songs on this phone", neither plays nor now playing are sent for songs the server hears: songs found online, and library songs with any copy on a server (a source whose id starts with "server:") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:101-110, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:84, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:98)
- [x] Row 2 is a switch row titled "Show what I'm playing now" with the helper "Shown on your profile while it plays." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:152-157)
- [x] Default on; key "now_playing" (boolean) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:34, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:47, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:170)

### Disconnect group (connected only)

- [x] A separate untitled group with one action row "Disconnect", its title in error red, no chevron (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:159-161, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:297)
- [x] Tapping it disconnects at once, with no confirmation and no message (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:84-88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:160)
- [x] Disconnect clears the typed token and any message, waits for a send in progress to finish, then deletes the saved token, user name and "needs a new token" flag, switches ListenBrainz off, deletes the keystore key, and deletes every waiting play (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:84-88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:119-121, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:96-105)
- [x] After Disconnect the page shows only the ListenBrainz switch, off (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:135)

### What counts as a play

- [x] A play counts once the song is at least 30 seconds long and at least half of it or 4 minutes (whichever is less) was heard (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayRules.kt:3-6, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt:278-286)
- [x] Each song start is a "now playing" moment for ListenBrainz (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt:272-276)
- [x] Radio stations are never sent; a file opened from another app is sent only with its title and artist (no album, no length); any song without a title or an artist is not sent (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:202-222, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:77-80)
- [x] A play is dated by when it started, in whole seconds (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:45-48, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:78-82)
- [x] The length sent is the real length learned by playing when the song's listing was wrong (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:43-44, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:204)
- [x] Title, artist and album are trimmed; a length of 0 and a track number of 0 or less are left out; MusicBrainz recording, release, release group and artist ids go only when well formed (8-4-4-4-12 lower-case hex), artist ids without repeats (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:77-99)
- [x] Plays are kept while switched on with a token saved, even while the token needs replacing, so none are lost before it is fixed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:51-55, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:78-89)

### Waiting plays and retries (seen as "Waiting to send")

- [x] Waiting plays are kept in their own file in the app's files ("listenbrainz_queue.json"), written whole and safely each change, so they survive restarts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:58-65, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:139-153)
- [x] The queue keeps each play once (same start time, title and artist), oldest first, at most 5000; past that the oldest are let go (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:42-43, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:112-116)
- [x] Sending is tried at app start, whenever a connection becomes available, after each new play (unless waiting after failures), when switched on, and right after a token is accepted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:60-76, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:87, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:114, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:126, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:138-141)
- [x] Nothing is sent unless switched on, connected and the token working; a saved token that can no longer be opened (for example a backup restored on another phone) marks it as needing a new token (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:142-148, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:77-79)
- [x] Plays go oldest first in batches of up to 1000, as listen type "single" for one play and "import" for more (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:39-40, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:118-122, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:149-152)
- [x] A batch ListenBrainz took leaves the queue and the failure count resets (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:153-156, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:177-183)
- [x] A 401 answer stops sending and marks the token as needing replacement (the page then shows the red note and the Connect group) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:157-160, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:282-285)
- [x] A 400 or 413 answer splits the batch in halves sent on their own, so one bad play does not hold back the rest; a single play refused is let go (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:174-191, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:285)
- [x] A 429 answer waits the time ListenBrainz gave (X-RateLimit-Reset-In seconds, 10 s when missing, held between 1 s and 1 hour) before trying again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:161-164, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:286, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:302-305, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:314-317)
- [x] No answer or any other status waits 30 s after the first failure in a row, doubling each time, never more than 6 hours, then tries again by itself (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:165-169, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:193-200, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:124-130)
- [x] Calls go at least 1 s apart and never two at once; when an answer says X-RateLimit-Remaining is 0 or less, calls wait until X-RateLimit-Reset-In seconds have passed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:171-208, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:241-242, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:293-298)
- [x] Sending never holds up playback and never shows a message; failures are only retried (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:31-34)

### Now playing

- [x] At each song start, when switched on, connected, the token working and "Show what I'm playing now" on (and the song passes "Send plays of"), the song is sent as "playing_now" with no time (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:91-103, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:132-148)
- [x] Now playing is skipped, not held back, while no calls are left in the rate window; its failures are ignored except a 401, which marks the token as needing replacement (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:267-272, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:101)

### What is sent, and where it may go

- [x] Plays go as POST https://api.listenbrainz.org/1/submit-listens with "Authorization: Token <token>" and a JSON body {"listen_type", "payload": [{"listened_at", "track_metadata"}]}; track_metadata holds "artist_name", "track_name", "release_name" (when there is an album) and "additional_info" with "media_player": "Octo", "media_player_version", "submission_client": "Octo", "submission_client_version" (the app version), and when known "duration_ms", "tracknumber" (as text), "recording_mbid", "release_mbid", "release_group_mbid", "artist_mbids" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:132-169, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:274-281)
- [x] Requests carry the user agent "Octo/<version> (Android)" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:47) (iPhone: user agent "Octo/<version> (iOS)")
- [x] The ListenBrainz client talks only to https://api.listenbrainz.org (same scheme, host and port), follows no redirects, trusts only the system's certificates, and carries none of the signed-in server's headers or certificates; any other address is refused, so the token cannot go anywhere else (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:322-356)
- [x] The token is never put in an address, logged or kept by the client; it is opened from the keystore only for the call that needs it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:232-238, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:77-79)

### Stored values and backup

- [x] Stored in the "listenbrainz" preferences: "enabled" (boolean, default false), "user" (string), "token_sealed" (encrypted token), "needs_attention" (boolean, default false), "send_plays" (enum name, default All), "now_playing" (boolean, default true); the user counts as connected only while a sealed token is saved (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:28-56, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:165-171) (iPhone: the same keys in the "listenbrainz" suite, with the token itself in the Keychain and "token_sealed" a true or false flag)
- [x] A backup keeps only the switch, "Send plays of" and "Show what I'm playing now"; never the token or user; restoring leaves who is connected as it is (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:132-139, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:115-122)
- [x] A backup with ListenBrainz switched on adds the restore note "ListenBrainz: connect again to send plays." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:371)

### Search entries for this page

- [x] "listenbrainz": page Scrobbling, no section, title "ListenBrainz", summary "Add the songs you play to your profile", keywords "scrobble", "history", "stats", "profile", "token" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:471-474)
- [x] "send_plays": title "Send plays of", summary "All songs, or only songs on this phone", keywords "phone only", "all songs", "counted twice" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:475-478)
- [x] "now_playing": title "Show what I'm playing now", summary "Your profile shows the song while it plays", keywords "now playing", "profile", "status" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:479-482)
- [x] "scrobbling_disconnect": title "Disconnect", summary "Stop sending plays and forget the token", keywords "sign out", "remove", "listenbrainz" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:483-486)
- [x] The "Send plays of", "Show what I'm playing now" and "Disconnect" rows exist only while connected and switched on, so a search result pointing at them lights nothing otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:135-162, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:155)

### Related: plays sent to the signed-in server (no control on this page)

- [x] Whatever the Scrobbling page says, each song start tells the signed-in server it is playing (scrobble with submission=false) for the song's copy on that server (lowest id first) or for a song found online (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListeningSync.kt:99-106, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListeningSync.kt:184-191)
- [x] Each counted play of a song with a copy on the server in use waits in that account's own queue (at most 500, oldest let go) and is sent with submission=true, oldest first; sending stops at the first play the server cannot take now (unreachable, not a Subsonic answer, wrong login) and a play the server refuses for good is dropped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListeningSync.kt:108-118, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListeningSync.kt:166-174, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/listening/Scrobbles.kt:5-19)
- [x] Those server plays are why the footer warns about counting twice: a server that passes its own plays on to ListenBrainz already has the plays of songs it holds (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ScrobblingPage.kt:143, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:101-110)

## Settings front page: the "This app" entry rows

- [x] The Settings front page's last group is titled "This app" (drawn uppercase as "THIS APP"), its separators inset 66 dp (IconRowInset), and holds exactly two category rows in this order: "Backup and restore", then "About" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:324-330)
- [x] The "Backup and restore" category row uses the icon OctoIcons.Backup (sym_settings_backup_restore) on an accent tile, title "Backup and restore", and the fixed one-line summary "Settings, playlists, likes and favorites"; tapping it opens the Backup and restore page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:325, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:111) (iPhone: SF Symbol archivebox on the accent tile)
- [x] The "About" category row uses the icon OctoIcons.Info (sym_info), title "About", and the summary "Version <version name>" (for example "Version 1.6.0"); when an update is downloaded and ready the summary becomes "Update ready: <new version>" instead (for example "Update ready: 1.6.1") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:326-329, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsSummaries.kt:113-114) (iPhone: SF Symbol info.circle; "ready" means a newer signed release was found, as the phone cannot download and install it)
- [x] The About summary reads the same live updater state as the About page (one shared updater), so a ready update found in the background changes the front-page line without opening About (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsScreen.kt:327-328)
- [x] The page enum titles are exactly "Backup and restore" (SettingsPage.Backup) and "About" (SettingsPage.About), the last two pages in the front page's order (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:22-23)

## Backup and restore page

### Page frame

- [x] The page uses the standard settings frame: floating round back button (content description "Back", 40 dp glazed icon button, 16 dp from the start, 8 dp below the status bar), large title "Backup and restore" in the display style, groups stacked 24 dp apart, the whole page scrolling vertically, top padding status bar + 8 dp + 56 dp, bottom padding navigation bar + 120 dp (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:95, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsPageFrame.kt:28-52, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:25-66) (iPhone: the floating back button and the large title "Backup and restore"; groups 24 apart in one scrolling column)
- [x] The page's state machine has six states: Idle, Working, Saved(ok), Ready(backup, plan), Restored(plan), Problem(message); it starts at Idle and lives in the page's view model (survives rotation) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:31-49)

### Idle view (every state except Ready)

- [x] In every state other than Ready the page shows one untitled group (no title, no icon) with the footer "Settings, sound, playlists, likes and ratings, in one file. Never passwords or keys." under it (caption, muted, 16 dp side padding, 8 dp above) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:110-119)
- [x] Row 1 is an action row titled "Save a backup" with no helper line and no chevron; while the state is Working (saving, reading a file, or restoring) it shows an 18 dp accent spinner (stroke 2 dp) on the right and cannot be tapped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:120-125, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:285-312) (iPhone: the system spinner, small, accent tinted; the row cannot be tapped while busy)
- [x] Row 2 is an action row titled "Restore from a backup" with no helper line and no chevron; while the state is Working it is disabled (title drawn muted, not tappable) but shows no spinner (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:126-131)
- [x] Row 3 appears only when there is a result to report: a note row (caption, muted, full width, no minimum height) with the message for Saved, Restored or Problem; Idle and Working show no note (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:111-116, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:132)
- [x] The result note stays until the state changes (for example "Backup saved." stays after cancelling a file picker, since a cancelled picker changes nothing) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:87-92)

### Saving a backup

- [x] Tapping "Save a backup" opens the system's create-document picker with MIME type "application/json" and the suggested file name "Octo backup <local date>.json", the date in ISO form yyyy-MM-dd (for example "Octo backup 2026-10-11.json") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:87-89, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:122) (iPhone: the system save sheet (.json), suggesting "Octo backup yyyy-MM-dd.json" in the phone's calendar)
- [x] Cancelling the save picker does nothing (no state change) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:88)
- [x] After a file is picked the state becomes Working, the backup is built from everything as it is now, encoded as JSON, and written to the file as UTF-8 bytes (no byte order mark), opening the file in truncate mode ("wt") so an existing file is replaced, off the main thread (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:51-54, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:122-127) (iPhone: the backup is made when Save is tapped (Working shows), then the system save sheet writes the UTF-8 bytes, no byte order mark, replacing any file there)
- [x] Saving reports success only when the output stream opened and the write finished; a null stream or any thrown error gives failure (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:124-126) (iPhone: the save sheet's answer: saved only when the system wrote the file; any error but cancelling counts as failure)
- [x] On success the note reads exactly "Backup saved."; on failure exactly "Couldn't write the backup there." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:112)

### Opening a backup file

- [x] Tapping "Restore from a backup" opens the system's open-document picker filtered to the MIME types "application/json", "text/plain" and "application/octet-stream" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:90-92, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:128) (iPhone: the system file picker, limited to JSON, plain text and data files)
- [x] Cancelling the open picker does nothing (no state change) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:91)
- [x] After a file is picked the state becomes Working and the file is read in 64 KiB chunks, stopping once 32 MiB (32 x 1024 x 1024 bytes) have been read; a file that cannot be opened or read gives "not a backup" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:41-42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:130-144)
- [x] The bytes are decoded as UTF-8 text, a leading byte order mark (U+FEFF) is removed, then the text is parsed as a backup (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:143, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:196-197)
- [x] Text that is not JSON, or JSON that is not a backup object, gives the Problem note "That file isn't an Octo backup." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:197-198, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:62)
- [x] A JSON object whose "kind" is present and not "octo-settings" gives "That file isn't an Octo backup."; a JSON object with no "kind" key at all is accepted, because "kind" defaults to "octo-settings" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:151, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:199)
- [x] A backup whose "version" is greater than 1 gives the Problem note "This backup was made by a newer Octo. Update the app to restore it."; a missing version counts as 1 and any version 1 or lower is read (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:25, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:152, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:200, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:61)
- [x] A readable backup is matched against the library as it is now (the restore plan) and the state becomes Ready, showing the review view; nothing is changed yet (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:58-60, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:147-155)

### Review view (Ready)

- [x] In Ready the page shows a group titled "In this backup" (drawn uppercase "IN THIS BACKUP", led by the 15 dp muted OctoIcons.Backup icon, heading semantics with the original-case title as its description) and the footer "Restoring replaces your settings and adds the rest to what you have." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:97-102, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:88-104) (iPhone: SF Symbol archivebox before the capitalized title, read as a heading in its own words)
- [x] The "In this backup" group holds one note row per line: first every "what the backup holds" line, then every "how much was found" line, each a separate row with a hairline between rows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:103)
- [x] Below it a second untitled group holds two action rows with no helper and no chevron, in order: "Restore" then "Cancel" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:105-108)
- [x] There is no separate confirmation popup: the review view itself is the confirmation, and nothing changes until "Restore" is tapped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:81-83)
- [x] Tapping "Cancel" returns to Idle (main group, no note) and discards the read backup (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:76-78)
- [x] Tapping "Restore" sets Working (the main group shows again with the Save spinner and Restore disabled), runs the restore, then sets Restored with the plan; tapping Restore when the state is no longer Ready does nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:67-74)
- [x] After a restore the note is one note row whose text is "Restored." followed by each "how much was found" line on its own line (joined with newlines) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:113)

### "In this backup" lines (what the file holds), in this order

- [x] Line "Settings: <parts>" when any settings part is present, the parts joined by ", " in this fixed order and only when present: "player", "streaming", "sound", "library", "lyrics", "cache and downloads", "scrobbling", "sort orders" (the last only when the sort map is not empty) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:342-352)
- [x] Line "<n> output with its own sound" / "<n> outputs with their own sound" only when the sound part says each output keeps its own sound and at least one saved sound key is not "all" (n counts keys other than "all") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:353-356)
- [x] Line "<n> equalizer preset" / "<n> equalizer presets" when presets are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:357)
- [x] Line "<n> playlist, <m> song" in singular or plural for each count (for example "1 playlist, 1 song", "3 playlists, 120 songs"), m being the total songs across all playlists, when playlists are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:358-360)
- [x] Line "<n> liked song" / "<n> liked songs" when likes are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:361)
- [x] Line "<n> rating" / "<n> ratings" when ratings are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:362)
- [x] Line "<n> favorite album" / "<n> favorite albums" when favorite albums are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:363)
- [x] Line "<n> favorite artist" / "<n> favorite artists" when favorite artists are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:364)
- [x] Line "<n> pin on Home" / "<n> pins on Home" when pins are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:365)
- [x] Line "Lyrics timing for <n> song" / "Lyrics timing for <n> songs" when per-song lyrics offsets are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:366)
- [x] Line "Lyrics timing for <n> sound output" / "Lyrics timing for <n> sound outputs" when per-output lyrics timings are present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:367)
- [x] Line "<n> playlist kept downloaded" / "<n> playlists kept downloaded" when the offline part names kept playlists (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:368-370)
- [x] Line "ListenBrainz: connect again to send plays." only when the ListenBrainz part exists and its enabled flag is true (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:371)
- [x] Line "Server: <address> as <username>. Sign in again to use it." when the server part exists (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:372)
- [x] Counts use singular wording only for exactly 1 ("1 <one>"); every other count, 0 included, uses the plural with locale digit grouping ("%,d", for example "1,234 songs") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:403)

### "How much was found" lines (restore plan), in this order

- [x] Line "Playlists: <found> of <total song|songs> found" when the plan makes at least one playlist, counting songs found and songs named across the playlists to be made (for example "Playlists: 331 of 340 songs found", "Playlists: 1 of 1 song found") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:378-380, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:287-288)
- [x] Line "Already here, left as they are: <names>" listing the skipped playlists' names as written in the backup, joined by ", ", when any were skipped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:381-383)
- [x] Line "Likes: <found> of <total> found" when the backup named any likes (total counts every entry, duplicates included; found counts distinct songs) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:384)
- [x] Line "Ratings: <found> of <total> found" when the backup named any ratings (total counts every entry, including ones outside 1 to 5) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:385)
- [x] Line "Favorite albums: <found> of <total> found" when the backup named any (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:386-388)
- [x] Line "Favorite artists: <found> of <total> found" when the backup named any (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:389-391)
- [x] Line "Pins: <found> of <total> found" when the backup named any pins (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:392)
- [x] Line "Lyrics timing: <found> of <total> songs found", always the word "songs" even for 1 (for example "Lyrics timing: 1 of 1 songs found"), when the backup named any per-song offsets (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:393-395)
- [x] Line "Kept downloaded: <found> of <total playlist|playlists> found" when the offline part named kept playlists (for example "Kept downloaded: 1 of 2 playlists found") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:396-398)
- [x] Found and total numbers in these lines use locale digit grouping ("%,d") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:401)

### Backup file format (JSON)

- [x] The file is pretty-printed JSON (kotlinx default 4-space indent), every field written even at its default value, null fields left out entirely, keys in the declaration order below (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:174-183) (iPhone: the sound profiles are written in key order, as iOS keeps them in a dictionary; a JSON object's order changes nothing when read)
- [x] Top-level keys in order: "kind", "version", "createdAt", "player", "streaming", "sound", "presets", "library", "server", "playlists", "likes", "ratings", "favouriteAlbums", "favouriteArtists", "pins", "lyrics", "offline", "listenBrainz", "sortOrders" (British "favourite" spelling in these stored key names is kept as is) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:149-172)
- [x] "kind" is always "octo-settings"; "version" is always 1 (the format number, raised only when an older app would read a newer file wrongly) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:23-28)
- [x] "createdAt" is the save time in Unix milliseconds (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:73, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:82)
- [x] A saved backup always contains "player", "streaming", "sound", "library", "lyrics", "offline" and "listenBrainz"; "server" is present only while a server is in use (signed in), otherwise omitted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:81-118)
- [x] "player" object keys: "liveBackground" (default true), "background" object, "crossfade" (false), "crossfadeSeconds" (8), "smartTransitions" (true), "filterSweeps" (true), "matchTempo" (false), "lyricsOnline" (true), "speed" (1.0), "keepPitch" (true), "pitchSemitones" (0), "skipSilence" (false), "autoplay" (true), "radioDiscovery" ("Balanced"), "radioAdventure" ("Balanced"), "radioVariety" ("Normal"), "radioFavorites" (false), "resumeWired" (false), "resumeBluetooth" (false), "resumeAlways" (false), "ambient" object, "reduceMotion" (false), "castRenderers" (true), "castKeepPlaying" (false), "libraryOnly" (false) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:38-89)
- [x] "player.background" keys: "mode" ("Default" | "Artwork" | "Colour" | "Classic", default "Default"), "brightnessCap" (50), "saturation" (180), "contrast" (1.3), "useBpm" (true), "fps" (60), "speed" (25) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:9-37)
- [x] "player.ambient" keys: "strength" ("Off" | "Subtle" | "Rich", default "Subtle"), "home", "library", "search", "settings", "bar", "pageArtwork" (all default true) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientPrefs.kt:10-28)
- [x] Enum values in "player": radioDiscovery one of "LibraryOnly", "ALittle", "Balanced", "Lots", "MostlyNew"; radioAdventure one of "Focused", "Balanced", "Wander"; radioVariety one of "Tight", "Normal", "Wide" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/radio/RadioTuning.kt:21-44)
- [x] "streaming" object keys: "copies" ("PhoneFirst" | "BestQuality", default "PhoneFirst"), "wifi" and "mobile" ("Original" | "Kbps320" | "Kbps256" | "Kbps192" | "Kbps128", default "Original"), "startAfter" ("Instant" | "Short" | "Steady" | "Safe" | "Long", default "Short") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:110-117, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/StreamQuality.kt:5-11, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/StartAfter.kt:5-11)
- [x] "sound" object keys: "perOutput" (whether each output keeps its own sound), "profiles" (a map from output key to a sound settings object; "all" is the shared set, others like "bluetooth:<name>") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:51-54, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sound/SoundEngine.kt:139-143)
- [x] Each sound settings object has keys "eqEnabled" (false), "mode" ("Graphic" | "Parametric"), "graphicGains" (10 numbers, default all 0), "filters" (list of {"type": "Peak" | "LowShelf" | "HighShelf", "frequency", "gainDb", "q" (default 1.41), "enabled" (true)}), "preset" (default "Flat"; omitted when null), "preampDb" (0), "autoPreamp" (true), "correction" (omitted when none; else {"name", "source", "preampDb", "filters"}), "replayGain" ("Off" | "Track" | "Album" | "Smart"), "replayGainPreampDb" (0), "replayGainFallbackDb" (0), "preventClipping" (true), "limiter" (true), "balance" (0, from -1 left to 1 right), "mono" (false) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/sound/SoundModel.kt:13-73)
- [x] "presets" is a list of {"name", "gains"} for the listener's own equalizer presets (10 gains each) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:48-49, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:86)
- [x] "library" object keys: "excludedFolders" (list of folder paths left out of the library, sorted), "syncQueue" (play queue sync with the server, default true), "newPlaylistsOnServer" (save new playlists to the server, default false) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:56-62, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:87-91)
- [x] "server" object keys: "address" and "username" only (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:141-145)
- [x] The saved server address is reduced to scheme, host, port and path with no user name, password, query or fragment, trailing slashes removed, scheme defaulting to "https" when missing; for example "https://me:hunter2@music.example.com/?t=abc&s=salt" saves as "https://music.example.com", "http://10.0.0.5:4533/music/" as "http://10.0.0.5:4533/music", and an address with no host part like "music.example.com?u=me&p=x" as "music.example.com" (anything after "?" or "#" cut, and anything up to the last "@" cut) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:204-211)
- [x] "playlists" is a list of {"name", "songs"} for playlists that exist only on the phone (server playlists are not saved), ordered by when each was created, songs in playlist order (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:93-95, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:88-99, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:186-188)
- [x] Every song in the file is a song key object {"relinkKey", "title", "artist", "album", "durationMs"}; the relink key is the library's relink key (album artist, album, disc, track, title and length); missing text fields are written as "" and a missing length as 0 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:30-40, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:209-217)
- [x] A playlist song that is an online find (not a library track) is still saved, its title, artist, album and length taken from the online song record (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:88-99)
- [x] "likes" is a list of song keys ordered by when each was liked; a liked song no longer in the library is still saved with its relink key and empty text (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:71-78, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:96)
- [x] "ratings" is a list of {"song": song key, "rating"} ordered by when each was rated; rows without a rating are left out (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:80-87, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:97)
- [x] "favouriteAlbums" is a list of {"relinkKey", "name", "artist"} ordered by when each became a favorite (name is the album title, artist the album artist, both "" when the album is gone) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:98, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:204-205)
- [x] "favouriteArtists" is a list of {"relinkKey", "name", "artist": ""} ordered by when each became a favorite (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:99, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:207)
- [x] "pins" is a list of {"kind", "item"} in Home row order; kind is "album", "artist" or "playlist"; album and artist items are held keys like the favorites; a playlist item is {"relinkKey": "", "name": <playlist name>, "artist": ""}; pins of a kind the app does not know, and playlist pins whose playlist is gone, are left out (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:100-107, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/Favourites.kt:37-46)
- [x] "lyrics" object keys: "keepScreenOn" (default true), "offsets" (list of {"song": song key, "offsetMs"} for songs whose lyrics were moved, only songs still in the library, sorted by relink key), "look" (the synced lyrics look; omitted when null), "outputOffsets" (map from output key such as "speaker" or "bluetooth:<name>" to milliseconds, sorted by key) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:78-88, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:108-114)
- [x] "lyrics.look" keys: "style" ("Flowing" | "Classic", default "Flowing"), "emphasis" (100), "glow" (100), "lift" (100), "speed" (100), "inactiveScale" (80), "fade" (100), "cascade" (100), "keepCompleted" (true), "arc" (false) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLook.kt:6-47)
- [x] "offline" object keys: "cacheSize" ("Off" | "Gb1" | "Gb2" | "Gb5" | "Gb10", default "Gb2"), "prefetchWifi" (3), "prefetchMobile" (1), "downloadQuality" (StreamQuality, default "Original"), "wifiOnly" (true), "streamOnWifi" (false), "keepLiked" (false), "keptPlaylists" (playlist names, sorted; a kept playlist id with no playlist left is dropped) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:90-130, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:20-46)
- [x] "listenBrainz" object keys: "enabled" (false), "sendPlays" ("All" | "PhoneOnly", default "All"), "nowPlaying" (true) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:132-139, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainz.kt:103)
- [x] "sortOrders" is a map from list key to "<option id>:asc" or "<option id>:desc" (for example "songs": "RecentlyAdded:desc"); list keys are "songs", "liked", "genre_songs", "folder_songs", "albums", "artist_albums", "artists", "playlists", "downloads", "favourites"; lists never reordered, or holding an order they no longer offer, are left out (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:169-171, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sort/SortSettings.kt:40-46, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/sort/Sorts.kt:129-160)

### What a backup never holds

- [x] A backup never holds passwords, API keys, header values, certificates or certificate aliases; the words "password", "token", "apikey", "api_key", "secret", "header", "certificate" and "alias" never appear in the file (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:141-145, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:63-69)
- [x] A backup never holds the ListenBrainz connection: no token, no user name, no sealed token, no "needs attention" flag; only the three choices (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:132-139, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:334-338)
- [x] Only the server in use is named, and only its plain address and user name, so signing in again is needed after a restore; other saved servers are not in the file (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:92)
- [x] The updater choices (check automatically, install updates, early versions) and the What's new "last seen" marker are not part of a backup (only the stores listed in the backup's make step are read) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:49-118)

### Reading rules and version handling

- [x] Reading ignores unknown keys (a newer file's extra fields are skipped) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:177)
- [x] An unknown enum value in the file falls back to that field's default (for example "copies": "Nonsense" reads as "PhoneFirst", "cacheSize": "Gb500" as "Gb2", "sendPlays": "Sometimes" as "All") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:178-179, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:94-99, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:305-311)
- [x] Every part is optional: a missing part reads as absent (null) or empty, and a part with only some fields gets defaults for the rest, so an older or smaller backup still reads (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:147-172, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:85-92)
- [x] A backup from before output lyrics timing reads with an empty output timing map and its description says nothing about sound outputs (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:87, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:259-264)

### Matching songs, albums and artists in this library

- [x] A backup song is found first by relink key (the first library track with that key, in library order), when the key is not empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:218, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:223)
- [x] A backup song with a blank title is not found unless its relink key matched (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:224)
- [x] Next a song is found by its whole key: the identity title key (SongIdentity.songKeys(artist, title).title, which keeps the kind of recording), the artist match key and the album match key joined with "|"; among several matches the one nearest in length wins, or the first when the backup gives no length (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:219, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:225, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:231-235)
- [x] With a blank artist the search stops there; otherwise the song is looked up as the same song by the same artist on any album: candidates from the title index, kept only when they are the same song (artists agree, same kind of recording, lengths within 10 seconds when both lengths are known), nearest in length winning (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:226-228, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/discovery/Matching.kt:13-17, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/discovery/Matching.kt:45-63)
- [x] Someone else's song of the same name is never taken (test: "Nightcall" by London Grammar does not match Kavinsky's) (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/backup/BackupTest.kt:107-120)
- [x] An album or artist is found by relink key (the library's search key) first; when several share it, the smallest id wins so the choice is the same every time (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:244-249)
- [x] Failing the key, an album or artist with a non-blank name is found by match key of name and of artist (case, accents, punctuation, spacing and bracketed extras ignored, "&" read as "and"), smallest id winning; a blank name finds nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:250-254, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogMerger.kt:276-284)
- [x] Library albums are matched by (id, search key, title, album artist); library artists by (id, search key, name) with no artist (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:152-153)

### The restore plan

- [x] A backup playlist is skipped when a playlist of the same name (trimmed, case-insensitive) already exists here, any playlist counting (phone or server); skipped playlists are left as they are (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:301-302, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:151)
- [x] Each playlist to make keeps its trimmed name, the ids of its songs that were found (in order, missing songs dropped), and how many songs the backup named (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:306-308)
- [x] Planned likes are the found songs, without duplicates (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:310-311)
- [x] Planned ratings keep only ratings from 1 to 5 whose songs were found; a song named twice takes the later rating (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:312-315)
- [x] Planned favorite albums and artists are the found ids, without duplicates (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:316-319)
- [x] A pin of an unknown kind is dropped; album and artist pins need the album or artist found; a playlist pin needs a playlist of that name (case-insensitive) either already here or made by this restore; the planned pins have no duplicates (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:303-304, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:320-328)
- [x] Planned per-song lyrics offsets are those whose songs were found (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:329-332)
- [x] Planned kept playlists are the backup's kept names, trimmed, that name a playlist already here or made by this restore, without case-insensitive duplicates (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backup.kt:333-336)

### Restore steps, merge vs replace (in this order)

- [x] If the backup has a player or streaming part, every player and streaming setting is replaced in one write; a missing one of the two keeps its current values (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:161-166, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:364-402)
- [x] Restored player values are kept in range: crossfade seconds clamped to 2 to 16, speed snapped to the speed steps, pitch clamped to the pitch range, background values clamped to their ranges with an unknown frame rate becoming 60 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:367-376, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/immersive/BackgroundPrefs.kt:39-45)
- [x] Sound is merged: each output named in the backup gets the backup's settings, outputs not named keep theirs, and the "each output keeps its own sound" switch takes the backup's value (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:167, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sound/SoundEngine.kt:145-153)
- [x] Equalizer presets are added: presets with a blank name or not exactly 10 gains are dropped, each remaining one replaces an existing preset of the same trimmed, case-insensitive name, others are kept (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:168, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sound/UserPresets.kt:64-73)
- [x] Library choices are replaced: the excluded folder set becomes exactly the backup's, and queue sync and new-playlists-on-server take the backup's values (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:169-173, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/Folders.kt:76-78)
- [x] ListenBrainz choices (enabled, send plays of, show now playing) are replaced; the connection itself is untouched (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:174, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzStore.kt:117-122)
- [x] Sort orders are restored only for lists this app has and orders that list offers; other lists keep theirs (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:175, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sort/SortSettings.kt:19-25, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/sort/SortSettings.kt:48-54)
- [x] Lyrics: keep-screen-on takes the backup's value; the lyrics look is replaced when the backup has one (each slider clamped to its range); each found song's offset is set (clamped to plus or minus 10,000 ms, 0 removes it); each output timing is set (clamped to plus or minus 2,000 ms, 0 kept) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:176-179, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:27-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:177-181, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsTiming.kt:212-217, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/lyrics/LyricsLookSettings.kt:58-67)
- [x] Playlists are made only when at least one of their songs was found, or when the backup's playlist was empty; a playlist whose songs were all missing is not made; each new playlist gets a new id and goes through the normal playlist store, so a connected server follows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:180-184, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaylistStore.kt:43-53)
- [x] Likes are added only for songs not already liked; existing likes are never removed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:185, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt:56-68)
- [x] Ratings are set for every planned song, overwriting a rating already there; the server copy is noted as rated too (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:186, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RatingStore.kt:29-41)
- [x] Favorite albums and artists are added only when not already favorites; existing favorites are never removed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:187-190, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/favourites/FavouriteStore.kt:38-56)
- [x] The offline settings are replaced from the backup (cache size, prefetch counts clamped 0 to 10, download quality, Wi-Fi only, stream on Wi-Fi, keep liked), and its kept playlists (resolved by name to ids, after the playlists are made) are added to the ones already kept here (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:191-195, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/offline/OfflineSettings.kt:120-133)
- [x] Pins are added last, in the backup's order, after the pins already here; a pin already present is not duplicated; pins stop being added once Home shows 12 pins (PIN_LIMIT); playlist pins resolve by name (case-insensitive) to existing or newly made playlists (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:196-200, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/Favourites.kt:62, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/Favourites.kt:116-121, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/favourites/PinStore.kt:73-81)
- [x] Likes, ratings, playlists and favorites go through the same stores as when the listener makes them by hand, so a connected server is updated as usual (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:157-159)

### Automatic backups

- [x] The app makes no automatic or scheduled backups; a backup file exists only when the listener taps "Save a backup" and picks where it goes (the Backups class is used only by this page) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/backup/Backups.kt:44-48, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/BackupPage.kt:47)
- [x] The manifest sets no allowBackup or data extraction rules, so only the operating system's default device backup applies (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:50-57) (iPhone: iOS device backups (iCloud or a computer) take the app's data by default; downloaded songs are marked to stay out of them)

## About page

### Page frame and order

- [x] The page uses the standard settings frame with the large title "About" and the "Back" button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:102)
- [x] Top to bottom, 24 dp apart: an untitled group (Version, update ready row and its notes when ready, What's new), the "Updates" group, then the credit line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:103-165)
- [x] The About page has no links, no open-source licenses list, no diagnostics or log rows, and no contact or feedback rows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:102-166) (iPhone: the one link is the ready release's page, in place of Install)

### Top group

- [x] Row 1 is an info row titled "Version" with the app's version name on the right (quieter body-small text, right-aligned, up to 2 lines) and no helper line, not tappable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:104, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/rows/SettingsRows.kt:316-321)
- [x] The version name is "1.2.0" or "1.3.0-beta.1" style for a release build (from the release tag), "0.2.0.<build number>" for a test build, "0.2.0" when built with no numbers, and gets the suffix "-debug" in a debug build (app/build.gradle.kts:18-30, app/build.gradle.kts:56-58) (iPhone: the version is the bundle's short version from Info.plist; a debug build stays off by the build flag)
- [x] When an update is ready and Octo is allowed to install apps, the next row is an action row titled "Update ready: <version>" with the helper "Your music and settings stay." and the right-hand word "Install" (no chevron); tapping it starts the install, with Android's own confirmation allowed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:105-113, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:82) (iPhone: "Update ready: <version>" with "Install it the way you put Octo on this iPhone." and "Open", which opens the release's page)
- [~] When an update is ready but Octo is not yet allowed to install apps, the row is titled "Update ready: <version>" with the helper "Allow Octo to install updates first." and the right-hand word "Allow"; tapping it opens the system's "install unknown apps" page for Octo (ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES, "package:<package name>", new task) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:114-123, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:157-163) (not on iPhone: an iPhone has no setting that lets an app install apps)
- [~] Whether installs are allowed is read when the page opens and again every time the page resumes (for example on coming back from the system's settings), so the row switches from "Allow" to "Install" by itself (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:95-100) (not on iPhone: an iPhone has no setting that lets an app install apps)
- [x] The version in "Update ready: <version>" is written as major.minor.patch with "-<early parts joined by dots>" for an early version (for example "1.7.0-beta.2") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerVersion.kt:50)
- [x] Under the ready row, when the signed manifest's release notes are not blank, a note row shows those notes trimmed, as plain text (not markdown), in the secondary text colour (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:124)
- [x] The last row of the top group is an action row titled "What's new" with a chevron and no helper; tapping it opens the What's new panel; closing the panel from here does not mark the Home card as seen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:126, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:166)
- [x] Whether the What's new panel is open survives rotation on the About page (saveable state) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:89) (iPhone: kept in the page's state, which a rotation keeps)

### Updates group

- [x] The second group is titled "Updates" (drawn "UPDATES"), led by the OctoIcons.Sync icon (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:128)
- [x] When the updater is off, the group holds only one note row with the reason, and none of the update controls (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:129-133)
- [x] Row 1: switch row titled "Check for updates automatically" with the helper "Every few hours, downloading on Wi-Fi."; the whole row toggles it; stored as "check_automatically", default on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:134-139, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:22, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:34) (iPhone: the helper says "Every few hours." as nothing downloads on iPhone; same key and default)
- [~] Row 2: choice row titled "Install updates", its current answer on the right ("Ask me" or "When I leave Octo") and a chevron, no helper; stored as "install" holding "Ask" or "OnQuit", default "Ask", any other stored text reading as "Ask" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:140-149, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:169-172, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:23, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:35) (not on iPhone: an iPhone cannot install an update itself, so there is nothing to choose)
- [x] Row 3: switch row titled "Try early versions" with the helper "Before they're finished. Can have rough edges."; stored as "early_versions", default off; read fresh at each check (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:150-155, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:24, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:36, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:120)
- [x] Row 4: action row titled "Check now" with no chevron; its helper line is the updater's last result line (hidden when there is none yet); while a check runs it shows the accent spinner and cannot be tapped; it works whether or not automatic checks are on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:156-162)
- [x] Before the stored update choices load, the rows show the defaults (on, "Ask me", off) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:62) (iPhone: the choices are read before the page draws)
- [x] A failed write of an update choice is logged ("update settings not saved") and otherwise ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:47-53) (iPhone: UserDefaults writes do not fail)

### Install updates chooser

- [~] Tapping "Install updates" pops up the choice list beside the row (glass popup anchored to the row), titled "Install updates", with two answers in this order: "Ask me" with the line "A ready update waits here until you tap Install.", and "When I leave Octo" with the line "A ready update goes in when you leave Octo with nothing playing. Android may still ask the first time." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:140-149, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:169-177) (not on iPhone: an iPhone cannot install an update itself, so there is nothing to choose)
- [~] The current answer is shown selected (the darker pill); picking an answer saves it and closes the list; a tap outside or back closes it without change (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/ChoiceSheet.kt:160-177, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:143-150) (not on iPhone: an iPhone cannot install an update itself, so there is nothing to choose)

### Credit line

- [x] The page ends with one centred line, 32 dp side padding: the italic caption "Made by Winters with" in the muted colour followed by a 12 dp muted filled-heart icon (OctoIcons.Liked) 4 dp after it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:179-203)
- [x] For accessibility the credit line is read as one element with the description "Made by Winters with love"; the text and the icon have no separate description (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:186, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:194, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:198)

### When the updater is off (reasons)

- [x] When the version name is not a player version (for example "0.2.0.189" or "0.2.0.189-debug"), the reason is "This is a test build, so it doesn't update itself." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:52-53)
- [x] Otherwise, when the build has no trusted key, the reason is "This build has no key to check updates with." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:54)
- [x] Otherwise a build forced on for testing (UPDATES_FORCED, from -Pocto.updates.force=true) runs the updater even as a debug build (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:55, app/build.gradle.kts:32-35) (iPhone: the launch step settings-state:updates-on turns the updater on in a debug build, and OCTO_UPDATES_API points it at another address)
- [x] Otherwise a debug build is off with "This is a test build, so it doesn't update itself."; a release build runs the updater (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:56-57)
- [x] When the updater is off nothing is ever checked or downloaded, and the update ready row never appears (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:78, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:100, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:117) (iPhone: nothing is ever downloaded on iPhone; when off nothing is checked either)

### Updater state and result lines

- [x] The updater state holds: the off reason, whether a check runs, a newer version found but not downloaded, a downloaded and checked update ready to install, and the last result line; it starts with only the off reason and no line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:35-46, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:75) (iPhone: no found-but-not-downloaded state, as an iPhone never downloads the update)
- [x] A check that finds the update downloaded and checked sets it as ready, clears the found-only version, and clears the line (the ready row speaks instead) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:129)
- [~] A check that finds a newer version but did not download it (metered network, automatic check) sets the line "Octo <version> is out. It downloads on Wi-Fi, or now if you check." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:130) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update, so a found release is shown as ready at once)
- [x] A check that finds nothing newer clears ready and found, sets the line "Octo is up to date.", and deletes every downloaded release folder kept from before (the cached release list file stays) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:124-126, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:131) (iPhone: no release folders to delete, as nothing is downloaded)
- [x] A newer version with no file for phones sets the line "Octo <version> is out, but not yet for phones." and leaves ready and found as they were (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:132)
- [x] A release or file that fails any safety check clears ready and found and sets the line "An update didn't pass its safety check, so it was deleted. Octo will look again later." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:133)
- [x] When GitHub cannot be reached, asks Octo to wait, or a download stops, the line is "Couldn't check for updates just now. Octo will try again later." and ready and found stay as they were (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:134)
- [x] A check is skipped when one is already running (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:118)

### Check schedule

- [x] The updater loop starts when the app process starts; the first check waits 90 seconds (90,000 ms) after start so opening the app stays quick (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/OctoApp.kt:55-56, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:99-108, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:38-40)
- [x] After each turn the loop waits 6 hours (21,600,000 ms), or longer when GitHub's rate limit asked to come back later (wait = the larger of 6 hours and retry time minus now) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:40-44, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:104-105)
- [x] Each loop turn checks only when "Check for updates automatically" is on (read at that moment); when off the loop still wakes every 6 hours but does nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:104)
- [~] An automatic check downloads the update only on a network that is not metered (an unknown network counts as metered); "Check now" downloads on any network (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:110-114, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:121, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:154-155) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update; a check reads only the release list and its small signed manifest)

### Release feed (where updates are looked for)

- [x] The updater uses its own HTTP client with the system's certificates only (never a server's trusted certificates or headers), connect timeout 20 seconds, read timeout 60 seconds (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:79-84)
- [x] Releases are listed from GitHub's API: GET https://api.github.com/repos/winters27/octo/releases?per_page=100 (the API base can be replaced in a test build with -Pocto.updates.api=<address>) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:15-18, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:86-93, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:85)
- [x] The request sends the headers "Accept: application/vnd.github+json", "X-GitHub-Api-Version: 2022-11-28", "User-Agent: Octo/<version> (android)", and "If-None-Match: <etag>" when a cached list exists (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:94-99, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:89) (iPhone: "User-Agent: Octo/<version> (ios)")
- [x] A 304 answer with a cached list uses the cached list; a successful answer is parsed and, when it carries an ETag, cached with that ETag in <cache>/updates/releases.json, written to a ".tmp" file then moved into place (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:101-109, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:126-138, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:72, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:89) (iPhone: written atomically, which goes through a temporary file)
- [x] A 403 or 429 answer means wait: the retry time is now plus "Retry-After" seconds when given, else "X-RateLimit-Reset" (seconds) times 1000 when "X-RateLimit-Remaining" is "0", else none (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:110-114)
- [x] Any other failing status, a network error, or an answer that is not a list of releases (a captive portal page, say) is a wait with no retry time (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:115-123)

### Choosing a release

- [x] Only releases tagged "android-v<version>" count; desktop tags ("desktop-v...") and the server's dated or bare "v" tags never do; draft releases never count (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerVersion.kt:3-9, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerVersion.kt:65-68, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/Releases.kt:44-52) (iPhone: only "ios-v<version>" tags count)
- [x] Without "Try early versions", a release counts only when GitHub does not mark it pre-release and its tag has no early part; with it, early versions count too (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/Releases.kt:53-54)
- [x] The newest counting release that is newer than the running version is offered; a running early version is still offered the final release of the same numbers (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/Releases.kt:39-56)
- [x] A version is "major.minor.patch" with an optional "-early.parts" (letters, digits and hyphens, dot-separated); numbers have no leading zeros and up to 9 digits; anything else is not a version (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerVersion.kt:52-61)
- [x] Versions order like semantic versioning: by major, minor, patch; a final release is newer than any early version of the same numbers; early parts compare one by one, numeric parts as numbers and lower than text parts, text parts by character order, and a shorter list of equal parts is older (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerVersion.kt:22-44)

### Manifest and signature check

- [x] The chosen release must carry "update.json" and "update.json.sig"; a missing one refuses the release ("the release has no update.json" / "the release has no update.json.sig") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:167-168, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateManifest.kt:35-36)
- [x] The manifest is fetched with a 256 KiB limit and the signature with a 4 KiB limit; a larger file is refused ("a release file is far bigger than it should be"); a failing status is a network error (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:169-170, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:188-205, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:251-254)
- [x] The signature file is base64 (MIME decoding, surrounding whitespace trimmed) of a 64-byte Ed25519 signature over the manifest's exact bytes; it must verify with one of the built-in 32-byte public keys, before the manifest is parsed at all (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateManifest.kt:44-61, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/Ed25519Check.kt:9-24)
- [x] The built-in keys come from trusted-keys.txt: one base64 key a line, "#" starting a comment, blank lines skipped; the shipped file holds one key, "M/nLIDZFkukIYfrJGWK+cPmo/asGBkr+SdHPAWOSPp0=" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateManifest.kt:84-106, shared/core/src/commonMain/resources/app/winters/octo/update/trusted-keys.txt:1-11)
- [x] The manifest must have "format" 1, "product" "android", "tag" equal to the release's tag, and "version" equal to the tag's version; its file entries must each have a 64-character lowercase hex "sha256", a "size" above 0, and a non-blank "name" with no "/" or "\" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateManifest.kt:13-33, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateManifest.kt:62-80) (iPhone: "product" must be "ios")
- [x] The manifest's "notes" text (default "") is what the About page shows under the ready row (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateManifest.kt:19, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:179)

### Download and file checks

- [x] The phone's file is the first manifest entry with "os" "android" and "kind" "apk"; when none exists the result is "not yet for phones" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/ApkChecks.kt:49-50, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:172) (iPhone: the first entry with "os" "ios" and "kind" "ipa")
