- [x] GitHub's listing must have a file of that name with the manifest's size, or the release is refused (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:173-174)
- [~] When asked not to download, the result is "found, not downloaded" with the version, tag, notes and file (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:175) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update)
- [~] The file goes to <cache>/updates/<tag as folder name>/<file name>, the tag's characters other than letters, digits, "." and "-" replaced by "_" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:72, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:176-177, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:257-258) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update file)
- [~] A copy already there with the right size and SHA-256 is used without downloading again (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:210) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update file)
- [~] The download streams into "<name>.part", refusing when the announced length differs from the manifest, when more bytes arrive than the manifest's size, when fewer arrive, or when the SHA-256 differs; only a file that passes is moved into place; any failure deletes the part and the target (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:209-245) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update file)
- [~] Once a release's file is ready, every other release folder under <cache>/updates is deleted (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:178, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:247-249) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update file)
- [~] Any refusal deletes that release's folder; a network error during the manifest or file fetch counts as "couldn't check" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/update/PlayerUpdater.kt:180-185) (not on iPhone: an iPhone never downloads the update file; a network error is still "couldn't check")

### APK checks before an update counts as ready

- [~] A downloaded APK counts as ready only when Android can read it, it is this same app (same package name), its version code is higher than the installed one, both are signed, and every certificate the installed app is signed with is among the download's signers or its signing history (a rotated key still counts) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/ApkChecks.kt:7-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:140-152) (not on iPhone: there is no APK; an iPhone app is installed from a computer)
- [~] Certificates are compared by the lowercase hex SHA-256 of each certificate; for a single-signer package the current signer is the last entry of its signing history, and the whole history counts for rotation; a multi-signer package lists all its signers and no history (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/ApkChecks.kt:31-47) (not on iPhone: there is no APK; an iPhone app is installed from a computer)
- [~] An APK that fails is deleted with its release folder, logged as "update refused: <reason>", and reported as a refused update (reasons: "this Octo could not be read", "the download is not an app Android can read", "the download is another app", "the download is not newer than this Octo", "the download is not signed", "the download is signed with another key") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:145-150, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/ApkChecks.kt:22-29) (not on iPhone: there is no APK; an iPhone app is installed from a computer)

### Installing

- [~] Installing does nothing unless an update is ready and Octo is allowed to install apps (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:169-171) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself)
- [~] The install uses a full-install package installer session for this package, with the file's size and the reason "user"; the file is written into the session as "octo.apk" and synced, then committed with a broadcast back to the install receiver carrying whether the listener asked (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:204-225) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself)
- [~] An install not asked for (leaving Octo) asks Android for no user action (Android 12 and newer) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:210-212) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself)
- [~] If the installer cannot be started (file unreadable, or Android refuses), the line becomes "The update couldn't start. It stays ready for the next try." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:172-184) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself)
- [~] When Android needs the listener to confirm and the install was asked for (tapped Install), Android's confirmation screen opens; an install started by leaving Octo does not open it and waits for the next tap (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateInstallReceiver.kt:13-24) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself)
- [~] Success and "waiting for confirmation" change nothing on the page; the listener cancelling Android's confirmation (aborted) leaves the line as it was; any other failure is logged and sets the line "The update didn't install. It stays ready for the next try."; the update stays ready in every failure case (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:196-202) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself)

### Install when leaving Octo

- [~] Each time the main screen stops (not for a rotation or other configuration change), Octo reports that it was left, with whether music is playing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:140-147) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself, so leaving has nothing to start)
- [~] On leaving, with nothing playing, an update ready, and "Install updates" set to "When I leave Octo", the update installs with no confirmation asked for; while music plays, or with "Ask me", nothing happens (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/AppUpdates.kt:187-194) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install an update of itself)

### Build, permissions and storage

- [x] The update choices live in their own store named "updates" with keys "check_automatically" (Boolean), "install" (String) and "early_versions" (Boolean) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateSettings.kt:20-24) (iPhone: UserDefaults suite "updates" with "check_automatically" and "early_versions"; no install choice)
- [~] The app declares permission to request package installs and to update itself without user action (Android 12 and newer), and network state access (for the metered check) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:14, app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:34-40) (not on iPhone: there are no install permissions on iPhone, and reading the network needs none)
- [~] The install receiver is not exported and its extra key is "app.winters.octo.update.ASKED" (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:202-204, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/update/UpdateInstallReceiver.kt:40-42) (not on iPhone: there is no install receiver on iPhone)

## What's new

### Home card

- [x] Home shows the What's new card near the top: after the "Home" title, the music access card (when asked for) and the resume card, before the shelves or the empty card (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/HomeScreen.kt:82-86)
- [x] The card is a glass panel with 20 dp corner radius, 20 dp side margins, 8 dp below, 20 dp inner padding, lines 4 dp apart (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:62-69)
- [x] Card line 1: "New in Octo" in the caption style, muted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:71)
- [x] Card line 2: the summary "A new equalizer, sorting, favorites, downloads and much more" in the body style, primary colour, at most 2 lines with an ellipsis (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:72-78, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:17)
- [x] Card buttons, 12 dp below the text and 12 dp apart: glaze button "See what's new" (opens the What's new panel) then glaze button "Dismiss" (puts the card away) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:79-82)
- [x] The card fades and expands in when due, and fades and shrinks out when put away (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:61) (iPhone: fades and slides in and out over 250 ms)
- [x] The card starts hidden and appears only once the stored state is known, so it never flashes up (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:43-44) (iPhone: the stored state is read before Home draws, so the card never flashes up)
- [x] "Dismiss" marks this version as seen; opening the panel from the card does not, but closing that panel (tap outside or back) marks it seen and puts the card away (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:50-52, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:85-91)

### When the card is due, and how it is remembered

- [x] The last seen version is stored in its own store named "whats_new" under the int key "last_seen_version", holding the app's version code (the build number, not the version name) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:28-30, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:44-46) (iPhone: UserDefaults suite "whats_new", key "last_seen_version", holding the bundle's build number)
- [x] The card is due when a last seen version is stored and it is lower than the running version code; with nothing stored it is due only when this is not a fresh install (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:23-26, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:35)
- [x] A fresh install is one whose first install time equals its last update time (never updated since installing); if the app's own package info cannot be read it counts as fresh (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:48-54) (iPhone: iOS gives no install time, so a fresh install is one where none of an earlier Octo's stored values are found)
- [x] When Home's card first loads, a fresh install with nothing stored stores the current version code as seen, so the first later update shows the card (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:37-42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:46-48)
- [x] The card shows once per version code: after it is marked seen it stays hidden until a build with a higher version code is installed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:25-26, app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewTest.kt:26-30)

### What's new panel

- [x] The panel is a floating glass card centred on screen (no anchor), at most 460 dp wide (and the screen width minus 12 dp margins each side), at most 82% of the screen height (and within the space between the system bars minus 12 dp margins), its content scrolling inside when longer (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:31-41, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:240-252) (iPhone: a system sheet at half height that pulls up to full, its content scrolling)
- [x] The panel closes on a tap outside the card or on back (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:28-30, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:187-239) (iPhone: the sheet closes when pulled away or on a tap above it)
- [x] The panel grows in from 92% to full size while fading in over 160 ms on the spring curve; with Reduce motion it only fades, in half the time; with the phone's animations off it appears at once (design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:175-224, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/OctoMotion.kt:39, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/OctoMotion.kt:121) (iPhone: the system sheet's own motion)
- [x] The panel's accessibility pane title is "What's new" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:38, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassPopup.kt:226)
- [x] Panel content padding: 24 dp start and end, 26 dp top, 28 dp bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:44-49) (iPhone: 30 points at the top, leaving room for the sheet's handle)
- [x] Panel line 1: "What's new" in the label style, secondary colour (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:50)
- [x] Panel line 2: "Octo <version name>" (for example "Octo 1.6.0") in the title style, primary colour, 2 dp above and 18 dp below, marked as a heading (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:51-56)
- [x] Then the notes from the app's bundled whats_new.md, read once when the panel opens and rendered as markdown; if the file cannot be read the notes area is empty and a warning is logged ("what's new: could not read the notes: <error kind>") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:42-43, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:57, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewPanel.kt:62-68) (iPhone: the notes are built into the app, so they can always be read)
- [x] The same panel opens from Home's card and from About's "What's new" row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/home/WhatsNewCard.kt:85-91, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AboutPage.kt:166)

### The notes file (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md)

- [x] The file holds the notes for the current update only (no per-version sections); it is rewritten with each update (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNew.kt:19-21)
- [x] It opens with one intro paragraph: "A big update: a new **equalizer**, **sorting** for every list, **favorites**, **downloads**, lyrics that follow the song, and much more." (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:1)
- [x] Then 8 level-2 headings, each followed by one bullet list: "Sound" (4 bullets), "Library" (8), "Playback" (8), "Server" (9), "Lyrics" (4), "Offline" (5), "Everyday touches" (10), "Settings" (4), 52 bullets in all, no nested bullets, no links, no rules (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:3-77)
- [x] "Sound" bullets: equalizer with live curve and presets; loudness; headphone correction and balance; per-output sound (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:3-8)
- [x] "Library" bullets: sort any list; favorites; pins on Home; browse by folder; history; star ratings; Play and Shuffle and a random album; pull to refresh (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:10-19)
- [x] "Playback" bullets: Play next and Add to queue while shuffling; queue history, clear, save, stop after; Autoplay; speed and pitch and skip silence; sleep timer extras; crossfade and resume on reconnect; swipe artwork and landscape player; Android Auto and assistant (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:21-30) (iPhone: the last line is about AirPlay in place of Android Auto and turning the phone sideways)
- [x] "Server" bullets: Discover; stations and radio; internet radio; two-way playlists; share links; queue pick-up; artist pages; scan and who is listening; Octo status in Settings (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:32-42)
- [x] "Lyrics" bullets: lyrics sources; synced lyrics with tap to seek; move timing; screen stays on (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:44-49)
- [x] "Offline" bullets: downloads; kept Liked songs and playlists; stream cache; prefetch; dimmed unplayable songs offline (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:51-57)
- [x] "Everyday touches" bullets: Undo and short notes; multi-select; Song info; swipe right to play next; widgets; launcher shortcuts; open audio files; share, ringtone, delete; recent searches and filters; playlist file import and export (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:59-70) (iPhone: widgets, Siri and Shortcuts, files from the Files app, and sharing or deleting a song's file)
- [x] "Settings" bullets: a page per topic and search; ambient glow; ListenBrainz; backup and restore (app/src/main/assets/whats_new.md:72-77)
- [x] Content rules enforced by tests: no long dash characters (U+2014 or U+2013) in the summary or any notes line; the headings are exactly the 8 above in that order; every heading is followed by a non-empty bullet list; no "**" or "`" is left visible after parsing; the Home summary is at most 70 characters (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/whatsnew/WhatsNewTest.kt:32-71)

### Markdown parsing (as used for the notes)

- [x] Line endings "\r\n" are read as "\n"; each line's trailing whitespace is dropped (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:65-66)
- [x] A blank line ends the current paragraph and the current list (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:67-71)
- [x] A line of three or more of the same "-", "*" or "_" (spaces allowed between) is a horizontal rule; this is checked before headings and bullets, so "---" and "* * *" are rules (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:32, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:72-77)
- [x] A line of 1 to 6 "#" then whitespace is a heading; levels 4 to 6 are drawn as level 3; trailing " #..." marks are removed (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:30, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:78-85)
- [x] A line starting (after optional indent) with "-", "*" or "+" and whitespace is a bullet; indent counts a tab as 4 spaces; an indent of 2 or more under an existing list makes it a child of the last top-level bullet, and deeper indents still sit one level in (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:31-35, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:86-99)
- [x] A non-bullet line that starts with a space right after a bullet continues that bullet (joined with one space); any other text line ends the list and joins the paragraph (paragraph lines joined with one space) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:100-108, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:46-51)
- [x] Numbered lists, tables, block quotes, images and code blocks are not recognised and read as plain paragraph text (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:3-7)
- [x] Inline: a backslash before one of \ ` * _ [ ] ( ) # + - . ! $ shows that character as is (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:137-140, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:229-231)
- [x] Inline code is text between two backticks with at least one character inside; a lone or empty pair stays literal (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:141-151)
- [x] Bold is "**text**" or "__text__", italic "*text*" or "_text_"; a mark opens only when text follows straight away (not a space or the same mark) and it is not inside a word, and only when a matching close exists that follows text and is not followed by a letter or digit; a single mark is never half of a double one; so "F*ck It", "$UICIDE" and "snake_case" stay as written (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:152-163, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:186-215)
- [x] A link is "[label](url)" with a non-blank label and a non-empty url without whitespace; the label's own marks are read; links never nest; anything else stays literal (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:164-175, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:217-227)
- [x] Neighbouring runs with the same look are joined and empty runs dropped (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/markdown/Markdown.kt:234-247)

### Markdown rendering (as used for the notes)

- [x] Level-1 headings use the headline style (line height 26 sp), primary colour, 28 dp above; level-2 the body style in bold (line height 22 sp), primary colour, 26 dp above; level-3 the label style (line height 19 sp), secondary colour, 18 dp above; every heading has 8 dp below, no space above when it is the first block, and heading semantics (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:40-42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:59-73)
- [x] Paragraphs use body-small with 21 sp line height in the secondary colour, 10 dp above (none when first) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:37-38, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:74-79)
- [x] A bullet list starts 6 dp below the previous block (none when first), items 8 dp apart; each item is a 5 dp muted circle dot (8 dp from the top, 12 dp before the text) then the text in the paragraph style (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:80-86, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:107-114)
- [x] Child bullets sit 18 dp further in, 6 dp apart, with a 4 dp dot in the muted colour at 35% opacity (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:86-91)
- [x] A rule is a 1 dp line of the primary colour at 8% opacity with 18 dp above and below (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:48, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:95-101)
- [x] Bold words are drawn in the primary colour at semi-bold weight (the body otherwise in the quieter secondary colour); italic slants; code is monospace at 13 sp on a white patch at 8% opacity; links are primary colour, underlined, and open their URL when tapped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:44-46, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/markdown/MarkdownText.kt:116-132)

## Family: when it exists and how it is reached

### Availability

- [x] Family exists for a session only when the server's OpenSubsonic extensions include exactly `octoFamily:1` (extension name `octoFamily`, version 1); the extension set is stored per kept server as "name:version" strings (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:41-42, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:12-15)
- [x] The server lists `octoFamily` only while the owner has Family on; the app re-reads extensions over time, so Family can appear or vanish for a kept server (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:12-14, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:200-213)
- [x] When the session is not a Family server, `FamilyModel.refresh()` wipes all family state (`forget()`) and asks nothing; `plan()` answers null without any call (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:176-178, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:205-210)
- [x] Test: with the extension missing, `refresh()` leaves `info` null, `plan()` is null and the server gets zero calls (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:206-213)

### Start-up and account changes (FamilyHub)

- [x] `FamilyHub` is one app-wide singleton started from the Application `onCreate` (`family.start()`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/OctoApp.kt:63-64, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:58-64) (iPhone: started once from the app's first window, an app-wide object)
- [x] On start it watches the session state, skipping the Loading state, and reacts each time the account id changes (distinct values only) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:126-128)
- [x] On every account change: the model forgets everything of the last account, the locally saved set (`savedHere`) is emptied, an open request sheet is closed (`asking = null`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:129-131)
- [x] On every account change: background request checks are scheduled when the new session has Family on, and cancelled otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:132-134) (iPhone: background app refresh scheduled or cancelled)
- [x] On every account change: the plan (abilities) is read once at once (`model.plan()`) so song menus and offline copies follow it before the Family screen is ever opened (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:135)
- [x] `model.forget()` clears: the watch loop, `info`, `problem`, `requests`, `inbox`, `devices`, `saved`, `login`, closes the hand-over sheet, clears `said` and the open `invite` (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:159-172)

### Entry points into the Family screen

- [x] Settings > Server and sync page, section "Connection": an action row "Family" with the line "Your plan, requests and devices, and the family's" shows only while the session has Family on; tapping it pushes the Family screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:232-233, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:304-307)
- [x] The "Family" row is findable in settings search by the words "family", "members", "requests", "devices", "approve", "plan", "storage" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:304-307)
- [x] Settings > Server and sync page, section "Connection": an action row "Sign in on another device" with the line "A QR code your phone or computer scans to sign in with your login" shows only while Family is on; tapping it first opens the hand-over sheet (`family.model.handOver.open()`) and then pushes the Family screen, so the sheet shows over the Family screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:152, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:234-239, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:308-311)
- [x] The "Sign in on another device" row is findable by "qr", "scan", "another device", "phone", "computer", "sign in", "login" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:308-311)
- [x] A tapped family notification (action `app.winters.octo.OPEN_FAMILY`) calls `family.open(FamilyOpen.Family)`; intents relaunched from recents/history are ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:94-104) (iPhone: a tapped notification reaches the app through its notification delegate)
- [x] An opened family link (VIEW intent whose data parses as a family link) calls `family.open(FamilyOpen.Join(link))`; the join form itself belongs to the join part (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:101-102) (iPhone: octo:// links arrive through onOpenURL)
- [x] The shell consumes `hub.opens` once (`opened()` resets it to null), switches to the last tab (Settings), and pushes `FamilyRoute` for `Family`, or `FamilyJoinRoute(familyAppLink(link))` for `Join` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:167-181, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:76-87) (iPhone: Family is pushed on the Settings tab; a join opens the sign-in form as a full screen cover)
- [x] `FamilyRoute` renders `FamilyScreen(back, onImport = { open(ImportRoute) })` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:404)

### Clipboard offer (links into the join part)

- [x] Each time the app window gains focus, the first clipboard item's text is read (failures ignored) and offered to `offerClipboard` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:85-92) (iPhone: checked each time the scene becomes active; iOS pattern check first, so reading asks only for a web link)
- [x] The text is trimmed; blank text, text equal to the last offered text, or text that is not a family link is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:94-98)
- [x] A valid link is remembered as offered (offered once per distinct text) and a message is shown: "A family invite is on the clipboard" for an invite link, else "A family sign-in link is on the clipboard", with the action "Use it" that opens the join flow (`FamilyOpen.Join(link)`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:98-100)
- [x] A message with an action stays up 6,000 ms; one without stays 3,500 ms; a new message replaces the one showing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Feedback.kt:55-57, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Feedback.kt:70-86)

## Family screen: layout shared by every account

### Frame and loading

- [x] The screen is one vertically scrolling list with top padding of status bar + 8dp + 56dp (`DetailTopGap`) and bottom padding of navigation bar + 120dp (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:101, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:25-29, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:52)
- [x] First item: the page title "Family" in the display style, 20dp sides, 8dp above, 16dp below (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:102, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:32-38)
- [x] A round floating back button sits at the top left (status bar + 8dp top, 16dp start) with the content description "Back" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:119, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:56-62) (iPhone: Liquid Glass back button)
- [x] While the screen is shown the model "watches": it reads the server at once and then again every 30,000 ms; leaving the screen stops the loop; a second watch while one runs is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:95-98, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:143-157, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:383-384)
- [x] Before the first successful read (`info == null`) the only item under the title is one line: the problem text when there is one, else "Asking the server about your plan." (bodySmall, muted, 20dp sides, 6dp top and bottom) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:103-105, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:166-169)
- [x] The problem text is "Octo can't reach the server right now." when the server cannot be reached, else the server's own error message, else "The server did not answer." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:227-231)
- [x] Once `info` has been read, a later failed read sets `problem` but the screen keeps showing the last good content and does not show the problem (the problem line only shows while `info` is null) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:103-116, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:211-231)
- [x] Test: a closed server gives the problem "Octo can't reach the server right now." (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:225-230)

### Order of the parts on the phone

- [x] After a successful read the items show in this exact order: notices permission row, the "said" line (when set), Your login (when the login was read), Requests waiting (when the account approves), Members (when the account manages), My plan, Requests, Devices, Saved (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:106-116)
- [x] The phone order above differs from the shared `FamilySection` order (Login, Plan, Saved, Requests, Devices, Members, Inbox) that `sections()` returns for other platforms; the phone does not use `sections()` (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:48-57, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:133-141)
- [x] `sections()` keeps Login only when `login != null`, Members only when the account manages (`info.manager != null`), Inbox only when the account approves (`abilities.approveRequests`), and every other section always (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:133-141)
- [x] Test: a listener's sections are exactly Plan, Saved, Requests, Devices; an owner without a login read gets every section except Login; with the login read, Login is first (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:57, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:81, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:136)
- [x] Each part's title is a section title (section style, primary color, 20dp sides, 10dp top and bottom) with 16dp extra space above it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:171-173, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:42-48)

### Notices permission row

- [x] The row shows only on Android 13 (API 33) and later and only while notification posting is not allowed; on older versions or once allowed it is absent (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:175-182, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:59-61) (iPhone: shown while iOS notifications are not allowed for Octo)
- [x] Row content: "Hear when a request is decided" (body, primary), "Octo can tell you when a request is approved, declined or added, and a manager when requests wait." (caption, muted), and a small glaze button "Turn on notices"; 20dp sides, 8dp top and bottom, 8dp between (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:183-187)
- [x] "Turn on notices" asks the system for the notification permission; the row hides when the answer is yes and stays when it is no (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:180-186) (iPhone: asks iOS; once refused, it opens Octo's notification settings)
- [x] The allowed state is read once when the row first composes and then only from the permission answer (a grant made in system settings shows only after the screen is rebuilt) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:180)

### The "said" line (results of actions)

- [x] `said` holds the server's words about the last thing asked (or the app's own success words) and shows as one line in the secondary text color, under the notices row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:108, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:105-107)
- [x] On the phone `said` is never cleared by a timer or a tap; it stays until another action replaces it or the account changes (`clearSaid` has no phone caller) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:361-363, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:170)
- [x] Every screen action (cancel, approve, decline, remove device, unsave, quality changes) runs through `act`: `working` is true while it runs, the success words go to `said`, an unreachable server gives "Octo can't reach the server right now.", any other server error gives its message or "The server said no.", `working` goes false, then the whole family is read again (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:365-380)
- [x] Manager actions (add member, reset password) run through `manage` with the same `working`, words and error texts, then a full read again (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:343-359)
- [x] While `working` is true every button that is gated on it is disabled (Cancel on a request, Approve, Decline, Remove from list, Reset password, Add and show their invite) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:244, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:271, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:306, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:350, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:378-379)

### Row shape used by Requests, Devices, Members and Saved

- [x] A list row is: optional 44dp cover with 6dp corners and 12dp gap, then a column with the title (bodySmall, primary, 1 line), the line under it when not blank (caption, muted, up to 2 lines), and for a request its state line (caption, state color); then the actions 12dp from the text; row padding 20dp sides, 8dp top and bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:384-398)
- [x] A row's cover is drawn only when the cover id resolves to a picture on the server in use; ids starting with "ext-" are drawn with the not-in-library mark (outside) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:389-390, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:111-116)
- [x] A cover id resolves to the server artwork reference (server source id + cover id, online when it starts with "ext-"); a blank id or no signed-in session gives no picture (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:111-116, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:78-79, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:100)
- [x] Request state colors: Done in signal green; Declined and Failed in signal orange; Pending, Approved and Cancelled in the secondary text color (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:400-404)

## Family screen: everyone (My plan, Requests, Devices, Saved)

### My plan

- [x] Title "My plan" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:192, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:21)
- [x] First line: "<display name, or username when blank>, <role label>", for example "Alex, Listener" (body, primary) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:195, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:207-208)
- [x] Role labels: Owner "Owner", CoAdmin "Co-admin", Member "Member", Listener "Listener", Kid "Kid", Unmanaged "Not in the family" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:42-49)
- [x] A role name this app does not know shows the server's own name split before each capital that follows a lower-case letter ("FamilyGuest" reads "Family Guest", "Guest" reads "Guest"); an empty name reads "Member" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:51-55, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:193-200)
- [x] Second line: "Your library" (bodySmall, secondary) on the left, and the storage line (caption, secondary) at the end (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:196-199)
- [x] Storage line: with a limit "<used> of <limit> GB used" ("5.4 of 10 GB used", "40 MB of 10 GB used", "0 of 5 GB used"); with no limit (0) "<used> used" ("5.4 GB used", "120 GB used") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:133-141, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:115-121)
- [x] Sizes are decimal: 0 bytes or less "0 GB"; under 0.1 GB whole megabytes "<n> MB"; under 100 GB one decimal with a trailing ".0" dropped; 100 GB and up whole gigabytes; US number format (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:120-131)
- [x] A 3dp storage meter shows only when there is a limit: track muted text color at 25% alpha, fill accent, 2dp corners, fraction used/(limit x 1,000,000,000) clamped 0 to 1 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:200, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:229-234, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:147-149)
- [x] A weekly line (caption, muted) shows when the weekly limit is above 0 or at least one request was made this week: with a limit "<used> of <limit> request(s) used this week" ("3 of 10 requests used this week"), with no limit "<n> request(s) this week" ("1 request this week") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:201-203, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:153-155)
- [x] Plurals: count 1 gives "1 <word>", any other count "<count> <word>s" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:210)
- [x] Under it, one row per ability line: a 16dp icon (check in secondary color when the line is "on", close/cross in muted when "off"), 12dp gap, the text (bodySmall, primary when on, muted when off); row padding 20dp sides, 6dp top and bottom; icons have no content description (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:206-216) (iPhone: SF Symbols checkmark and xmark)

- [x] The add is two family page calls: `POST <server>/api/family/members` with JSON `{"username": <trimmed>, "displayName": <trimmed>, "preset": "CoAdmin"|"Member"|"Listener"|"Kid"}`, then `PUT <server>/api/family/members/<username>` with `{"edits":{"away":<true|false>}}`, using the username from the answer (or the typed one trimmed) percent-encoded (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:92-110)
- [x] If either call fails nothing is shown but the error in said (no invite sheet), even when the member was already made by the first call (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:285-300, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:343-359)
- [x] On success the invite sheet opens for: name = the answer's display name, else the typed name, else the typed username; username = the answer's username, else the typed one trimmed; link = `inviteLink`; the two links (`links`), `anywhereAvailable` and `homeOnly` from the answer; away allowed only when the switch was on and the answer's `awayAllowed` is true (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:285-298)
- [x] Success also sets said "Added <name>", then a full read (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:299, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:357)
- [x] The app remembers (for this account session only) which usernames it added with away off ("kept home"), and drops a username from that set when added with away on; a later reset link for a kept-home member offers only the home link (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:289, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:302-304, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:318)
- [x] Test: adding "sam" as Kid opens an invite titled "Invite Sam" with the server's link, signed like every call, with no password parameter and no separate auth call (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:151-165)

## Invite sheet and new sign-in link sheet (manager)

### Opening and sizing

- [x] The sheet shows over the Family screen while the model holds an invite (after Add a member or a password reset); it exists only on the Family screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:122, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:135-148)

### Which link (Anywhere or At home)

- [x] The invite's link choices: when the server sent `links`, Anywhere = `links.anywhere`, At home = `links.home`, Anywhere available only when `anywhereAvailable`, `links.anywhere` is present and not `homeOnly`; without `links`, a home-only or not-anywhere server makes the one link the home link (Anywhere unavailable), otherwise the one link is the Anywhere link with no home link (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:171-176, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:31)

- [x] The link for a choice: with no home link, the Anywhere link; At home picked or away not allowed, the home link; Anywhere picked and available, the Anywhere link; Anywhere picked but unavailable, none (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:159-166)

- [x] The question over the choices reads "Where will they use it?" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:127-129, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:502)

## Shared sheet parts (invite, hand-over, password)

### Fit one screen, sticky footer, More below

### QR code drawing

- [x] The QR code is made on the device from the full link text with ZXing at error correction level M (medium) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt:24-31) (iPhone: made on the phone by Core Image's QR generator at level M)

- [x] The code sits in a well of side + 16dp (white 4% fill, 1dp white 8% border, 20dp corners), centered (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:681-690)
- [x] The code tile is white with 14dp corners and a quiet border of side/13 on each side; dark modules are drawn black, each half a pixel oversized to avoid seams (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:692-705)
- [x] When the link changes the code cross-fades over 300ms (scaled by the motion setting) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:685, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/OctoMotion.kt:47-48)

- [x] Test: a code drawn 4 pixels per module with a 4-module light border reads back to the same link, also inverted, and from a padded camera brightness plane (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:202-224) (iPhone: Core Image draws the code and the camera's QR reader reads it; neither runs on Linux, so no unit test)

### Link row

- [x] A pill (white 4% fill, 1dp white 8% border, fully rounded, 14dp start, 2dp end): a 16dp link icon in the accent hover color, the shortened link (bodySmall, accent hover, up to 2 lines, shrinking down to 10sp, never cut), and a "Copy link" copy button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:734-758) (iPhone: SF Symbol link)

- [x] The shown link drops the scheme, keeps the address before "#" without a trailing slash, and adds "…#" plus the first fragment parameter (up to its "&", at most 20 characters); it is held to 32 characters by cutting the address at its last "/" that fits (leaving at least 10 characters of room); a link with no fragment is shortened in the middle with "…" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:746, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:69-94)

- [x] An `octo:` link opened from a sheet always opens this app's own package; any other link opens in the default handler; failures are ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:862-868)

## Sign in on another device sheet (as hosted by the Family screen)

The hand-over flow itself (codes, renewal, sealing, the new device's side) is covered by the hand-over part; this lists the sheet as drawn from the Family files and where Family links into it.

- [x] Opened by: Your login card "Sign in on another device", Devices "Sign in on another device", and Settings > Server and sync > "Sign in on another device" (which then shows the Family screen under it) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:160, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:261, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:235-238)
- [x] The sheet is hosted only on the Family screen; it is passed the server address, the account's username and its display name (or username) for the avatar (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:121, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:187-193)
- [x] Each opened sheet is keyed by its id; code renewal pauses while the app is stopped (out of sight) and resumes when started (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:163-180) (iPhone: renewal pauses while the app is in the background and picks up when it comes back)
- [x] Tall, non-scrolling sheet; dismissing it closes the hand-over (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:182) (iPhone: a system sheet; swiping it away closes the hand-over)
- [x] Away rule passed in: the link chooser may offer Anywhere unless the account's `away` ability is false (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:189)
- [x] Header: avatar initial, title "Sign in on another device", timer icon only while not finished or failed, subtitle "Handed over" when done, "Making a code" while waiting, "This code expired" when stale, else the countdown "Works once · expires in m:ss" ("Works once" without an expiry, "Works once · getting a new code" at 0) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:226-242, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:46-55) (iPhone: SF Symbol timer)
- [x] The countdown ticks on each whole second; under 30 seconds left (and not stale or ended) the subtitle and timer turn signal orange; at 30 seconds or less a screen reader hears "Thirty seconds left", at 0 "This code expired. Getting a new one." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:219-225, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:238, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:243, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:57-67) (iPhone: VoiceOver announcements posted as the words change)
- [x] Body by state: finished shows a card with a 32dp green check circle and the done words; a failed hand-over or a failed first code shows a card with an orange info icon, the words and a small glaze "Try again"; no code yet shows a placeholder square (side + 16dp) described "Making a code" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:245-253, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:323-336, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:803-815)
- [x] With a code: the chooser (question "Where will you use it?") when choosable, then the QR code labeled "QR code to sign in on another device" (test tag `family-sheet-qr`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:254-256, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:279-280, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:129)
- [x] A stale code is dimmed to 12% with "Still there?" (body) and a small accent "Make a new code" over it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:280-287, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:115-116)
- [x] Under the code, for 3,000 ms after each renewal, a pill (accent selected fill) with a 13dp check and "New code" replaces the caption and is announced; otherwise the caption "Scan with Octo on the new device, or with its camera" (centered); the swap cross-fades over 200ms (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:266-301, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:117, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:191, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:79)

Allow it only if this device is yours." ## Request a copy sheet

### Where it opens from

- [x] The sheet is hosted once by the main shell over every screen (above the share sheet, under server downloads and the message pill) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:346-350) (iPhone: a system sheet hosted at the app's root, over every screen)
- [x] It opens from a found-online song's menu item "Request a copy" (download icon, opens a page), offered only when Family is on and the account's add-to-library is Request; it sits in the keeping group after Download/Save (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:176, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:216-219, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenu.kt:172-174, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenu.kt:206, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:147) (iPhone: a system menu item in the song menu's keeping group)
- [x] It also opens from the Family screen's Saved rows ("Request") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:286, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:294)
- [x] Opening does nothing while the plan has not been read; the song id has its "find:" prefix removed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:151-154, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/Online.kt:17)
- [x] It shows while there is an ask and the plan is read; any dismissal closes it with no request sent (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:409-417, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:156-158)
- [x] It is a standard sheet: up to 70% of the screen tall, scrolling when it does not fit (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:414, design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/GlassSheet.kt:96) (iPhone: a system sheet sized to its content)

### Content

- [x] Title "Request a copy" (section style, primary, 20dp sides, heading) then the song or album title (bodySmall, secondary, 20dp sides, 4dp top and bottom) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:422-423)
- [x] Choices always in the order Best, FLAC, MP3, each a full-width radio row (20dp sides, 12dp top and bottom): name (body) over its detail (caption, muted), a 18dp check with the description "Picked" on the picked one (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:424-438, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:63-64)
- [x] Details: Best "The best copy Octo can find"; FLAC "Lossless FLAC only"; MP3 "A smaller MP3" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:63-68)
- [x] A quality above the plan's request quality (order Mp3 < Flac < Best) cannot be picked: its name is muted, its detail reads "Not on your plan", and the row does not respond (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:428, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:433, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:41-45, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:28-39)
- [x] The sheet opens with the best allowed quality picked; the pick resets each time the sheet opens for a new ask (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:421, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:74)
- [x] Under the choices (20dp sides, 8dp top and bottom, 4dp between): the library-full line in signal orange when full, the quota line in the secondary color when there is a weekly limit, then the approval line (muted) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:439-443)
- [x] Library-full line: "Your library is full (<used> of <limit> GB)" when the limit is above 0 and used bytes are at least limit x 1,000,000,000 ("Your library is full (10.2 of 10 GB)") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:81, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:143-151)
- [x] Quota line: none without a weekly limit; with all used "You have used all <limit> request(s) this week. Songs the family already has can still be added."; else "<left> of <limit> request(s) left this week" ("7 of 10 requests left this week"); left is the limit minus this week's requests, never below 0 (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:70-80)
- [x] Approval line: "Approved right away" when the plan auto-approves, else "Waits for approval" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:82)
- [x] The figures come from the plan as last read (they are not re-read when the sheet opens) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:413-415, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:140)
- [x] A large accent "Request" button, full width, 20dp sides, 8dp top and bottom; it is never disabled, not even when the week is used up or the library is full (the server still adds songs the family already has) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:444, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:54-56)

### Sending

- [x] "Request" closes the sheet at once, then calls `requestCopy?id=<id>&quality=<Mp3|Flac|Best>` (no kind parameter) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:180-187, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:236-244, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:268-285)
- [x] The answer's words: Done gives the outcome line ("Already in the family library", "Added from the family library", "Downloaded", "In your library"); Approved "Approved. Getting it now"; Pending "Requested. You'll hear when it's decided"; any other state its state line (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:386-392)
- [x] On success the made request goes to the top of the account's requests (replacing one with the same id), it is recorded as already told so no notification repeats it, and the words show as a message pill (no icon) and as the said line (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:240-244, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:73, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:185)
- [x] On a refusal the server's own words show (for example "You have used all 10 requests this week."), else "The request did not go through."; `working` is false afterwards (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:245-249, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:109-115)
- [x] After either result the whole family is read again (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:248-251)
- [x] With no client the request is not sent and the message pill repeats whatever said line was last set (if any) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:236-237, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:184-186)

## Save for members who cannot add directly (heart and menu)

- [x] Outside actions by plan: no family, or add-to-library Direct, keeps the app's usual add; Request turns add into "Save" and offers "Request a copy"; SaveOnly turns add into "Save" with no request (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRules.kt:8-25, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:50-57)
- [x] When add reads "Save", a found song's add button becomes a heart: liked icon (white, lit) when saved, outline (white 60%) when not; content description "Save", state "Saved" or "Not saved" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/AddToLibraryButton.kt:87-110, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/AddToLibraryButton.kt:126-130)
- [x] When add reads "Save", the song menu's Download item becomes "Save" (outline heart) or "Remove from Saved" (filled heart) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:180, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/menu/SongMenuPopup.kt:202-207) (iPhone: system menu items with SF Symbols heart and heart.fill)
- [x] A song counts as saved when it was saved in this app session or it is in the Saved list read from the server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:160)
- [x] Saving works only for found-online songs ("find:" ids): it calls `star?id=<id without find:>` or `unstar`, updates the local saved set, and shows "Saved <title>. Find it in Family, under Saved." or "Took <title> off Saved"; a refusal shows the server's words or "The server did not save it."; then the family is read again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:162-178)
