- [x] The app's own quality applies without a family or when this device is left to the app; otherwise the group "This app's quality" shows the note "Your account's choice applies now. These apply when this phone is left to the app." and streams ask for `format=raw` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:116-121, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:84-94, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Streams.kt:105-109)
- [x] When the app picks, a stream asks `format=raw` for Original, else `format=opus&maxBitRate=<kbps>` (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQuality.kt:90-94, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/AudioQualityTest.kt:24-29) (iPhone: MP3 at that bitrate in place of Opus, which iOS cannot play from Ogg)
- [x] Under a family limit the app's own Wi-Fi choices (home limit) and mobile choices (away limit) cannot be Original: it shows disabled with "Your family plan streams up to <limit> kbps" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:117-127, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/AudioQualityPage.kt:142-147)

## Background request notices

### Schedule

- [x] Checks are scheduled only for a server with Family on: one check now (unique work "family-notices-now", replacing any queued one) and a periodic check every 30 minutes (unique work "family-notices", kept if already scheduled), both needing a network connection (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:123-139) (iPhone: a background app refresh about every 30 minutes, and one check at once)
- [x] A server without Family, or no server, cancels both (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:141-146)
- [x] A check waits up to 20,000 ms for the saved sign-in to load; not signed in, or no Family, ends quietly (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:107-112)
- [x] An unreachable server makes the check retry later; any other server error ends it quietly (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:113-120) (iPhone: a failed check ends quietly and the next background refresh tries again)

### What one check does

- [x] Calls `getFamily`, then the account's own `getFamilyRequests`, and only when the account approves, `getFamilyRequests?all=true&state=Pending` for the waiting count (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:74-82)
- [x] The memory of what was told is kept per account (the kept server's id) in a store named "family_notices" under the key "told@<account>", as JSON `{"told": {<request id>: <state name>}, "waitingTold": <count>, "started": <bool>}`; unreadable memory starts fresh; a failed save is logged and ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNoticeStore.kt:20-41, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:15-24) (iPhone: kept in the app's defaults under family_notices.told@<account>, same JSON)
- [x] The first check for an account (not started) tells nothing about requests, only marks the starting point; it can still tell a manager's waiting count (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:31-42, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:280-285)
- [x] After the first check, a request is told when its state is Approved, Declined, Done or Failed and differs from the state last told for it (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:26-27, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:37-42)
- [x] After each check the memory becomes exactly the current requests' states (requests no longer listed are forgotten), the current waiting count, and started (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:47-52)
- [x] A waiting notice is told only when the count is above 0 and above the count last told; the same or a lower count tells nothing, and a rise after a drop tells again (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:43-46, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:306-316)
- [x] A request answered in the Request a copy sheet is written into the memory as already told for that state (skipped when the account id is empty) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNoticeStore.kt:43-47, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:55-57)
- [x] Each account keeps its own memory: a second account on the phone starts from its own first check (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNoticesTest.kt:80-81)

### Notice words

- [x] "<what>" is the request's "<title> · <artist>" (blank parts left out, the target id when both are blank) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:59-60, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyWords.kt:186-188)
- [x] Approved: title "Approved", text "<what> is on its way to your library." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:62)
- [x] Declined: title "Declined", text "<what>. \"<note>\"" when the manager left a note, else "<what>" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:63-67)
- [x] Done: title "Added", text "<what>. <outcome line>." ("Song · Artist. Added from the family library.") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:68)
- [x] Failed: title "Could not get it", text "<what>. <failure text>" or "<what>. Ask for it again later." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:69)
- [x] Waiting (managers who approve): key "family-waiting", title "<n> request(s) waiting", text "Open Family to approve or decline." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:29, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:43-46)

### Posting

- [x] Nothing posts without notification permission (Android 13+) or with no notices (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:63-64)
- [x] Channel id "family", name "Family", default importance, description "Requests approved, declined or added, and requests waiting for you", created once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:54, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:65-72) (iPhone: iOS has no channels; notices share the thread family and need Octo's notification permission)
- [x] Each notice: the listeners small icon, its title and text, expanded big text, auto-cancel on tap, tapping opens the app at the Family screen (`OPEN_FAMILY`, single top) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:73-88) (iPhone: a local notification with title and text; tapping it opens Family)
- [x] Each notice is posted under its key (the request id, or "family-waiting"), so newer news about the same request or the waiting count replaces the earlier notification rather than adding one (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:89-93, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyNotices.kt:10-13)

## Family data reading (FamilyModel) and server calls

### Reading

- [x] `plan()` returns the cached plan when already read; otherwise reads `getFamily` (a failure answers null and sets no problem), then this account's devices (a failure is ignored), and caches both (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:174-185, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt:215-223)
- [x] A full read: `getFamily` first (the screen updates right away), then in parallel the account's requests, its devices, the waiting inbox (approvers only), the starred list and the login; requests, devices and inbox failures fail the whole read (problem set, earlier values kept); starred and login failures are tolerated (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:205-232)
- [x] A successful `getFamily` clears the problem (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:212-214)
- [x] Unknown enum values from a newer server read as the defaults (unknown add-to-library reads Direct, unknown quality Best, unknown place Home) and an unknown role keeps its name with no known role (subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt:109-120, subsonic/src/test/resources/fixtures/getFamilyFuture.json:1-26)
- [x] Defaults when a field is missing: abilities add Direct, request quality Best, no auto-approve, all limits 0 (none), away allowed, downloads, offline, share, import, family playlist editing and instant-from-family on, clean only, manage and approve off; role Unmanaged for me and Member for a member; device quality mode Account (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:90-149, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:218-232)

### Subsonic calls (all signed like every Subsonic call, JSON)

- [x] `getFamily` answers `family` with `me` and, for managers only, `manager` (`members`, `pendingRequests`, `liveStreams`) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:163-177, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:249-252)
- [x] `getFamilyRequests` answers `familyRequests.request` (empty when missing); `all=true` only for every member's, `state=<StateName>` only when filtering (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:254-266)
- [x] `requestCopy` (`id`, `quality`, optional `kind`), `cancelFamilyRequest` (`id`) and `decideFamilyRequest` (`id`, `approve`, optional trimmed `note`) answer `familyRequest` (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:268-302)
- [x] `getFamilyDevices` (optional `all=true`) answers `familyDevices.device`; `forgetFamilyDevice` (`id`) answers only ok or an error (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:304-317)
- [x] `getFamilyLogin` answers `familyLogin` (`username`, `servers.anywhere`, `servers.home`, `anywhereAvailable`, `awayAllowed`) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:237-247, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:319-320)
- [x] `setFamilyQuality` answers `quality` (falls back to the values sent when the key is missing); `setFamilyDeviceQuality` answers `device` (falls back to the id and mode sent) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:322-338)
- [x] `removeFromMyLibrary` (`id`) answers only ok or an error; the server refuses songs of the shared library (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:340-342)
- [x] `createFamilyPlaylist` (`name` trimmed, `approveAdditions=true` only when asked) exists in the client but no phone screen calls it (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:344-355)

### Family page calls (`/api/family`)

- [x] Every family page call goes to `<server base>/api/family/<path>` (a server path such as `/octo` is kept), carries the same Subsonic sign-in query as other calls (`u`, `t`, `s`, `v`, `c`; never `p`), and the headers `X-Octo-Family: 1` and `Accept: application/json`; bodies are JSON (`{}` when none) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:59-69, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:124-129, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:85-90, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:647, subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWebTest.kt:106-132)
- [x] Error words: the server's JSON `message` (or `error`) when given; otherwise 401/403 "The server did not take this sign-in for that.", 404 "This server has no family page. Update Octo on the server.", 410 "That invite has been used or has expired. Ask for a new one.", other "The server said no (HTTP <status>)."; no connection is "unreachable" (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:139-156)
- [x] Add member answer fields: `member`, `inviteLink`, optional `links`, `inviteDays` (default 7), `anywhereAvailable` (default true), `awayAllowed` (default true), `homeOnly` (default false), `publicUrl`; new invite and reset answer the same minus `member`, plus `expires` (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:24-54)
- [x] An empty answer body reads as an empty object (defaults) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:121-144)

### Secrets never printed

- [x] Links, invites and the hand-over sheet never print their tokens or keys in logs (`toString` hides them) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:158-161, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:33, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:168, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:36, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:53)

## Links into the join part (QR scanner and deep links)

- [x] The camera scanner lives in the family files but opens from the sign-in screen (join part); found family links start the join (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:131, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:67-71)
- [x] Scanner: full screen black, back camera, latest frame only, the brightness plane decoded (also inverted); a 240dp square guide with a 2dp white 80% border and 20dp corners in the center (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:58-121, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt:33-58) (iPhone: AVFoundation's back camera and Vision's QR reader, latest frame only, also with light and dark swapped)
- [x] The camera permission is asked on open; the line at the bottom reads, in priority: a camera start failure "The camera did not start. Type the code instead."; refused "Octo can't use the camera. Allow it in the phone's settings, or type the code."; not yet allowed "Allow the camera to scan the code."; a non-family code seen "That QR code is not a family link. Show the one from Devices or an invite."; else "Hold the family QR code inside the square." (body, centered), with a medium glaze "Cancel" under it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:74-137)
- [x] The first family link found closes the scanner with that link; later frames are ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:99-110)
- [x] The app opens `octo://handover?...`, `octo://join?...` and `octo://signin?...` links itself (browsable deep links) (app/src/androidTest/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyLinkFilterTest.kt:12-34) (iPhone: the octo URL scheme opens the app through onOpenURL)

## Family links: how the app is opened with one

### Deep link registration and activity handling

- [x] The camera permission is declared so the join screen can scan a family QR code, and the camera hardware is declared not required, so a phone without a camera still installs Octo (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:9-12) (iPhone: the camera usage line is declared; no camera is required to install)
- [x] The main activity is `singleTop`, so a family link tapped while Octo is open arrives as a new intent on the running activity instead of a second copy (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:58-61) (iPhone: one scene; a link tapped while Octo is open reaches the running app)
- [x] The app registers the custom scheme `octo` with exactly three hosts as VIEW + DEFAULT + BROWSABLE links: `octo://join?server=...&invite=...` (invite), `octo://signin?server=...&username=...` (sign-in to fill in), `octo://handover?t=...&k=...&s=...&h=...` (sign-in handed over from another device) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:71-83) (iPhone: the octo URL scheme is registered; only join, signin and handover read as family links)
- [x] The family web page also hands the hand-over on as an Android `intent://handover?...#Intent;scheme=octo;...;end` link, which the browser turns into the `octo://handover` link the filter catches (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:73-75) (iPhone: intent:// is an Android browser form; its text still reads as a hand-over when pasted or scanned)
- [x] No https App Link / universal link is registered: https family links (`<server>/family/join#...`, `<server>/family/signin#...`) open the browser, never the app directly; the app only reads them when scanned, pasted, or found on the clipboard (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:76-83)
- [x] On first launch (no saved instance state) and on every new intent, the activity checks the intent for a family link (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:61-65, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:70-75) (iPhone: every link opened with the app arrives through onOpenURL)
- [x] An intent relaunched from Recents (flag FLAG_ACTIVITY_LAUNCHED_FROM_HISTORY) is ignored, so reopening Octo from Recents never replays an old family link (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:98) (iPhone: iOS never hands an opened link to the app a second time)
- [x] An ACTION_VIEW intent's data string is run through `parseFamilyLink`; when it is a family link the app is asked to open the join form with it (`FamilyOpen.Join(link)`); when it is not a family link nothing happens beyond the app opening (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:101-102)
- [x] The family notice action (`FamilyNotifier.OPEN_FAMILY`) opens the Family screen instead (`FamilyOpen.Family`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:100) (iPhone: a tapped notice opens Family through the notification delegate)

### Clipboard offer

- [x] Every time the activity gains window focus it reads the clipboard's first item as text (failures read as null) and offers it to the family hub (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:87-92) (iPhone: read each time the scene becomes active, after iOS's own pattern check)
- [x] Clipboard text is trimmed; empty text is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:95)
- [x] The exact same trimmed text that was already offered is not offered again (memory is in-process only; a fresh process can offer it again) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:90, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:96)
- [x] Text that is not a family link is ignored and is NOT remembered as offered (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:97)
- [x] A family link on the clipboard shows a feedback toast with the action "Use it": the words are "A family invite is on the clipboard" for an invite link, and "A family sign-in link is on the clipboard" for any other family link (sign-in prefill or hand-over) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:98-100)
- [x] Tapping "Use it" opens the join form with that link, exactly as a tapped link would (`FamilyOpen.Join(link)`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:100)

### Routing to the join form

- [x] A pending family open is consumed once: the shell clears it (`opened()`), selects the last tab (Settings) and pushes a new route onto that tab's stack (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:169-181, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:76-87) (iPhone: the link opens the sign-in form as a full screen cover, once)
- [x] A family link is pushed as `FamilyJoinRoute(link)` where the link is re-written into the app's own `octo://` form with `familyAppLink`; the route is serializable (survives process restore) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:178, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/Routes.kt:83-84) (iPhone: the cover is given the link in its octo:// form)
- [x] Because the route stores the `octo://` form, a hand-over link that came as an https link loses its `base` (the address the link was opened on): after the round trip the token is redeemed on the link's `s` server address instead (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:178, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:131-136, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:101-102)
- [x] The route shows the sign-in screen with `join = parseFamilyLink(route.link)` and no form or editing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:405)
- [x] The sign-in screen starts the join whenever its `join` link is non-null (a `LaunchedEffect` keyed on the link, so re-entering composition with the same link starts it again) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:111-117)

## Family link formats and parsing

### Kinds of link

- [x] Every family link carries `server` (the address that works from anywhere, which may carry a path such as `https://example.com/octo`) and an optional `home` (the server's home network address) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:19-27)
- [x] Invite link: fields `server`, `token`, `home`; its text form never prints the token (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:29-31)
- [x] Sign-in prefill link: fields `server`, `username` (default ""), `home`; the person types the password (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:33-35)
- [x] Hand-over link: fields `base` (where the token is redeemed), `token`, `key`, `server`, `home`, `username` (optional); its text form never prints token or key (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:37-51)

### Parsing rules (`parseFamilyLink`)

- [x] Leading and trailing whitespace is ignored before parsing, so a pasted link works (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:55-56)
- [x] Prefix match is case-insensitive: `octo://join`, `octo://signin`, `octo://handover`, `intent://handover`, `https://`, `http://`; anything else is not a family link (null) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:57-65)
- [x] Query parameters are read from the text after the first `?` and before the first `#`; for web links the parameters come from the fragment after the first `#` (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:60-62, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:69, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:82)
- [x] Parameters split on `&`; a part with no `=` or with `=` as its first character is dropped; values are URL-decoded (a plus reads as a space; an undecodable value is kept as typed); a repeated name keeps the last value (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:111-114, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:156-157)
- [x] `octo://join`: needs non-blank `server` and non-blank `invite` (both trimmed), else null; `server` is kept as given (trimmed, NOT normalized); `home` is read as an address (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:68-73)
- [x] `octo://signin`: needs `server` that normalizes to an address, else null (e.g. `octo://signin?username=alex` is null); `username` trimmed, empty when missing; `home` read as an address (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:75-79, subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt:333-342)
- [x] `octo://handover` and `intent://handover`: need non-blank `t` (token) and `k` (key) and a `s` (server) that normalizes to an address; with any of them missing it is not a link; `base` is the server; `h` is the home address; `u` is the username when non-blank (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:60-62, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:98-103)
- [x] An `intent://handover?...#Intent;scheme=octo;package=...;S.browser_fallback_url=...;end` text reads the same as `octo://handover` (everything after `#` is ignored) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:61-62, subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt:362-366)
- [x] https/http web link: must have a non-empty fragment after `#`, else null (so `https://music.example.com/family/signin` with no fragment is not a link) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:82-83)
- [x] Web link path (empty segments dropped) must have at least 2 segments and the second-to-last must be exactly `family` (case-sensitive) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:84-86)
- [x] Web link base is the same scheme, host and port with the path segments before `/family/<kind>` kept, query dropped, no trailing slash (e.g. `https://example.com/octo/family/join#...` gives base `https://example.com/octo`) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:87)
- [x] Web link last segment `join` gives an invite when the fragment has a non-blank `invite`, with `server` = base and `home` from `home`; otherwise null (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:89-90)
- [x] Web link last segment `signin` gives a hand-over from the fragment's `t`, `k`, `s`, `h`, `u`, with `base` = the web link's base; a missing or unreadable `s` falls back to the base as the server (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:91, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:101-102)
- [x] Any other last segment (e.g. `/family/other#t=1&k=2`) is not a link; `/family/signin#t=tok_1` without a key is not a link (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:92, subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt:371-374)
- [x] An address inside a link (`s`, `h`, `home`, signin `server`) is normalized: scheme added as `http://` when missing, a trailing `rest` or `app` path segment dropped, user/password/query/fragment dropped, trailing slash removed; a value that is not an address becomes no address (a `home` like `://` or empty is no home) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:105-109, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/ServerUrl.kt:8-25, subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt:471-474)

### Link builders

- [x] Invite web link: `<server trimmed, no trailing slash>/family/join#invite=<token encoded>` plus `&home=<home encoded>` when home is non-blank (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:117-118, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:138)
- [x] Hand-over web link: `<base trimmed, no trailing slash>/family/signin#t=<token>&k=<key>&s=<server, no trailing slash>`, then `&h=<home, no trailing slash>` when home is non-blank, then `&u=<username trimmed>` when non-blank, each value encoded (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:122-128)
- [x] App forms: invite `octo://join?server=<enc>&invite=<enc>[&home=<enc>]`; prefill `octo://signin?server=<enc>[&home=<enc>][&username=<enc>]` (username only when not blank); hand-over `octo://handover?` followed by the same parameters as the web fragment (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:131-136)
- [x] Encoding is RFC 3986: A-Z, a-z, 0-9, `-`, `.`, `_`, `~` stay; every other UTF-8 byte becomes `%XX` with upper-case hex (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:142-153, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:159)
- [x] Round trip holds for every kind: `parseFamilyLink(familyAppLink(link)) == link` for invite and prefill, and the hand-over app form equals `octo://handover?` + the web fragment (subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt:333-375, subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt:454-475)

## Family QR scanner (QrScanner)

### Opening and closing

- [x] The scanner is drawn full screen over the whole sign-in screen (over the form and its back button) while `scanning` is true (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:127-132)
- [x] The scanner's background is solid black (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:83)
- [x] A "Cancel" button (GlazeButton, Medium size) sits at the bottom centre under the status line; it sets `scanning = false` and leaves the join card as it was (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:123-137, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:131)
- [x] The bottom column is inside system bar padding plus 24dp padding, centred, 12dp between the line and Cancel; the line is body text, primary colour, centred (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:123-135)
- [x] The scanner registers no back handler of its own (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:70-139)
- [x] A family link found closes the scanner and starts the join with that link (`startJoin(link)` sets `scanning = false`), with the link object passed directly (its `base` kept) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:131, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:201-202)

### Camera permission

- [x] When opened, the scanner checks whether camera permission is already granted (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:74)
- [x] When not granted, it asks for the camera permission once, as soon as it appears (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:78-82)
- [x] The answer sets `allowed` to the result and marks that it was asked (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:78-81)
- [x] While the permission is not granted and not yet answered, the line reads "Allow the camera to scan the code." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:131)
- [x] After a denial, the line reads "Octo can't use the camera. Allow it in the phone's settings, or type the code." and no camera preview or square is shown; there is no button to ask again or open settings, only Cancel (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:84, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:130, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:136)

### Camera and frame reading

- [x] With permission, a full-screen camera preview of the back camera runs, bound to the screen's lifecycle; any previous camera use is unbound first (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:84-119) (iPhone: AVFoundation's back camera preview full screen, stopped when the scanner goes)
- [x] Frames are analysed on one background thread, keeping only the latest frame (older frames dropped while one is being read); the thread is shut down when the scanner leaves (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:85-87, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:96-99) (iPhone: one serial queue, late frames dropped by AVFoundation)
- [x] Each frame's brightness plane is read (row stride honoured, whatever the frame's rotation) and decoded as a QR code (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:60-65) (iPhone: each frame read by Vision's QR reader, whatever its rotation)
- [x] A frame with no readable QR code changes nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:102)
- [x] After a family link is found, every later frame is skipped (no second hit) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:86, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:101, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:105)
- [x] The found link is handed back on the main thread (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:106)
- [x] A QR code whose text is not a family link sets the line to "That QR code is not a family link. Show the one from Devices or an invite." and scanning goes on; this line stays (it does not reset) until a family link is read (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:107-109, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:132)
- [x] Any failure starting the camera sets the line to "The camera did not start. Type the code instead." (takes priority over every other line) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:114, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:129)
- [x] With the camera running and nothing wrong, the line reads "Hold the family QR code inside the square." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:133)
- [x] Line priority is: camera problem, then denied, then not yet allowed, then not-a-family-link, then the hold line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:128-134)
- [x] A centred 240dp square guide is drawn over the preview: 2dp border, white at 80% opacity, 20dp rounded corners (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:120-121)
- [x] There is no field to type a code; the only typed path is pasting a family link into the address field (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:222-224)

### QR decode rules (FamilyQr)

- [x] Decoding looks only for QR codes, tries harder, and reads the text as UTF-8 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQrViews.kt:64, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt:33-37) (iPhone: Vision reads QR codes only, as UTF-8 text)
- [x] A frame is decoded as it is and, when that finds nothing, again with light and dark swapped, so a light-on-dark code reads too (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt:49-50)
- [x] A decode that finds no code or fails reading answers nothing (no error shown) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt:52-58)
- [x] The QR text is accepted only when `parseFamilyLink` reads it as an invite, a sign-in prefill or a hand-over (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt:61)
- [x] Codes the app draws are made on the device with medium (M) error correction, as a square matrix of dark and light modules (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt:18-31) (iPhone: Core Image makes the codes the app draws, at level M)
- [x] A drawn code (4 px a module, 4 modules of light border) reads back to the same link, inverted reads too, a grey picture reads as nothing, and a padded luminance plane reads (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:202-224) (iPhone: Apple's own encoder and reader stand in for ZXing; neither runs on Linux, so no unit test)

## Sign-in screen in join mode

### Starting, switching and stopping a join

- [x] `startJoin()` with no link closes the scanner, clears the error and turns join mode on without touching any other field (a held invite or hand-over stays) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:201-207)
- [x] `startJoin(link)` closes the scanner and clears the error (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:202-203)
- [x] `startJoin(link)` types the link's server into the address field as if pasted: a scheme in it sets the scheme button and stops the scheme following the address; without a scheme the scheme follows the address (http for a home address, https otherwise) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:208, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:177-188, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/ServerScheme.kt:14-27)
- [x] A link's home address replaces the Advanced "Home network address" field; a link without a home leaves that field as it was; the Advanced section is not opened by it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:209-211)
- [x] Every `startJoin(link)` first forgets any earlier sign-up result, invite, hand-over and hand-over step (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:212-215)
- [x] An invite link shows the sign-up card (join mode on, invite held) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:217-220)
- [x] A hand-over link turns join mode on, holds the link, and starts receiving at once (no button press) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:221-225)
- [x] A sign-in prefill link turns join mode off (back to the ordinary password form), fills the username only when the link's username is not blank (otherwise keeps what was there), empties the password, and switches "Sign in with an API key" off (Legacy sign-in is left as it was) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:226-231)
- [x] `stopJoin` ("Sign in with a password") turns join mode off and clears invite, hand-over, hand-over step and error; it does not cancel a hand-over that is still running, which can still finish and sign in (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:235-241)
- [x] The address field accepts a family link: every change is first tried as a family link; when it parses, the join starts with it and the pasted text is never put in the field (the field shows the link's server instead) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:224, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:195-199)
- [x] The parse runs on every keystroke, so typing that becomes a valid family link part way (e.g. `octo://join?server=a&invite=t`) starts the join at that keystroke (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:224)
- [x] Only the address field takes a pasted family link; the username and password fields do not (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:242-262)
- [x] Once signed in by any join path the screen shows the notice (when there is one) as a done toast and goes back (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:120-125)

### Heading and line under it

- [x] With no form heading (the join route and the plain sign-in screen), the big word "Octo" shows in display style (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:185-186)
- [x] The line under "Octo" (body small, secondary colour, 4dp above) reads, in this priority: "Sign up for your family's server" while an invite is held (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:187-189)
- [x] ... "Signing in with your other device's login" while a hand-over is held (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:190)

### Password strength words

- [x] Empty password: no line (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:37)
- [x] Fewer than 8 characters: "Too short: at least 8 characters" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:38)
- [x] Kinds counted: has a lower-case letter, has an upper-case letter, has a digit, has a character that is neither letter nor digit (0 to 4) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:39)
- [x] 16 or more characters, or 12 or more with at least 3 kinds: "Strong" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:41)
- [x] Otherwise 12 or more characters, or at least 3 kinds: "Good. Longer is stronger." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:42)
- [x] Otherwise: "Weak: make it longer, or mix in numbers and symbols" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:43)

## Signing up from an invite

### The sign-up call

- [x] Sign up does nothing when busy, when something is missing, or when the address field is not an address (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:304-305)
- [x] It sets busy and clears the error before starting (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:306-307)
- [x] The address used is the address field with its scheme (so editing the field changes where the sign-up goes), not the link's original text (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:304, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:169)
- [x] The home address used is the Advanced home field, trimmed, normalized, or none when blank (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:311)
- [x] A one-off client is built for the address with the Advanced custom headers, the certificates trusted so far on this form (by host), the chosen client certificate, 20 second connect and call timeouts, and the user agent `Octo/<app version> (Android)` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:312, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ServerClients.kt:25-37) (iPhone: the trust session with its certificates, the custom headers, 20 second calls and the user agent Octo/<version> (iOS))
- [x] Sign-up call: POST `<address>/api/family/join`, JSON body `{"token":<invite token>,"password":<password>,"displayName":<name trimmed>}`, headers `X-Octo-Family: 1` and `Accept: application/json`, with no Subsonic sign-in (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:87-88, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:121-131, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:62)
- [x] The page's cookies from the answer are kept only for that one sign-up object (a later cookie with the same name replaces the earlier) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:65-83)
- [x] The answer is `{"username": "...", "role": "..."}`; the username is what this phone signs in with, with the password the person chose (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:20-22, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:62-63)
- [x] Nothing else is called for the sign-up: no pairing, no device registration (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt:243-244)

### Home address fallback

- [x] When the address can't be reached at all (no connection) or its certificate failed, and the link (or Advanced field) named a home address different from the address, the sign-up is tried again at the home address (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:71-75)
- [x] It is never retried at home after the server turned the invite down (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:73-74)
- [x] A sign-up at home that works wins; a certificate question from home is asked when the first failure was not a certificate question; otherwise the first answer stands (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:76-80)

### Sign-up errors (exact words)

- [x] Server answers with a blank username: "The server did not answer with a username. Ask for a new invite." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:84-85)
- [x] HTTP 401 or 403: the server's own `message` (or `error`) field when it gives one, else "The server did not take this sign-in for that." (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:147-151, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:86-87)
- [x] HTTP 404: "That server has no family sign-up. Check the address, or update Octo on the server." (always, whatever the server said) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:90-91, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:152)
- [x] HTTP 410: the server's own words, else "That invite has been used or has expired. Ask for a new one." (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:153, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:92-93)
- [x] Any other HTTP error: the server's own words, else "The server said no (HTTP <status>)." (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:154, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:92-93)
- [x] Not reached (network error): "Octo can't reach that address. Check it and try again." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:88-89)
- [x] A TLS failure becomes a certificate question about the address's host; when the rejected certificate is known the trust popup opens with the host and its SHA-256 fingerprint; when not, the error reads "This server's certificate is not trusted." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:89, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:320-325)
- [x] Every failure turns busy off and shows its words in the join error line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:326-330)
- [x] Only Subsonic-style failures are turned into words; any other exception from the sign-up (for example a 200 answer that is not JSON) is not caught by this step (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:83-94) (iPhone: a 200 answer that is not JSON reads as That address did not answer like an Octo server.)

### Signing in after sign-up, and what is kept

- [x] An account made by the invite is remembered until signed in, so a retry (after a certificate question or a failed sign-in) signs in with it without signing up again (an invite works once); the remembered password is the one typed at sign-up, even if the fields change later (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:77-79, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:314-317)
- [x] The sign-in then sent is: the address field's address, the username the server answered, the chosen password, Token sign-in, the Advanced home address, custom headers, trusted certificates and client certificate; no name for the server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:332-341)
- [x] The sign-in goes through the same path as a typed sign-in: the new server becomes the one in use (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:342, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:158-166)
- [x] On success: the notice is kept, both password fields are emptied, the invite and remembered account are forgotten, and the screen closes (the name field is not emptied) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:344-351)
- [x] On a certificate question the trust popup opens; on any other failure the error line shows the sign-in's own words (e.g. "Wrong username or password.", "Couldn't reach the server. Check the address, and that you're on the right network.") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:352-353, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:6-29)
- [x] Busy turns off at the end of every path (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:355)

### Certificate questions during a join

- [x] Trusting in the popup pins that certificate's fingerprint for that host (host lower-cased), closes the popup, and in join mode runs Sign up again (outside join mode it submits the ordinary form) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:561-566, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/connection/Certificates.kt:31)
- [x] Trusting while a hand-over (not an invite) is held does not retry anything: Sign up returns at once because no invite is held; "Try again" is then the way on (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:565, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:305)
- [x] Not trusting closes the popup with the error "Not connected. The server's certificate wasn't trusted." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:568-571)

## Receiving a hand-over (the new device)

### Starting the receive

- [x] Receive does nothing when no hand-over is held or when already busy (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:255-256)
- [x] A hand-over link whose base is not an address is dropped silently by the screen (no error, no busy) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:257)
- [x] Receive sets busy and clears the error (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:258-259)
- [x] The receive uses a one-off client for the link's base with no headers, no trusted certificates and no client certificate (20 second timeouts, `Octo/<version> (Android)` user agent), so a server with a certificate the phone does not trust fails as not reached (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:261, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/ServerClients.kt:25-37) (iPhone: an ephemeral session with no saved trust, 20 second calls and the user agent Octo/<version> (iOS))
- [x] The device name sent is the phone's own name from its settings, else "<Maker> <Model>" (just the model when it already starts with the maker; "Android phone" when nothing is known) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:263, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/DeviceIds.kt:40-42, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/DeviceIds.kt:61-75) (iPhone: the phone's name as iOS gives it to apps, iPhone unless Apple grants the device name entitlement)
- [x] The platform sent is the wire name of the app's platform: "Android" on Android; the iPhone app's value is "iOS" (others "Windows", "macOS", "Linux") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:263, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:57-64)
- [x] The receive runs off the main thread; each step's words update the card as they come (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:262-264)
- [x] Leaving the sign-in screen cancels a wait still in progress (it runs in the screen's own scope) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:260)

### Redeem and wait

- [x] A base that is not an address answers "This sign-in link has no server in it. Scan the QR code again." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:124)
- [x] The first step shown is "Waiting for your other device" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:99, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:125)
- [x] Redeem call: GET `<base>/rest/redeemFamilySignIn?v=1.16.1&c=Octo&f=json&token=<token>&deviceName=<device name trimmed>&platform=<platform>` with no sign-in of any kind (no u, t, s, p, apiKey); the answer's `familySignInRedeemed.id` is the id to wait on (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:408-412, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:418-443)
- [x] The key and anything after `#` are never sent to the server (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:202-223)
- [x] Redeem not reached: "Octo can't reach the server. Check this device is online, then try again." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:104, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:128-129)
- [x] Redeem answered with Subsonic error 70, error 40 or 44, or HTTP 404: "This sign-in link expired. Make a new one on your other device." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:103, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:130-133)
- [x] Any other redeem failure shows that failure's own message as is (for example "HTTP 500 from redeemFamilySignIn", a Subsonic error's message, or "The server did not answer like a Subsonic server"), falling back to the expired words (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:132-133, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:445-453, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:706-720)
- [x] Status call: GET `<base>/rest/getFamilySignInRedeem?v=1.16.1&c=Octo&f=json&id=<id>` with no sign-in; answer `familySignInRedeem` = `{state: Waiting|Allowed|Denied|Expired, box?}` (a missing state reads as Waiting) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:381-388, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:414-416)
- [x] The status is asked right after redeeming, then every 1.5 seconds (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:121, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:135-163)
- [x] The wait is counted by adding 1.5 s per poll (network time is not counted) up to 130 seconds: at most 87 polls, then "This sign-in link expired. Make a new one on your other device." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:122, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:135-164)
- [x] A poll that can't reach the server is treated as still waiting (the wait goes on) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:139-140)
- [x] Any other poll failure ends with the expired words (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:141-142)
- [x] Denied: "Your other device said no. Nothing was shared." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:102, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:157)
- [x] Expired: "This sign-in link expired. Make a new one on your other device." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:158)
- [x] Allowed without a box yet: the step becomes "Allowed, signing in" and the wait goes on (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:100, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:155)
- [x] Allowed with a box: the step becomes "Allowed, signing in" and the box is opened with the link's key; a box that won't open ends with the opening error's words (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:145-154)
- [x] The steps seen on a normal run are exactly "Waiting for your other device" then "Allowed, signing in" (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:212)

### After the receive

- [x] A refusal clears the step (the row goes back to "Ready to sign in to ..."), shows the refusal words in the error line and turns busy off, so "Try again" appears (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:265-270)
- [x] "Try again" redeems the same token again (a used token then answers the expired words) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInScreen.kt:389-393, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:254-264)
- [x] The received sign-in is signed in like a typed one: address = the box's server, username, secret, way of signing in from the box's mode name (Token, LegacyPassword or ApiKey; anything else reads as Token), home = the box's home or none; no headers, no trusted certificates, no client certificate, no name (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:271-282)
- [x] The link's own `s` and `h` addresses are not what is kept: the box's server and home are (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:272-277)
- [x] On success: the step becomes "Signed in", the notice is kept, the hand-over is forgotten, and the screen closes showing the notice (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:284-289, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:101)
- [x] On a certificate question the trust popup opens and the step stays as it was ("Allowed, signing in") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:290)
- [x] On any other failure the step is cleared and the sign-in's own words show (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:291-294)
- [x] Busy turns off at the end (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:296)
- [x] A new hand-over link opened while one is still being received replaces the held link, but the new receive does not start (busy); the running one finishes with the old link (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:221-225, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/signin/SignInViewModel.kt:256)

### What is stored on the new device (join and hand-over alike)

- [x] The address is normalized; a bad one fails with "That doesn't look like a server address."; a bad home with "The home network address doesn't look like a server address."; a home equal to the main address is dropped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:250-252, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/UserMessages.kt:22-23)
- [x] The main address is pinged first; when it can't connect (not a certificate failure) and there is a home address, the home address is pinged instead (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:276-285, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:587-593)
- [x] A home address with a certificate the phone does not trust is asked about during this sign-in (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:288-296)
- [x] With an API key the server is asked whose key it is, and that name becomes the username (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:300-303)
- [x] The same address and username already kept is brought up to date (its id and its name kept); another username on the same server gets a new id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:256-260, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:309-314, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:326)
- [x] Kept: the server address, username, the secret sealed in the vault, server type and version, whether it is Octo, its extensions, and the connection (home, way of signing in, headers, pinned certificates, client certificate) sealed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:315-327) (iPhone: the secret, headers and certificates kept in the iOS keychain)
- [x] The new server becomes the one in use; a different server in use before first puts its things away (what was playing counts for it, its queue is kept) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:336-342, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionHandoff.kt:6-21)
- [x] The notice is null when no server was in use before; otherwise "Now on <new>." or, when music had to stop, "Now on <new>. The music from <old> stopped, and its queue is kept for when you come back." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:158-166, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt:61-65)

## The sealed box (shared by both sides)

- [x] The travelling sign-in is JSON with keys `username`, `secret`, `mode` (default "Token"), `server`, `home` (default null), `displayName` (default ""); fields equal to their default are left out when sealing; unknown keys are ignored when opening (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:31-39, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:57)
- [x] The sign-in's text form never includes the secret (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:40-41)
- [x] Cipher: XChaCha20-Poly1305 with a 32 byte key, a fresh random 24 byte nonce per seal, a 128 bit tag, and associated data the UTF-8 bytes of "octo-family-signin-1" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:48-56, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:64-72)
- [x] A new key is 32 random bytes as URL-safe base64 without padding (43 characters) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:58, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:61-62, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:63-64)
- [x] The box is URL-safe base64 without padding of nonce followed by ciphertext with tag; sealing the same sign-in twice never gives the same box (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:71, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:68-69)
- [x] Opening trims the box; a box that is not base64 or is 40 bytes or shorter fails with "The sign-in from your other device is damaged." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:75-76)
- [x] A wrong key or a changed box fails with "This sign-in could not be opened here. Make a new QR code on your other device." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:82-86)
- [x] Opened bytes that are not the sign-in JSON fail with "The sign-in from your other device is damaged." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:87-88)
- [x] A key (trimmed) that is not base64 or not exactly 32 bytes fails with "This sign-in link is not complete. Scan the QR code again." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt:91-95)

## Giving a hand-over (the signed-in device): "Sign in on another device"

### Entry points

- [x] Family screen, Your login card: an accent button "Sign in on another device" (Small, accent fill, QR code icon) opens the hand-over sheet; beside it "Open in Octo" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:456-459, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:152-164)
- [x] Family screen, Devices section: under "Sign out everywhere" and its line, a row with "Change password" and a GlazeButton "Sign in on another device" (Small, QR code icon) that opens the hand-over sheet (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:255-264)
- [x] The sheet is hosted by the Family screen with the server address in use and the family username (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:121)
- [x] Another account or server coming into use closes any open hand-over sheet (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:159-172)

### Code lifecycle

- [x] Opening makes a new sheet with its own number (each opening counts up), the opening time and a key made on this device, then asks for a code (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:116-122)
- [x] Any answer for a sheet number that is no longer the open one is dropped (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:22-25, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:157, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:196-199)
- [x] Asking for a code stops any running watch, sets loading and clears the error (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:194-197)
- [x] Code call: GET `/rest/startFamilySignIn` signed as every Subsonic call; answer `familySignIn` = `{token, expires (ISO time), links {anywhere, home}, servers {anywhere, home}, anywhereAvailable (default true)}` (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:357-369, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:390-393)
- [x] Each code gets a fresh key made with it (the key made at opening is replaced) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:208-216, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:204)
- [x] No session, or any Subsonic failure (including a missing `familySignIn`), gives the sheet error "Couldn't make a code. Try again." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:80, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:200-203, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:209-215)
- [x] With a code, loading ends and the watch starts (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:204-205)
- [x] The watch ticks every 250 ms (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:103, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:74-77, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:252-253)
- [x] The watch does nothing while the app is out of sight, while loading, sending, asking, done, failed or stale, or before a code exists (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:236-238) (iPhone: out of sight is the app in the background)
- [x] The code renews 5 seconds before its expiry, with a new key (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:87, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:239-245)
- [x] A code with no expiry (or one that can't be read) never renews and never goes stale; the asking check goes on (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:239-251, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt:37)
- [x] When a renewal falls due 10 minutes or more after the sheet was opened (or last given a new code), renewing stops and the sheet turns stale instead (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:89, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:240-244)
- [x] A renewal sets loading, gets a new code and key, ends loading, clears the renewal failure and counts one more renewal (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:218-224)
- [x] A failed renewal keeps the old code on screen, marks the renewal failed and tries again 10 seconds later (and every 10 seconds after a failure) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:88, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:225-227, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:239)
- [x] While a renewal is failing, the server is not asked whether a device scanned the code (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:246)
- [x] A renewal restarts the watch only when none is running (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:228)
- [x] Every 2 seconds of ticks (counted in ticks, first one right away) the server is asked `GET /rest/getFamilySignInPending?token=<token>` (signed); `familySignInPending` null, missing or with an empty id means nobody yet; failures count as nobody (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:104, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:232-249, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:371-373, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:395-397)
- [x] A device that redeemed the code is shown only if the sheet is still the same one with the same token (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:249)
- [x] Renewing and asking pause while the app is out of sight (screen stop) and pick up when it is back (screen start); a code that expired meanwhile renews at once on return (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:168-180, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:167-200) (iPhone: out of sight is the app in the background)
- [x] "Make a new code" (from stale) and "Try again" (from a failure or error) both reset the opening time, clear stale, renewal failure, error, failed, done and the asking device, then ask for a code; the renewal count is not raised (no "New code" pill) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:134-141)
- [x] While that new code is coming, the previous code stays drawn (not dimmed) until the new one crossfades in (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:137, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:682-690)
- [x] Closing stops the watch and removes the sheet; a send already under way still reaches the server but its answer is dropped (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:124-128, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:157)

### Allow and Deny

- [x] Nothing is decided or sent before the person taps Allow (shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:122-128)
- [x] Allow (needs a sheet, an asking device, a code and a session) stops the watch and sets sending (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:145-151)
- [x] Allow then calls `GET /rest/decideFamilySignIn?token=<token>&allow=true` (signed), and only after it POSTs `/rest/putFamilySignInBox` with form body `token=<token>&box=<box>` (signed in the address; the box never in the address) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:153-156, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:399-406, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:601-604)
- [x] The box holds this app's own sign-in: its username, its secret (the password, or the API key), its way of signing in by name, the server = the code's outside server address (else the address in use) without trailing slash, home = the code's home server address, and the family display name (or "") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:179-192, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:635-637, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt:77)
- [x] The box is sealed with the key that was in the QR code; the key is never sent to the server and the password never travels in the clear (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:155, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:137-146)
- [x] Both calls working: sending ends, the asking device is cleared and the sheet shows done: "Sent to <device name>. It's signing in now." (with "your other device" for a blank name) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:158-159)
- [x] Either call failing: sending ends, the asking device is cleared and the sheet shows the failure "Couldn't send your sign-in. Make a new code and try again." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:81, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:160-161)
- [x] Deny (needs a sheet, a code and a session) clears the asking device, sets loading, calls `decideFamilySignIn` with `allow=false` (its failure ignored), then gets a fresh code and key (one more renewal, so the "New code" pill shows) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:166-177, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt:149-165)
- [x] The answer of `decideFamilySignIn` (`familySignInDecided.state`) is not read (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:400-402)
- [x] The old device stays signed in after handing over; nothing on it is signed out or changed (the sign-in is copied, not moved) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:143-164)

### Sheet host

- [x] Nothing shows while no hand-over sheet is open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:165-166)
- [x] Each opened sheet is its own (state such as the reach choice starts fresh per opening, keyed on the sheet number) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:167, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:240)

- [x] The link's away rule comes from the member's abilities (away allowed unless the plan says no, or while unknown); the avatar name is the family display name, else the username; owner when the family role is Owner; the username named in the link is the family username (none when blank) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:187-201)

### The link inside the code
