### Asking page (a device scanned the code)

- [x] Centred column (24dp sides, 16dp top and bottom, 10dp gaps): a phone icon in a 64dp accent circle, the caption "Sign in on", the device name (or "A new device") in title style marked as a heading, the platform line when the server gave one, and the muted caption "It gets your sign-in for this server. Allow it only if this device is yours." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:341-353) (iPhone: SF Symbol iphone in the 64 point circle)
- [x] It is announced politely as "Sign in on <device name>?" ("Sign in on a new device?" for a blank name) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:354, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:73)
- [x] Below a hairline, a row of two equal buttons: "Deny" (GlazeButton, Medium, disabled while sending) and "Allow" (accent, Medium, spinner while sending) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:356-364, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:82-83)
- [x] The asking page has no header, close button or Done footer; dismissing the sheet closes the hand-over without telling the server no (the new device keeps waiting until the code expires) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:182-186, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:124-128)
- [x] The shared `askingLine` ("<platform> · It gets your sign-in for this server. Allow it only if it's yours.") is not used by the phone, which shows its own wording above (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:75-76, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheets.kt:352-353)

## Server API calls used by join and hand-over

- [x] Open calls (new device, no sign-in): built as `<server>/rest/<endpoint>?v=1.16.1&c=Octo&f=json&<params>`; a network failure is "not reached"; a non-2xx answer is "HTTP <code> from <endpoint>" with its status; the body is read as a Subsonic envelope (status "ok", else error code 40/44 wrong sign-in, 41/42 not supported, 70 not found, other server error) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:418-453, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:28, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:706-720)
- [x] Signed calls (giving device): the account's sign-in parameters (`u`,`t`,`s` for token; `u`,`p=enc:<hex>` for legacy; `apiKey` for a key), then `v`, `c`, `f=json`, then the call's parameters (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:72-81, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Credentials.kt:35-42)
- [x] `startFamilySignIn` (signed GET, answer key `familySignIn`) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:392-393)
- [x] `getFamilySignInPending` (signed GET, `token`, answer key `familySignInPending` = `{id, deviceName, platform}`, may be null or missing) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:396-397)
- [x] `decideFamilySignIn` (signed GET, `token`, `allow=true|false`, answer key `familySignInDecided` optional) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:400-402)
- [x] `putFamilySignInBox` (signed POST, form body `token`, `box`; answer only ok or error) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:406, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:672-674)
- [x] `redeemFamilySignIn` (open GET, `token`, `deviceName` trimmed, `platform`; answer key `familySignInRedeemed.id`) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:411-412)
- [x] `getFamilySignInRedeem` (open GET, `id`; answer key `familySignInRedeem` = `{state, box}`) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:415-416)
- [x] `POST /api/family/join` (family page JSON call, `X-Octo-Family: 1`, `Accept: application/json`; 204 or empty answer reads as an empty object) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:87-88, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt:121-144)
- [x] Every token, key, link and box is kept out of text forms used in logs (`FamilySignInStart`, `FamilySignInStatus`, `FamilyLinkChoices`, `HandOverSheet`, `FamilyHandOverLink`, `FamilyInviteLink`, `JoinOutcome.SignedUp`, `SignInRequest`) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:368, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:387, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt:159-160, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt:64-65, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:30, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:49-50, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt:15-17, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/SessionRepository.kt:108)

## Entry points for Octo admin and Import

- [x] Settings, Server page, "Connection" group (shown only while signed in): an action row titled "Octo admin" with a trailing chevron opens the Octo admin screen; the row is shown only when the session runs Octo (`runsOcto`) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:199,230)
- [x] Settings, Server page, "Connection" group: an action row titled "Import" with a trailing chevron, placed directly under "Octo admin", opens the Import screen; shown only when the session runs Octo (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:231)
- [x] `runsOcto` is true when any of: the admin status page answered at sign-in (probed), the server type equals "octo" ignoring case, or any listed OpenSubsonic extension name starts with "octo" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/data/OctoProbe.kt:16-19)
- [x] Neither row checks the Navidrome admin role; any Octo user sees "Octo admin" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/ServerPage.kt:230)
- [x] Settings search entry for Octo admin: id "octo_admin", page Server, title "Octo admin", description "What Octo is doing: services, stations and downloads", keywords "admin", "health", "services", "stations", section "Connection" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:292-295)
- [x] Settings search entry for Import: id "import", page Server, title "Import", description "Your music from another service, a file or a list, and fetching what is missing", keywords "import", "get my music", "move", "transfer", "spotify", "apple music", "youtube music", "amazon music", "tidal", "deezer", "qobuz", "soundcloud", "pandora", "youtube", "tunemymusic", "playlists", "liked", "file", "csv", "paste", "missing", "download", section "Connection" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/settings/SettingsIndex.kt:296-303)
- [x] Family screen: when the plan allows importing (`can.importPlaylists`, default true), a block shows the heading "Get my music" (body, primary), the caption "Bring your playlists and liked songs over from Spotify, Apple Music, YouTube Music and more." (muted) and a small glaze button "Import" that opens the Import screen (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyScreen.kt:217-225, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:404)
- [x] A list shared to Octo from another app opens Import (see "Share to Octo"); this path does not check `runsOcto` (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:182-189)
- [x] Both screens are detail pages pushed on the current tab's stack, each with the floating round back button (40dp, content description "Back", 16dp from the start, 8dp below the status bar) that pops the stack (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:402-403, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:56-66) (iPhone: pushed on the Settings stack with the Liquid Glass back button; the edge swipe also goes back)
- [x] Both routes belong to the Settings ambient area for the background glow (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ambient/AmbientSource.kt:59-61)
- [x] The settings search keyword constant `IMPORT_SERVICE_WORDS` ("spotify apple music youtube music amazon music tidal deezer qobuz soundcloud pandora youtube") exists in shared code but the Android app does not reference it; the settings index carries its own keyword list (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:23-24)

## Octo admin screen

### Layout and places

- [x] The screen is a vertical list wrapped in pull to refresh; pulling calls refresh; the refresh indicator shows while `refreshing` is true (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:84-89) (iPhone: the system pull to refresh, its spinner showing while the refresh runs)
- [x] The list's content padding is the status bar height + 8dp + 56dp (detail top gap) at the top, and the navigation bar height + 120dp at the bottom (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:90, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:25-29,52)
- [x] First item is the screen title "Octo admin" (display style, primary color, padding start/end 20dp, top 8dp, bottom 16dp) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:91, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:32-39)
- [x] Place "Looking" (still finding the admin pages): only a centered spinner (22dp, accent color, 2dp stroke) with 24dp padding above it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:93,219-224)
- [x] Place "Away" (no address answered): centered text "Octo's admin is only available on your home network" (bodySmall, secondary color, centered), then an accent button "Try again" that runs a fresh look; column padded 32dp horizontal, 48dp vertical, 16dp spacing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:94,231-247)
- [x] Place "Found": sections in this order: Health, Stations, Downloads, Library, then the "Open full admin" row (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:95-106)
- [x] Each section loads and fails on its own: while loading it shows a centered spinner with 8dp vertical padding; on failure it shows its failure message as a note (bodySmall, muted, 20dp horizontal padding); when ready it shows its rows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:209-229)
- [x] Section titles use the section style, primary color, 20dp horizontal and 10dp vertical padding (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:42-49)
- [x] The back button floats over the list (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:110) (iPhone: the Liquid Glass back button floats over the list)

### Health section

- [x] Section title "Health" with 8dp extra top padding (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:115)
- [x] First row is always named "Octo" with the server's own `octo` health (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:117)
- [x] Then one row per entry of `services`, in the order the server's JSON lists them, named by the service name mapping (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:118-120)
- [x] Service names: "navidrome" shows "Navidrome", "slskd" shows "Soulseek", "lidarr" shows "Lidarr", "ytDlpShim" shows "Streaming helper", "lastfm" shows "Last.fm", any other key shows the key itself unchanged (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:29-36)
- [x] Health state is picked in this order: not configured gives "Not set up"; else not ok gives "Down"; else warning gives "Warning"; else "Ok" (so not ok with warning is Down) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:20-25)
- [x] Ok draws a dot #8FBFA0 with accessibility word "Working" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:72,254)
- [x] Warning draws a dot #E3B35B with accessibility word "Needs a look" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:73,255)
- [x] Down draws a dot in the error color with accessibility word "Not working" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:256)
- [x] Not set up draws a dot in the accent color at 30% alpha with accessibility word "Not set up" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:74,257)
- [x] Health row layout: 20dp horizontal, 8dp vertical padding; an 8dp circle dot 6dp from the top; 14dp gap; then name and detail; the whole row is one accessibility element (merged), the dot carrying the state word as its description (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:259-272)
- [x] Name text is bodySmall; muted color when not set up, primary otherwise (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:274-278)
- [x] Detail line: "Not set up" when not set up, else the server's `detail`; hidden when blank; caption, muted, at most 3 lines, ellipsized (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:279-282)
- [x] Health failure note: "Couldn't load Octo's health just now. Pull down to try again." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:137,153)

### Stations section

- [x] Section title "Stations" with 18dp extra top padding (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:130)
- [x] When stations are disabled on the server (`enabled` false): only the note "Stations are switched off in Octo" shows; no learning line, no list, no refresh button (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:132-135)
- [x] When the server sends a `learning` object, a block (20dp horizontal padding, 2dp spacing) shows the learning line in bodySmall primary (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:136-139)
- [x] Learning line: when `needed` <= 0 or `plays` >= `needed`: "Learned from N plays" ("Learned from 1 play" for one); otherwise "Learning your taste: P of N plays" ("of 1 play" when needed is 1); numbers use the locale's thousands grouping (for example "1,234") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:67-74)
- [x] Under it, caption muted: "Last refreshed <ago>" from `lastRefreshSuccessUtc`, or "Not refreshed yet" when missing or unreadable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:140-145)
- [x] Under that, when `lastRefreshError` gives a problem line, it shows in caption, error color, at most 2 lines, ellipsized (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:146-148)
- [x] Refresh problem wording: trimmed error; blank gives no line; if it starts with "{", "[" or "<", contains a newline, contains "Exception", or is longer than 160 characters, the line is "The last refresh did not finish."; otherwise "The last refresh did not finish: <error>" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:78-83)
- [x] Empty station list (stations enabled): note "No stations yet" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:152)
- [x] Station rows, in server order, keyed by position: 52dp tall, 20dp horizontal padding, vertically centered: name (bodySmall, primary, 1 line, ellipsized) over the track count caption muted, "1 song" or "N songs" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:153,287-299, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Layout.kt:82)
- [x] Station rows are not tappable (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:287-299)
- [x] Refresh button (glaze, large size, 20dp start/end, 8dp top padding): label "Refresh stations"; while a refresh asked here is running or the server says `learning.refreshing` is true, label "Refreshing" and disabled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:154-157)
- [x] Under the button, a station refresh problem shows in caption, error color (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:158)
- [x] Tapping "Refresh stations": ignored unless the admin pages are found or while a refresh asked here is still running; clears the problem; sends POST /api/admin/lastfm/radio/refresh with JSON body {"user":"<signed-in username>"} (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:85-92, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:179-182)
- [x] After the refresh is accepted, the stations are read again up to 5 times, waiting 4,000 ms before each read; each successful read replaces the shown stations; looking stops early as soon as a read fails or the read says `learning.refreshing` is not true (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:93-100,158-159)
- [x] When the refresh request fails (server error, no answer, not signed in), the problem line reads "Octo could not start a refresh just now." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:103-104)
- [x] A failed re-read during the follow-up loop shows no message and leaves the stations shown as they were (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:97-99)
- [x] The "refreshing here" state always ends when the follow-up finishes, fails or is cancelled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:105-107)
- [x] Stations failure note: "Couldn't load the stations just now. Pull down to try again." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:138,153)

### Open full admin row

- [x] Last item, 18dp top padding: a full width tappable row (button role), 52dp tall, 20dp horizontal padding, label "Open full admin" in body style primary, trailing 22dp chevron in muted color with no accessibility description (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:100-105,331-345)
- [x] Tapping opens <found address>/admin/ in the system browser (path replaced by "/admin/", no sign-in added); nothing happens when no address is found or no browser opens (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminScreen.kt:101-104, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:83, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:185) (iPhone: opens in Safari through the system's open URL)

### Loading, refresh and server change

- [x] Opening the screen starts a fresh look: place "Looking", then the four sections load (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:62-63)
- [x] A fresh look cancels any load in progress, sets place to "Looking" and does not show the pull refresh indicator (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:111-115)
- [x] Pull to refresh is ignored while one is already running; otherwise it keeps what is shown (place and section data) until new answers arrive, showing the pull indicator (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:77-80,115)
- [x] Finding fails (no address, an error, or not signed in) sets place "Away", including on a pull to refresh from a found page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:118-128)
- [x] On a fresh look, or when the found address differs from the one shown, all four sections reset to loading before reading (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:129-134)
- [x] The four sections are read in parallel: status, stations, library, downloads; each sets its own ready or failed state (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:136-141)
- [x] Every section failure, whatever the cause (including a 403 for a non-admin account), shows "Couldn't load <what> just now. Pull down to try again." with <what> being "Octo's health", "the stations", "the library folder" or "recent downloads"; the underlying reason is never shown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:148-154)
- [x] The pull indicator ends only when the latest load finishes; a load replaced by a newer one leaves it alone (generation counter) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:59-60,113,142-144)
- [x] When the server in use changes (by kept server id), a station refresh in progress is cancelled, the refreshing flag and station problem are cleared, and a fresh look starts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:64-72, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:129)
- [x] There is no automatic polling of the admin screen; data changes only on open, pull to refresh, "Try again", server change, or the station refresh follow-up (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt:62-146)

### Finding the admin pages and admin calls

- [x] Not signed in: finding returns nothing (screen goes "Away") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:133-134)
- [x] Candidates are tried in this order: the client's current base address, its primary address, then the address last found for this kept server; each reduced to its root (path "/", no query) and each root tried once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:136-142,201)
- [x] An address answers when GET <root>/api/admin/status (unsigned, 4 second total call timeout) returns a success status and a JSON object with an "octo" key (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:117,187-193)
- [x] Only when none of those answers, the station list is read (getInternetRadioStations, waiting at most 5,000 ms, failures count as none) and each station's `streamUrl` root is tried, skipping roots already tried (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:143-146,195-199,264)
- [x] The address found is saved per kept server under key "address@<server id>" in the "octo_admin" store, and the older single key "address" is removed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:106,148-151,259-261) (iPhone: kept in the app's defaults as octo_admin.address@<server id>; the older single key is removed)
- [x] The older single "address" value counts as this server's last found address only while exactly one server is kept (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:163-167)
- [x] Removing a kept server with "forget here" also forgets its saved admin address (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:155-158, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:183-195)
- [x] Trusted addresses for the sign-in: the roots of the base and primary addresses; station hosts are added only when they were looked at; trust is tied to the kept server it was found for (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:121-126,140-145)
- [x] An admin call carries the Subsonic sign-in (u/t/s token, u/p legacy, or apiKey, plus v=1.16.1 and c=Octo, no f) only when the target's root (scheme, host and port) is in the trusted set of the current server; otherwise it goes unsigned (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:41-42,247-251, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:85-90)
- [x] Read calls: GET <base>/api/admin/status, /api/admin/downloads, /api/admin/lastfm/radio?user=<signed-in username> (user left out when there is none), /api/admin/library-status; 30 second total call timeout (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:118,169-176,203-221,239-240)
- [x] Read call errors: 403 is "Needs a Navidrome admin account"; other non-success is "HTTP <code> from <path>"; network failure is "Octo did not answer"; unreadable JSON is "Octo answered in a way the app does not understand" (none of these reach the screen) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:212-220)
- [x] JSON is read leniently: unknown keys ignored, nulls and bad values fall back to defaults (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:119)
- [x] The change call (station refresh) is a POST with header "X-Octo-Admin: 1" and content type application/json; any non-success status is an error (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:224-237)
- [x] Defaults for missing fields: health ok false, detail "", warning false, configured true; status services empty; download fields empty strings, cover null, size 0, requestedBy empty; learning plays 0, needed 0, refreshing false; station trackCount 0; radio enabled false; library writable, visibleToOcto and rescanAuthenticated false (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt:45-102)

### Admin time wording

- [x] Times from Octo are read as an offset date time, or, when written without a zone, as UTC; blank or unreadable gives nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:39-44)
- [x] Under 1 minute ago, and any time in the future: "Just now" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:53)
- [x] 1 to 59 minutes: "<minutes> min ago" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:54)
- [x] Same local calendar day, or under 6 hours: "<whole hours> hr ago" (for example "3 hr ago" for 23:00 seen at 02:00) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:55)
- [x] Previous local calendar day (and 6 hours or more): "Yesterday" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:56)
- [x] 2 to 6 calendar days: "<days> days ago" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:57)
- [x] Older, same year: day and short month, pattern "d MMM" in the device locale (for example "12 Sep") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:58)
- [x] Older, other year: pattern "d MMM yyyy" (for example "30 Dec 2025") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:59)
- [x] Calendar days use the device time zone (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt:48-51)

## Import screen

### Lifetime, polling and server change

- [x] The Import state lives as long as the Import page is on the back stack, so a Spotify sign-in waiting for the browser or a wait for a TuneMyMusic file survives the app going to the background (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:75-91) (iPhone: the page's model lives while the page is on the Settings stack, so a Spotify sign-in waiting goes on)
- [x] When the page shows, watching starts: read now, then wait 3,000 ms while anything moves or a Spotify sign-in is waiting, otherwise 20,000 ms, and read again, in a loop (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:73-82, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt:27-28)
- [x] "Anything moves" means the Spotify reading is busy, or the trickle has queued songs, or songs downloading (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt:31)
- [x] When the page leaves the screen (another page pushed on top, or popped), watching stops; it starts again when the page shows again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:134-137,104-112)
- [x] When the signed-in server changes (by kept server id, ignoring the loading state), everything is forgotten (overview, services, step back to the grid, problem, opened list, server words, sign-in cancelled) and, if the page is showing, watching starts for the new server (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:95-102, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:89-101)
- [x] Leaving the page for good forgets everything (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:114)
- [x] Each read: GET getImports for the overview; on success the problem clears and the services are read when needed (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:103-108)
- [x] Read fails with no connection: problem "Octo can't reach the server right now." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:109-110)
- [x] Read fails otherwise: problem "This server has no imports yet. Update Octo on the server to use it." when nothing was read before, else the error's own message (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:111-114)
- [x] A failed read keeps the last overview on screen; the problem text only shows while there is no overview, so a failure after a good read shows nothing (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:105-114, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:158-159)
- [x] With no signed-in client, a read does nothing (the page stays on its waiting line) (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:104)
- [x] When a list is open, each read also fetches that list (GET getImport?id=<id>) and shows it only if the same list is still open; failures are ignored (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:115-116)
- [x] Services: asked whether the server lists extension "octoImports" at version 2 once per server; "could not tell" is asked again on the next read; when yes and not yet read, GET getImportServices; a failure is retried on the next read; once read they are kept for this server (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:119-129, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Imports.kt:9-13)
- [x] A server without octoImports version 2 never gets getImportServices and shows the Spotify import alone (no "Get my music") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:41-43,123, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicTest.kt:98-106)

### Screen layout and order

- [x] Title "Import" (screen title style) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:156, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:13)
- [x] Before the first overview arrives: one line, the problem text, or "Asking the server about your lists." when there is none; nothing else on the page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:157-159)
- [x] Lines are bodySmall, 20dp horizontal, 6dp vertical padding, muted color unless stated (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:185-188)
- [x] With an overview, when the server reports `libraryProblem`, it shows first as a line in signal orange (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:161)
- [x] With services listed: "Get my music" items, then the server words line (secondary color) if any, then a "Spotify" section title (12dp extra top padding), then the Spotify account card and the public link block (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:162-167)
- [x] Without services: the Spotify account card and public link block right under the title (no "Spotify" section title), then the server words line if any (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:168-171)
- [x] An empty server words value ("") still shows as an empty padded line (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:165,170)
- [x] Then either the opened list (when a list is open and still in the overview) or "Your lists"; then the Trickle section (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:172-174)
- [x] There is no pull to refresh on Import (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:154-182)

### Get my music: service grid

- [x] The grid is a settings style group with title "Get my music" while choosing, and "Pick another service" in every other step (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:77-79)
- [x] While choosing, the caption above the tiles reads "Pick where your music is now. Octo opens TuneMyMusic, which saves your lists to a file for Octo to read." (muted) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:81, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:27)
- [x] Tiles in server order, two per row, 8dp gaps; an odd last tile keeps half width (an empty spacer fills the rest) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:166-177)
- [x] Tile: at least 56dp tall, 14dp rounded corners; the chosen tile is filled with the accent with dark ink #0C0C0D and a 14dp check icon at the top end (6dp in); other tiles are accent at 6% alpha with a 1dp border of muted at 25% alpha and primary ink; fill and ink animate (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:163-206) (iPhone: SF Symbol checkmark in the chosen tile)
- [x] Tile name: centered, 15sp, up to 2 lines, shrinking in steps down to 10sp so the name is never cut; 20dp horizontal, 8dp vertical padding (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:195-202)
- [x] Tiles are radio buttons for accessibility, selected when chosen, described by the service name (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:192-193) (iPhone: buttons with the selected trait when chosen)
- [x] The chosen tile is the service of the current step (Spotify ways, waiting, or sending); none while choosing or after sending (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:60-66)
- [x] Tiles are disabled (50% alpha) while a file or list is sending (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:82,191)
- [x] Tapping a tile: ignored while sending; clears the server words; if the server can sign in to Spotify (`spotifyConnect`) and the tile's id is "spotify", goes to the Spotify ways step; any other tile opens its export page (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:135-140, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:99)
- [x] Opening an export page: the address must be https, host exactly "tunemymusic.com" or "www.tunemymusic.com", no user or password, port 443; it is trimmed and normalized (for example "HTTPS://WWW.TuneMyMusic.com" opens "https://www.tunemymusic.com/") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:71-78, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicTest.kt:60-81)
- [x] A valid export page opens in the system browser (new task) and the step becomes waiting for that service (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:144-153, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:121-123) (iPhone: TuneMyMusic opens in Safari, so its file lands in Downloads)
- [x] Any other export address is refused: nothing opens, the step goes back to choosing, and the server words line reads "That export link does not go to TuneMyMusic, so Octo will not open it." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:145-150, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:92)
- [x] Tapping the already chosen tile opens its export page again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:172)

### Get my music: steps

- [x] Choosing: the grid first, then a group titled "Already have a file" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:90-98)
- [x] Every other step: its step card first, so it shows without scrolling, then the grid under it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:87-136)
- [x] Step cards: a settings group with a 16dp padded column, 10dp spacing: heading (body, primary), the line (bodySmall, secondary, left out when null), then the buttons full width one under another with 8dp spacing and 4dp extra above (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:139-150)
- [x] Spotify ways card: heading is the service name; line "Connecting keeps your Spotify lists updating in Octo. A file is a copy of them as they are today." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:99-101)
- [x] Spotify ways buttons: accent "Connect Spotify (keeps updating)" (medium, disabled while a sign-in is waiting) which returns the step to choosing and starts the Spotify sign-in; glaze "Use a file" (medium) which opens Spotify's export page; glaze "Back" (small) back to the grid (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:102-104, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:162-172)
- [x] "Back" and returning to the grid are ignored while sending (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:169-172)
- [x] Waiting card heading: "Waiting for your <service name> file", or "Waiting for your file" with no service (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:88-89)
- [x] Waiting card line with a service: "Tap the <tile> tile, sign in, choose your playlists or Liked songs, then Export to file as CSV." where <tile> is the service's `tile` words, or its name when blank (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:81-82)
- [x] Waiting card line with no service: "Pick a list file saved from TuneMyMusic or another app: CSV, TXT, JSON or ZIP." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:85)
- [x] Waiting buttons: accent "Use the file I just downloaded" (medium) opens the file picker; glaze "Paste a list" (medium) opens the paste sheet; with a service, glaze "Open TuneMyMusic again" (small) opens its export page again (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:109-119)
- [x] Sending card: heading "Sending <name>" (the file name or list name), line "Octo reads your lists, then checks them against your library.", one disabled glaze button "Sending" (medium) showing a loading spinner (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:120-127)
- [x] Sent card: heading is the server's message, or "Your lists are in." when blank; line is the approval line when this member's downloads need approval, otherwise none; accent button "Import more" (medium) back to the grid (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:128-135)
- [x] Approval line: "Your lists are in. <approver> approves downloads before Octo fetches the missing songs." with <approver> the trimmed `approval.by`, or "The owner" when missing or blank; only when `approval.needed` is true (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:101-107)

### Paste sheet

### Sending files and text

- [x] Before sending a file: a name not ending in .txt, .csv, .zip or .json (any case) is refused with "Octo reads lists from CSV, TXT, JSON or ZIP files."; an empty file with "That file is empty."; over 67,108,864 bytes with "That file is over 64 MB, more than Octo takes."; refused files do not change the step and are not sent (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:176-186, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:33-36,94-96, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Imports.kt:16)
- [x] Sending text: blank text is ignored; a blank name becomes "Pasted list" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:191-195, shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt:19)
- [x] A send is ignored while another is sending, or with no signed-in client (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:204-205)
- [x] Sending sets the step to sending (keeping the service of the step it came from) and clears the server words (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:206-208)
- [x] File upload: POST /rest/importFile?name=<name> (signed, f=json) as multipart form with one part "file" named <name>, its type by ending: csv text/csv, json application/json, zip application/zip, txt text/plain, else application/octet-stream; read timeout 3 minutes (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:68,233-242,828-834)
- [x] Text upload: POST /rest/importText with a form body name=<name>, text=<text> (sign-in in the address); read timeout 3 minutes (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt:246-249,601-604)
- [x] The server's answer is read from the "importAction" key: ok, message, url, listId, count (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Imports.kt:244-253)
- [x] Answer ok: the overview is read again, then the step becomes sent with the server's message (for example "Read 2 lists, 340 songs.") and the approval line from that fresh overview (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:212-216)
- [x] Answer not ok: the server words line shows the message, or "The server could not read that file." when blank; the step returns to waiting for the same service (waiting with no service when sent from choosing), so another file can be tried (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:217-219)
- [x] Send error (no answer, server error): the words show the error's message, or "Octo could not reach the server." when it has none; the step returns to waiting; the overview is read again (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:221-225)

### Spotify account card

- [x] Starting a sign-in is ignored while one waits or with no client; it sets the words to "Allow Octo in your browser, then come back here." while it waits (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:230-235)

This page notices on its own when that is done."; else the server's answer (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt:137-141) (iPhone: the sign-in page opens in Safari inside Octo, so Octo stays awake to catch the answer)
- [x] Otherwise the app catches Spotify's answer on the device; when the registered redirect is not a loopback address without a port, it ends with "The apps sign in with the redirect http://127.0.0.1/callback, with no port. - [x] Usable redirect: scheme http, no port, host resolving to a loopback address (brackets of an IPv6 host ignored), and not "localhost" in any case; the path is the registered path, "/" when empty (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt:93-102)
- [x] The catcher listens on that loopback address at a free port; it asks action "connect" with redirect=http://<host>:<port><path>; if the answer is not ok or has no url it ends with that answer; else the url opens in the browser (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt:147-151, subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt:27-28) (iPhone: a Network framework listener on the loopback address at a free port)
- [x] It waits up to 10 minutes for the browser to come back; none gives "Spotify did not answer. Connect again when you are ready." (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt:134,152)
- [x] Error "access_denied" gives "Spotify says access was not allowed."; any other error gives "Spotify sent back an error: <error>" (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt:153-155)
- [x] Otherwise action "finish" with code=<code> and state=<state> (empty when missing), and its answer is the result (for example "Connected to Spotify as Brandon.") (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt:156)
- [x] The catcher only accepts the registered path with a "code" or "error" query value (values URL decoded); any other request gets a 404 page and keeps waiting (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt:72-86,52-70)
- [x] Catcher page on success: title and heading "Back to Octo", text "Octo has Spotify's answer and is finishing the sign-in. You can close this page and go back to Octo." (HTTP 200) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt:110)
- [x] Catcher page on an error answer: "Spotify said no", "Spotify did not let Octo in. Go back to Octo to try again." (HTTP 200) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt:111)
- [x] Catcher page for anything else: "Nothing here", "This page only catches Spotify's answer for Octo." (HTTP 404) (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt:112)
- [x] Catcher pages are dark: 16px system font, background #0d0f14, text #e8e8ef, centered, content at most 26rem wide with 2rem padding, viewport set for phones (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt:104-108)

## Share to Octo

### Share receiver

- [x] The share target is an invisible activity labeled "Octo", exported, left out of recents and history, taking action SEND (not SEND_MULTIPLE) with category DEFAULT for MIME types text/plain, text/csv, text/comma-separated-values (zip and json are not share targets) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:86-103) (iPhone: a share extension named Octo, for shared text, links and CSV or text files; ZIP and JSON are not taken)
- [x] On first create only, when the share is one Import takes, it hands it to the main screen in Octo's own task (new task, single top) with the same action, type and extras, passing read permission for the shared file; it always closes at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/ShareToImportActivity.kt:14-36) (iPhone: the extension leaves the share in the app group's inbox and opens Octo with octo://import, then closes)
- [x] The main screen takes the share on a fresh start and on a new intent, but not when launched again from recents (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:60-83, app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:85-87) (iPhone: the app takes the inbox when it opens octo://import or becomes active; iOS never replays a share)

### Parsing a share

- [x] Only action "android.intent.action.SEND" counts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:25,37) (iPhone: the share extension takes one share at a time)
- [x] The type is cut at ";" (parameters dropped), trimmed, lower cased, and must be text/plain, text/csv or text/comma-separated-values (for example "Text/Plain; charset=utf-8" counts as text/plain) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:28,38-39) (iPhone: the extension reads CSV and plain text files by their types; types with parameters are cut the same way when naming)
- [x] A shared file wins over shared text (apps often add a line of text to a file) and keeps the cleaned type (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:40)
- [x] With no file, the shared text is the list itself; blank text is not an import; the subject, trimmed, becomes the list name, or none when empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:41-42)
- [x] The share is read from the stream extra, the text extra and the subject extra (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:46-53) (iPhone: read from the share's attachments and its title)

### Handing the share to Import

- [x] A share waits in one app wide inbox (a newer share replaces an older one waiting) and is handed over once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:69-83)
- [x] When a share is waiting, the app selects the last tab (Settings) and pushes Import on it unless Import is already on top (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:182-189)
- [x] Import sends the share only once the server has answered with an overview; until then it keeps waiting in the inbox (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:143-153)
- [x] A shared file is read and sent like a picked file, named using the shared type; shared text is sent as a pasted list named by the subject, or "Pasted list" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportScreen.kt:148-151)
- [x] The send follows the normal rules: the step becomes sending, then sent or waiting, with the server's words (shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt:203-227)

### File naming and reading limits

- [x] A file keeps its trimmed display name when it ends in .txt, .csv, .zip or .json (any case) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:57-59)
- [x] Otherwise an ending is added by type: text/csv or text/comma-separated-values gives ".csv", application/zip ".zip", application/json ".json", anything else ".txt"; a missing or blank name becomes "Shared list" (for example "Liked" with text/csv gives "Liked.csv", "song.mp3" gives "song.mp3.txt", no name with application/json gives "Shared list.json") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt:60-66)
- [x] For a picked file the type is the one the system reports for it; for a shared file, the share's type (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:272)
- [x] The file is read in 64 KiB chunks and reading stops as soon as it goes past 67,108,864 bytes (a file of exactly that size is accepted); an empty file reads as empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt:294-306)

## Launcher shortcuts

### The four shortcuts

- [x] Exactly four launcher shortcuts exist, in this fixed order, which is also their rank 0, 1, 2, 3: Shuffle all, Liked songs, Recently added, Resume (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:18-24, 35, 41) (iPhone: Home Screen quick actions, in this order)
- [x] Shuffle all: id "shuffle_all", label "Shuffle all" (string shortcut_shuffle_all), icon shortcut_shuffle (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:20; app/src/main/res/values/shortcuts.xml:7) (iPhone: quick action shuffle_all with the SF Symbol shuffle)
- [x] Liked songs: id "liked", label "Liked songs" (string widget_quick_liked, shared with the quick play widget), icon shortcut_liked (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:21; app/src/main/res/values/widget.xml:41) (iPhone: quick action liked with the SF Symbol heart.fill)
- [x] Recently added: id "recently_added", label "Recently added" (string widget_quick_recent, shared with the quick play widget), icon shortcut_recent (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:22; app/src/main/res/values/widget.xml:42) (iPhone: quick action recently_added with the SF Symbol square.stack)
- [x] Resume: id "resume", label "Resume" (string widget_quick_resume, shared with the quick play widget), icon shortcut_resume (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:23; app/src/main/res/values/widget.xml:44) (iPhone: quick action resume with the SF Symbol play.fill)
- [x] Each shortcut's short label and long label are the same string (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:36-39) (iPhone: a quick action has one title and no subtitle)
- [x] Every shortcut icon is an adaptive icon: solid background color #FF1A1A1B (Octo's "dark glass"), foreground a white glyph inset 31% so it sits inside the launcher's mask like the launcher's own shortcuts (app/src/main/res/values/shortcuts.xml:5-6; app/src/main/res/drawable/shortcut_shuffle.xml:1-11) (iPhone: iOS draws quick action icons itself, as plain glyphs)
- [x] Shuffle all icon glyph: sym_shuffle (white, 24dp, 256 viewport) (app/src/main/res/drawable/shortcut_shuffle.xml:8; design/src/main/res/drawable/sym_shuffle.xml) (iPhone: SF Symbol shuffle)
- [x] Liked songs icon glyph: sym_favorite_filled, a filled heart (app/src/main/res/drawable/shortcut_liked.xml:7; design/src/main/res/drawable/sym_favorite_filled.xml) (iPhone: SF Symbol heart.fill)
- [x] Recently added icon glyph: sym_album, the album glyph (app/src/main/res/drawable/shortcut_recent.xml:7; design/src/main/res/drawable/sym_album.xml) (iPhone: SF Symbol square.stack)
- [x] Resume icon glyph: sym_play_arrow, the play triangle (app/src/main/res/drawable/shortcut_resume.xml:7; design/src/main/res/drawable/sym_play_arrow.xml) (iPhone: SF Symbol play.fill)
- [x] The drawable file names are exactly shortcut_liked.xml, shortcut_recent.xml, shortcut_resume.xml, shortcut_shuffle.xml (app/src/main/res/drawable/) (iPhone: SF Symbols, so no drawable files)

### Publishing

- [x] The shortcuts are dynamic (there is no static shortcuts XML); they are handed to the launcher with setDynamicShortcuts, which replaces the whole dynamic set each time (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:50) (iPhone: the app's shortcutItems, replaced as a whole each time)
- [x] They are published every time MainActivity is created, including a re-creation after rotation (the call comes before the saved-state check), so their names follow the phone's current language (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:59; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:32-34) (iPhone: set each time the app becomes active, so the names follow the phone's language)
- [x] All four are always published; none is ever hidden, disabled or reordered by state (no liked songs, an empty library, or no saved queue still shows all four) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:35-48)
- [x] If the launcher refuses the update (IllegalStateException, such as being rate limited or asked from the background), the shortcuts already published stay, a log line "could not publish shortcuts" is written, and nothing is shown to the listener (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:49-54) (iPhone: iOS takes the quick actions an app sets; there is nothing to refuse)
- [x] Each shortcut's intent is an explicit intent for MainActivity with action "app.winters.octo.SHORTCUT" and a string extra "app.winters.octo.shortcut" holding the shortcut's id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:14-16, 42-46) (iPhone: each quick action's type is its id)
- [x] An intent is a shortcut only when its action is exactly "app.winters.octo.SHORTCUT" and the extra equals one of the four ids; an unknown id ("nothing") is not a shortcut, and a known id with another action (for example android.intent.action.MAIN) is not a shortcut (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt:26-30; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt:62-67) (iPhone: a quick action whose type is not one of the four ids does nothing)

### Tapping a shortcut: the path in

- [x] Cold start: MainActivity.onCreate builds the screen, publishes the shortcuts, and only when there is no saved instance state hands the intent to SystemEntries.handle (after the voice, widget, family and import checks) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:54-67) (iPhone: a quick action that launches Octo waits until its scene is active)
- [x] Android launches a launcher shortcut's intent with FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK and FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TASK (platform rule for shortcut launches), so a shortcut tapped while Octo is open still arrives through onCreate with no saved state (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:61-67) (iPhone: a quick action while Octo runs reaches the scene delegate)
- [x] Any intent delivered to the running MainActivity (it is singleTop) goes through the same handling in onNewIntent (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:61; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:70-77) (iPhone: the scene delegate takes every quick action the same way)
- [x] An intent handed over again because the app was reopened from Recents (FLAG_ACTIVITY_LAUNCHED_FROM_HISTORY) is ignored, never treated as a new tap (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:31-35) (iPhone: iOS never replays a quick action)
- [x] An intent with neither a shortcut nor an opened file does nothing in SystemEntries (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:36-38)
- [x] Nothing starts until the activity is resumed (on screen), so the app is allowed to start the playback service; the work then runs in the activity's lifecycle scope (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:39-41) (iPhone: runs once the scene is active)
- [~] Every shortcut tap reports the shortcut's use to the launcher (reportShortcutUsed with its id) before doing anything else (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:47-48) (not on iPhone: iOS has no way for an app to report a quick action's use)
- [x] When the shortcut started something, the full player is opened: MainActivity bumps its openPlayer counter and the shell sets the full player open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:42-43; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:51-52, 66; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:202-205)
- [x] The full player is only drawn while a song is loaded (visible = playerOpen and hasTrack), so it appears as soon as the first song of the shortcut's list loads (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:319)
- [x] When a shortcut has nothing to play, the full player is not opened and the feedback pill shows the shortcut's empty message (one line, 3.5 seconds, tap to dismiss) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:67-71; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Feedback.kt:55-57, 141-143)

### What each shortcut plays

- [x] Shuffle all plays every song in the library (all track ids ordered by sortKey), shuffled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:50; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/QuickPicks.kt:27-28; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:67-68)
- [x] Shuffle all with an empty library shows "No songs in your library yet" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:50)
- [x] Liked songs plays the liked songs the library still has, newest like first in the list, shuffled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:51; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/QuickPicks.kt:20; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:33-35)
- [x] Liked songs with no liked songs shows "No liked songs yet" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:51)
- [x] Recently added plays the songs of the 10 most recently added albums (album addedAt descending), newest album first, each album's songs in album order (albumOrder), not shuffled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:52-54; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/QuickPicks.kt:8-9, 21-22; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:14-15, 64-65)
- [x] Recently added with no albums shows "No songs in your library yet" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:53)
- [x] Shuffle all, Liked songs and Recently added replace the whole queue: the shuffle switch is set to the shortcut's value (so Recently added turns shuffle off), the songs go to the service as id-only requests, then prepare and play (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:72; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:140-152)
- [x] Songs started by a shortcut carry the source word "list" (a played list with no name), so the queue heads them "Up next" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:144-146; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/QueueSources.kt:24-27, 60-64)
- [x] With shuffle on, the queue is set with reset position, so playback starts at the first song of the new shuffle order; with shuffle off it starts at the first song, position 0 (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:147-148)
- [x] A play request made before the app is connected to the playback service is kept and run as soon as the connection is up (the connection is started on demand) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:106-124, 265-271) (iPhone: the player lives in the app, so there is no service to connect to first)
- [x] Resume does exactly what the home screen widget's play button does: it sends the widget command "play" to the playback service through a foreground-service PendingIntent (action "app.winters.octo.widget.COMMAND", extra "app.winters.octo.widget.command" = "play", request code 1, immutable and update-current) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:55-58; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetIntents.kt:9-10, 19-33; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetCommand.kt:17-23, 35-43) (iPhone: Resume runs the widgets' play command in the app)
- [x] Resume counts as started (and opens the full player) whenever the PendingIntent was sent, even if nothing ends up playing; only a cancelled PendingIntent logs "resume shortcut refused" and does not open the player (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:57-63)
- [x] In the service, the Resume command waits until last session's queue has been restored, then: with a queue, it does Media3's play button action (prepare when idle, back to the start when ended, then play); with no queue, it plays every song in the library shuffled, starting at a random song (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:253-266; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetRemote.kt:33-35, 50-66)
- [x] Resume with no queue and an empty library plays nothing and shows no message (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetRemote.kt:60-61)
- [x] When the service was started by a widget command but nothing will play, it keeps Android's foreground promise with a silent notification (id 20939, on Media3's default media channel, which it creates at low importance if missing, with Media3's small icon) and removes it at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetRemote.kt:17-19, 46-47, 68-89) (iPhone: iOS has no foreground service promise to keep)
- [x] When a widget command arrives with no app connected, the service adds its session by hand so the media notification shows once music plays (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:256-259) (iPhone: the app runs the command itself, and Now Playing shows once music plays)

## Open with (opening audio files from other apps)

### The entry activity

- [x] "Open with Octo" is handled by OpenWithActivity: exported, excluded from Recents, no history, Theme.NoDisplay (nothing is drawn) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:105-112) (iPhone: a document type for audio, so Octo is in Open in and the share sheet for audio files; opened in place)
- [x] Intent filter 1: action android.intent.action.VIEW, category DEFAULT, schemes "content" and "file", MIME types audio/*, application/ogg, application/x-ogg, application/x-flac (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:113-122) (iPhone: the public.audio document type covers every audio type)
- [x] Intent filter 2, for apps that call every file application/octet-stream: action VIEW, category DEFAULT, schemes "content" and "file", host "*", MIME application/octet-stream, and the file name must end in .mp3, .flac, .m4a, .ogg, .opus, .wav or .aac (lower case), each listed both as ".*\\.ext" and ".*\\..*\\.ext" so names with another dot also match (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:123-146) (iPhone: iOS types files by their name's ending, so .mp3, .flac, .m4a, .ogg, .opus, .wav and .aac files arrive as audio)
- [x] Only the single file in the intent's data is taken; nothing else (no list of files, no clip data) is read (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenWithActivity.kt:20)
- [x] When the intent has a file and this is a fresh create (no saved state), it keeps access to the file, then starts MainActivity; with no file it starts nothing; in every case it finishes at once (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenWithActivity.kt:17-27) (iPhone: the file's address reaches the running app, which first holds the right to read it)
- [x] The hand-on intent is explicit for MainActivity, action "app.winters.octo.OPEN_FILE", data = the file's URI, flags NEW_TASK, SINGLE_TOP and GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION, so the main screen runs in Octo's own task (never a second copy inside the other app's task) and holds the right to read the file while it is open (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenWithActivity.kt:10-16, 29-35) (iPhone: the file's address reaches the running app through onOpenURL)
- [x] Keeping access: only when the other app's intent carries FLAG_GRANT_PERSISTABLE_URI_PERMISSION does Octo take a persistable read permission on the URI; if that throws, a log line "could not keep access to an opened file" is written and the file still opens (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:52-61) (iPhone: the right to read a file opened in place is held for as long as the app runs; a copy handed over needs none)
- [x] Without a persistable grant, the file plays only while the hand-over's temporary access lasts (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:52-53) (iPhone: that right lapses when the app quits, so a queue brought back later drops the file)
- [x] No temporary copy of the file is made; Octo plays straight from the URI it was handed (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:43-50)

### In the main screen

- [x] SystemEntries treats an intent as an opened file only when its action is "app.winters.octo.OPEN_FILE" and it has data (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:37) (iPhone: a file address opened with the app is an opened file)
- [x] The same Recents rule applies: an opened-file intent handed over again from Recents is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:35) (iPhone: iOS never hands an opened file a second time)
- [x] It waits until the screen is resumed, then works out what to play (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:39-42) (iPhone: works once the scene is up)
- [x] When the file cannot be opened at all, the feedback pill shows "Octo cannot open that file" and the player is not opened (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:76-81)
- [x] Otherwise the queue is replaced by that one song (playTracks with a single id: shuffle switched off, start at it, source word "list", prepare, play) and the full player opens (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt:83-84, 42-43; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:142-152)

### Which song a file is

- [x] All of the matching runs off the main thread (IO dispatcher) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:69-70)
- [x] Step 1: when the URI carries the phone media index's own id, that id is looked up in the library; a match plays the library song itself (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:71) (iPhone: a file inside Octo's own folder carries its library id in its place there, and is looked up by it)
- [~] URIs that carry the media index id (whole string must match): "content://media/<any volume>/audio/media/<digits>" and "content://com.android.providers.media.documents/document/audio:<digits>" or with the colon written "%3A" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:28-37) (not on iPhone: iOS hands opened files as file addresses, never with a music library id)
- [~] Media index id examples: content://media/external/audio/media/42 gives 42; content://media/external_primary/audio/media/7 gives 7; content://com.android.providers.media.documents/document/audio%3A1234 and .../audio:1234 give 1234 (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt:8-14) (not on iPhone: iOS hands opened files as file addresses, never with a music library id)
- [~] No media index id for: content://media/external/images/media/42, content://media/external/audio/media/42/albumart, content://com.android.providers.downloads.documents/document/42, file:///sdcard/Music/song.mp3 (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt:16-22) (not on iPhone: iOS hands opened files as file addresses, never with a music library id)
- [x] The library lookup finds the source song of source "device" whose native id is the media index id, and plays the library song it is merged into (or the source row's own id when it has no merged id); the first match wins (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:78-79; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/SourceDao.kt:59-67; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/device/DeviceCatalogBuilder.kt:13) (iPhone: the phone song with that id, from Octo's folder or the Music app, and the library song it is merged into)
- [x] A media index id that is not in the library (for example a file in a folder the library leaves out) falls through to the next steps (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:71-73) (iPhone: a file in Octo's folder whose place the library does not hold, for example a folder switched off, falls through the same way)
- [x] Step 2, what the file says about itself: for a "file" URI, the name is the file's name and the size its length when above 0; for any other URI, the name and size are asked of the app that shared it (DISPLAY_NAME and SIZE), a missing size is none, and any failure leaves both unknown (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:81-102) (iPhone: every opened file arrives as a file address; its name and size are read from the file itself)
- [x] The file's tags are read for title, artist and length in ms; each text is trimmed and an empty one counts as none; a file whose tags cannot be read has none (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:103-113)
- [x] Step 3: when the file's name is known, the phone's music index is searched for songs with exactly that file name (DISPLAY_NAME equals the name), reading each one's id, length and size; without permission to read the phone's music this step is skipped (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:116-139) (iPhone: the phone's songs from the last library scan: Octo's folder always, the Music app's songs once Octo may read them, which go by their title and type)
- [x] Choosing among same-named songs: a candidate fits when both its length and the file's tag length are known and are at most 1,500 ms apart; when either length is unknown it fits only if the file's size is known and equal to the candidate's size (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:21-23, 39-45)
- [x] Among the fitting candidates, the first one with the same size wins, else the first fitting one; none fitting means no match (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:46)
- [x] Matching examples: candidates (id 1, 200,000 ms, 5,000 bytes) and (id 2, 180,900 ms, 4,000 bytes) with a file of 180,000 ms and unknown size pick 2; a file of 150,000 ms picks none (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt:24-33)
- [x] Tie examples: candidates (1, 180,000 ms, 5,000 bytes) and (2, 180,000 ms, 4,000 bytes) with a file of 180,200 ms and 4,000 bytes pick 2; with unknown size they pick 1 (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt:35-43)
- [x] No-length examples: a candidate (3, unknown length, 9,000 bytes) is picked for a 100,000 ms file of 9,000 bytes, not for 8,000 bytes, and not when the file's length and size are both unknown (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt:45-51)
- [x] A same-named match is looked up in the library the same way as step 1; a match not in the library falls through (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:73)
- [x] Step 4: if the file cannot be read right now, the file cannot be opened (the "Octo cannot open that file" case) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:69, 74)
- [x] "Can be read" is tested by trying, not guessing: a "file" URI must be a readable file; a "content" URI must open for reading (any exception, refused or gone, means no); any other scheme means no (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:62-74) (iPhone: the file is opened for reading; anything but a file address is no)
- [x] Step 5: the file plays as itself, under an opened-file id built from the URI's text, a title and an artist (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:75)
- [x] The title stored is the tag title, else the file name without its last extension, else empty; the artist stored is the tag artist, else empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:49-50, 75)
- [x] File name to title: everything before the last "." trimmed, empty means none: "A song.mp3" gives "A song", "v1.2 mix.flac" gives "v1.2 mix", "plain" gives "plain", ".mp3" gives none, no name gives none (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt:49-50; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt:53-60)

### The opened-file id

- [x] Format: "file:" + url-encoded address + ":" + url-encoded title + ":" + url-encoded artist, each part trimmed before encoding, with UTF-8 form encoding (so ":" becomes %3A and the three parts split cleanly) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:14-30, 76)
- [x] Any id starting with "file:" is an opened file (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:22-24)
- [x] Reading an id back: split the rest on ":", decode each part (a part that fails to decode reads as empty); a blank address means it is not a playable file; a missing title or artist reads as empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:32-38, 78)
- [x] Id examples: a content URI with "%3A" and "%20" in it, title "A song: live", artist "The Band" round-trips exactly; " content://a/b " with no title and artist reads back as address "content://a/b", empty title, empty artist; "file::title:artist" is not a file; "radio:...", "device:12" and "find:abc" are not files (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItemsTest.kt:10-36)
- [x] Nothing about an opened file is stored anywhere but its id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:14-17)

### Playing an opened file

- [x] Opened-file ids are never looked up in the library; each is turned into a playable item at the moment it is queued (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:59, 64-65)
- [x] At that moment the file must still be readable; if not (the other app took its permission back, or the file is gone) it is quietly dropped, which also drops it from a queue brought back later (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:40-46)
- [x] The item's title is the stored title, else the URI's last path segment, else "Audio file" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:52)
- [x] The item's artist is the stored artist, or none when empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:53)
- [x] The item is playable, not browsable, type music, and has no artwork, no listed length and no MIME type (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:47-59)
- [x] The notification, lock screen and app show that title and artist (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt:50-58) (iPhone: the lock screen and Control Center show that title and artist)
- [x] The notification's heart shows for an opened file as an unfilled "Like", but tapping it does nothing, since a like needs a library song (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeart.kt:15-17; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt:56-63) (iPhone: the car's heart shows unfilled for an opened file, and tapping it does nothing)
- [x] With no listed length, its length comes from the player once the phone's player opens it, like any song, and that measured length is what plays are counted by (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:352-357)
- [x] ListenBrainz receives an opened file with its stored title and artist, no album, as a play that does not reach the server; radio stations are left out entirely (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/ListenBrainzSync.kt:202-208)
- [~] While casting, opened files are skipped, and once per cast the pill says "Files opened from other apps are skipped while casting" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/output/CastSources.kt:39; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/output/Casting.kt:136-144) (not on iPhone: the owner dropped casting; AirPlay plays opened files like any song)
- [x] An opened file that fails while playing shows "Couldn't play <title>. <why>" when it is the only song (or every song has failed, or there is no next song), using the shared failure words (for example "Octo can't play that kind of file." or "That song's file is damaged.") (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:480-488; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailures.kt:3-18)
- [x] A file reported as not found while playing gets the "Missing" words "That song isn't on the server any more.", the same as a server song (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailureWords.kt:12; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailures.kt:5)

## Deep links and intents

### MainActivity's intent filters

- [x] MainActivity is exported and singleTop (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:58-61) (iPhone: one scene)
- [x] Filter: action MAIN, category LAUNCHER; a plain launch opens the app on the first tab, Home, with no player opened (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:62-65; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:137-143) (iPhone: a plain launch opens on Home with no player, unless iOS brings the scene back as it was left)
- [x] Filter: action android.media.action.MEDIA_PLAY_FROM_SEARCH, category DEFAULT, for "Play something on Octo" asked of the voice assistant (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:66-70) (iPhone: Siri's play media requests and the App Intents)
- [x] Play from search reads only the spoken text (the SearchManager.QUERY extra, "query"; missing reads as empty); the assistant's focus extras (artist, album, title) are ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:114-118) (iPhone: Siri hands the name, artist, album and kind, and the voice rules use them)
- [x] Play from search replaces the queue with one request carrying the search text, then prepare and play; the service decides what that means (see Media session, voice requests) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:182-189)
- [x] Play from search does not open the full player (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:114-118)
- [x] Play from search has no Recents check: it runs on every fresh create without saved state and on every new intent with that action (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:61-62, 72, 115-116) (iPhone: iOS hands each request once)
- [x] Filter: action VIEW, categories DEFAULT and BROWSABLE, scheme "octo" with host "join", "signin" or "handover" (family links from a QR code the camera read or a link tapped) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:71-83) (iPhone: the octo URL scheme)
- [x] A VIEW intent is read as a family link from its full data string; a family intent handed over again from Recents is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:94-104) (iPhone: an opened octo:// address is read as a family link; iOS never replays it)
- [x] A valid family link opens the Settings tab (the last tab) and pushes the family join page, carrying the link rewritten in Octo's own "octo://" form (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:81-87; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:167-181; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/Routes.kt:83-84) (iPhone: a family link opens the join form as a full screen cover from its octo:// form)
- [x] Family link reading: spaces around the text are ignored and the "octo://join", "octo://signin", "octo://handover" and "intent://handover" prefixes match regardless of case (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:53-66)
