- [x] octo://join needs non-empty "server" and "invite" query values ("home" optional); otherwise it is not a link (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:68-73)
- [x] octo://signin needs a "server" value that reads as a server address ("username" and "home" optional); otherwise it is not a link (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:75-79)
- [x] octo://handover (and the browser's intent://handover...#Intent;scheme=octo;...;end form) needs "t" (token), "k" (key) and an "s" address; "h" (home) and "u" (username) are optional (subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt:16-17, 60-62, 96-103)
- [x] A VIEW intent that is not a valid family link does nothing and shows nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:101-102)
- [x] Action "app.winters.octo.OPEN_FAMILY" (sent by a tapped family notice, single-top) opens the Settings tab and pushes the Family page (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:100; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyNotices.kt:56-57, 73-78; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:177) (iPhone: a tapped family notice opens Family through the notification delegate)
- [x] Each time Octo's window gains focus, the clipboard's first text item is read; a family link not offered before shows the pill "A family invite is on the clipboard" (an invite) or "A family sign-in link is on the clipboard" (any other kind), with the action "Use it" that opens the join page; the pill stays 6 seconds (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:85-92; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/family/FamilyHub.kt:89-101; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/common/Feedback.kt:56-57) (iPhone: checked each time the scene becomes active, after iOS's own pattern check; the line stays 6 seconds)
- [x] A widget's artwork or title tap opens MainActivity with the boolean extra "open_player" = true, which opens the full player; the same intent handed over from Recents does not (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:106-112; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetIntents.kt:12-17, 39-50) (iPhone: a widget's artwork or title opens Octo with octo://player, which opens the full player)
- [x] An ACTION_SEND intent handed on by ShareToImportActivity offers the shared list to Import, which opens the Settings tab and pushes the Import page unless it is already on top; a Recents re-delivery is ignored (Import itself is covered elsewhere) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:79-83; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:182-189) (iPhone: Share to Octo leaves the list in the shared inbox and opens octo://import, which offers it to Import on the Settings tab unless Import is already on top)
- [x] The order every intent is checked in: play from search, open player, family, shared import, then shortcuts and opened files (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:61-67, 70-77) (iPhone: each address kind is told apart by its form)
- [x] None of the intent checks run again when the activity is re-created with saved state (rotation) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:60-61) (iPhone: iOS hands each address once)
- [x] No link opens an album, artist, playlist or song page; the only pages reachable from outside the app are the Family page, the family join page and Import (plus the full player) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/Routes.kt:1-97; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/nav/MainShell.kt:167-205)

### Other manifest entries

- [x] ShareToImportActivity exists for "Share to Octo" of a list: exported, excluded from Recents, no history, label "Octo", Theme.NoDisplay; filter action SEND, category DEFAULT, MIME text/plain, text/csv, text/comma-separated-values (behavior covered by the Import part) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:86-103; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/ShareToImportActivity.kt:10-37) (iPhone: the share extension)
- [x] The playback service OctoPlaybackService is exported, foregroundServiceType "mediaPlayback", with intent filter actions androidx.media3.session.MediaLibraryService and android.media.browse.MediaBrowserService (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:149-157) (iPhone: MPNowPlayingInfoCenter and MPRemoteCommandCenter in the app, and a CarPlay scene)
- [x] The artwork provider ArtworkProvider has authority "<applicationId>.art", is not exported and grants URI permissions per item (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:159-164) (iPhone: CarPlay is handed each cover as an image, so no provider is needed)
- [~] Android Auto: meta-data com.google.android.gms.car.application points at automotive_app_desc, which declares uses name="media" (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:172-175; app/src/main/res/xml/automotive_app_desc.xml:1-4) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; the CarPlay scene is declared and built in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] Google Cast: meta-data OPTIONS_PROVIDER_CLASS_NAME = app.winters.octo.cast.CastSetup (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:166-170) (not on iPhone: the owner dropped Chromecast; AirPlay is the way out to other speakers)
- [x] Media3's MediaButtonReceiver is exported with filter action android.intent.action.MEDIA_BUTTON, for headphone buttons and "resume" in the media controls (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:206-213) (iPhone: MPRemoteCommandCenter takes headphone buttons and resume from the system's controls)
- [x] The two home screen widget receivers (NowPlayingWidget labeled "Now playing", QuickPlayWidget labeled "Quick play") are not exported and filter APPWIDGET_UPDATE (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:177-199) (iPhone: a WidgetKit extension with Now playing and Quick play)
- [~] UpdateInstallReceiver is declared, not exported, with no filter (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:201-204) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install its own update)
- [~] Package visibility query: intents with action android.media.action.DISPLAY_AUDIO_EFFECT_CONTROL_PANEL (the phone's own equalizer) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:43-48) (not on iPhone: iOS has no system equalizer panel for apps; Octo's own Sound page is the equalizer)
- [x] Permissions tied to these entries: FOREGROUND_SERVICE and FOREGROUND_SERVICE_MEDIA_PLAYBACK (playing with the screen off), WAKE_LOCK, POST_NOTIFICATIONS, READ_MEDIA_AUDIO and READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE up to API 32 (the phone's music, also used to match opened files) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:5-41) (iPhone: the background audio mode, notification permission, and the media library usage line)

### Other MainActivity system hooks

- [x] While casting, the hardware volume keys step the TV or speaker's volume up or down and are consumed (key up is consumed too); otherwise they behave normally (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:120-131; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:199-206) (iPhone: with AirPlay the side buttons set the speaker's volume, as iOS does)
- [x] Coming to the front connects the app to the playback service and checks the server for a queue left on another device (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:133-138)
- [~] Leaving (onStop, not a configuration change) tells updates the app is being left (with whether music is playing) and lets go of the playback service connection, so the service can stop when nothing plays (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/MainActivity.kt:140-147; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaybackConnection.kt:126-133) (not on iPhone: an iPhone app cannot install its own update, and the player stays in the app as iOS allows)

## Media notification and media session

### Session setup

- [x] Playback runs in OctoPlaybackService, a Media3 MediaLibraryService holding one MediaLibrarySession over the session player (the phone's own two-deck player, or while casting the player that drives the TV or speaker) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:99-103, 176-191) (iPhone: the app's own two-deck engine with MPNowPlayingInfoCenter and MPRemoteCommandCenter; AirPlay takes the same player out)
- [x] The notification is Media3's default media notification (Octo sets no notification provider of its own): content title = the song's title, text = its artist, large picture = its artwork, small icon and channel = Media3's defaults (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:191-201) (iPhone: the system's Now Playing on the lock screen and in Control Center, with title, artist and artwork)
- [x] Tapping the notification (the session activity) opens MainActivity (request code 0, immutable, update-current) with no extras: the app as it was, not the full player (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:193-200) (iPhone: tapping Now Playing opens Octo as it was)
- [x] Artwork for the notification and lock screen is loaded through a caching loader: "octo-art:<artwork ref>" addresses are turned into a picture about 512 px wide off the main thread; a song with no picture shows none; any other address uses Media3's standard loader (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:192; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/MediaItems.kt:21, 44-46, 101-118; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ArtworkBitmap.kt:15-30) (iPhone: artwork drawn about 512 px wide off the main thread from the cover cache; none for a song without one)
- [x] A library song's session metadata: title, artist, album title, track number, disc number, release year, listed length, artwork address "octo-art:<ref>", playable, not browsable, type music (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/MediaItems.kt:48-71) (iPhone: the lock screen's fields: title, artist, album, track and disc number, length and artwork)
- [x] An explicit song carries Android's explicit key "android.media.IS_EXPLICIT" = 1, so a car's screen marks it too (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/MediaItems.kt:33-36, 79)
- [x] A radio station's metadata: title = the station's name, or its stream address when it has no name; artist "Radio"; type radio station (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItems.kt:31-47)

### Notification buttons

- [x] Previous, Play/Pause and Next keep their standard places; Octo adds at most two buttons: the heart (or the download that stands in for it) first, then Close (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:293-329) (iPhone: the lock screen keeps Apple's Previous, Play or Pause and Next; the heart or download goes on CarPlay's now playing screen)
- [x] The heart (or download) is offered for slot FORWARD_SECONDARY, then OVERFLOW; Close for slot BACK_SECONDARY, then OVERFLOW; so controls that know Media3's slots put the heart beside Next and Close beside Previous, and the phone's own media controls show both after Previous, Play and Next (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:293-298, 306, 314, 324) (iPhone: iOS places its own lock screen buttons; the heart goes on the car's now playing screen)
- [x] The buttons are set as the session's media button preferences (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:328) (iPhone: set as remote commands and the car's now playing buttons)
- [x] Like button for a song not liked: Media3's ICON_HEART_UNFILLED, display name "Like", session command "app.winters.octo.LIKE" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:302-308) (iPhone: a car now playing button with the SF Symbol heart)
- [x] Like button for a liked song: ICON_HEART_FILLED, display name "Unlike", same command (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:303-305) (iPhone: the same button with heart.fill)
- [x] Download button (for a song found online): custom icon sym_add (white plus glyph), display name "Add to your library", session command "app.winters.octo.DOWNLOAD" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:309-316; design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/OctoIcons.kt:52) (iPhone: CarPlay's own Add to Library button stands in for the download)
- [x] Close button: custom icon sym_close, display name "Close", session command "app.winters.octo.CLOSE"; it is always present (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:319-326; design/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/design/OctoIcons.kt:86) (iPhone: Close is the stop remote command; iOS draws no custom button for it)
- [x] Which heart shows: no song or a radio station shows neither heart nor download; a song that is not a find (library song, or an opened file) shows the heart, filled when its id is in the liked set; a find shows the download while its download state is None; a find whose download is asked for or done shows neither (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeart.kt:6-20; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeartTest.kt:10-27)
- [x] A find's download state is None when never asked or when the download failed (so the button comes back after a failure); queued, searching, downloading and being added count as asked for; arrived counts as done (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/discovery/DownloadProgress.kt:37-52, 99-104)
- [x] The buttons are rebuilt whenever the current song's id, the liked set or the download states change, and only republished when the result differs (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:213-217) (iPhone: worked out on each publish; the car's buttons change only when the result differs)
- [x] Before any song is current, only Close is offered (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:145, 215-217; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeart.kt:16)

### Custom session commands

- [x] Command names (each with empty extras): "app.winters.octo.LIKE", "app.winters.octo.DOWNLOAD", "app.winters.octo.CLOSE", and "app.winters.octo.PLAY_NEXT" whose arguments carry the song ids as a string list under "ids" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:79-89) (iPhone: remote commands like, bookmark (download) and stop (close); the app's own menus play next directly)
- [x] LIKE toggles the like of the song on now, unless it is a find (then nothing) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:574)
- [x] The like toggle: a liked song is unliked and the change is passed to listening sync; a song not liked is liked only if the library has it (saved with its relink key and the current time) and the change is passed on; a song the library does not have stays as it is (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt:56-67)
- [x] DOWNLOAD asks the server to download the find on now: it reads the stored find, and asks the signed-in server for it (starring the server id without "find:", marking it asked for now); with no server, or a server error, nothing changes, a log line "notification download: not asked" is written and no message shows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:368-375; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/discovery/Downloads.kt:210-227)
- [x] Once asked, the download state becomes asked-for, so the Download button disappears and only Close remains (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeart.kt:18-19; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:215-217)
- [x] CLOSE: saves the queue, cancels the sleep timer, ends casting if casting, marks closed, sets the notification to never show for a stopped player, pauses, stops, then pauses every player and stops the service; the queue itself is kept for next time (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:331-344) (iPhone: the stop remote command closes: the queue is kept, the timer cancelled, the music stopped and Now Playing cleared)
- [x] After Close the notification goes away; the service stops once the app also lets go of it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:331-332, 343) (iPhone: after Close nothing shows on the lock screen)
- [x] When something plays again after Close (the player leaves the idle state), the normal rule returns: a stopped or failed player keeps its notification (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:150-151, 503-507)
- [x] PLAY_NEXT turns the ids into playable songs (dropping any gone), marks them as the listener's own picks (heading "Next from you") and puts them right after the song on now, in shuffle order too (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:577-579; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:220-233) (iPhone: the app's Play next puts the songs right after the song on now, as the listener's own)
- [x] Any other custom command gets Media3's default answer (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:580) (iPhone: other remote commands are left to iOS)
- [x] LIKE, DOWNLOAD, CLOSE and PLAY_NEXT answer success at once, before their work finishes (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:582) (iPhone: remote commands answer at once)

### Who may control the session

- [x] Trusted controllers (the app itself, the system) and three car or voice apps get Octo's commands: com.google.android.projection.gearhead, com.google.android.carassistant, com.google.android.googlequicksearchbox (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:91-97, 552) (iPhone: iOS decides which controllers see Now Playing; every one gets the same commands)
- [x] Any other app gets Media3's default connection, answered at once, without Octo's four custom commands (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:550-552) (iPhone: iOS decides which controllers see Now Playing; every one gets the same commands)
- [x] A trusted or car app's connection is held until last session's queue is restored, so it never sees a player still restoring as empty; it then gets Media3's default session and library commands plus LIKE, DOWNLOAD, CLOSE and PLAY_NEXT (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:553-564) (iPhone: the play command answers no item only once the saved queue has finished coming back)
- [x] No rating support: Octo does not handle Media3's set-rating call; liking from outside the app goes only through the LIKE command (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:545-687)
- [x] Skip, previous, seek, play and pause from the notification, lock screen, headphones or a car use Media3's standard handling on the player; any seek or skip first ends a crossfade in progress (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:185-190)

### Requests to play (set items), including voice

- [x] A single request that carries a search query (a voice request such as "play Drake on Octo", from the app's play-from-search or from the assistant or car) is answered by the voice rules, even when the query is empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:603-606) (iPhone: Siri's play media requests and App Intents carry the words; the voice rules answer them, even with no words)
- [x] Voice rules: the query is trimmed; empty plays every library song (ordered by sortKey) shuffled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:87-92)
- [x] Voice rules: an album named exactly wins first: among up to 5 albums whose search key contains the query (ordered by sortKey), the first whose title's match key equals the query's match key (case, accents, punctuation, "&" as "and" and bracketed extras like "(Deluxe)" ignored; an empty key never matches); it plays the album's songs in album order, not shuffled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:93-95; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogMerger.kt:277-284)
- [x] Voice rules: then an artist named exactly: among up to 5 artists whose search key contains the query, the first whose name is the same artist as the query (case, accents, a leading "The", stylized characters and aliases ignored, never split into guests); it plays all of the artist's albums (newest year first, then sortKey), each in album order, shuffled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:96-100; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/catalog/SongIdentity.kt:1101-1110)
- [x] Voice rules: otherwise up to 50 songs whose search key contains the query (ordered by sortKey), not shuffled (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:101; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:132-133)
- [x] Voice rules search only the library, never songs found online (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:90-102)
- [x] A voice answer sets the shuffle switch to the rule's value, drops songs that cannot play, and starts at a random song when shuffled (else the first), position 0; nothing matching means an empty queue and no message (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:606-610)
- [x] Songs started by voice carry no source word, so the queue heads them "Up next" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:607-610; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/QueueSources.kt:66-73)
- [~] A single request whose id is a car id (an album, a playlist, Liked songs, or a song inside one) plays the whole list from the chosen song: songs that cannot play are dropped and the start stays on the chosen song by its place (first song when it is gone); position 0; the shuffle switch is left as it is (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:612-616; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt:29-34) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; in CarPlay.swift a list plays from the chosen song with shuffle as it was)
- [x] Any other request (the app's own) plays the ids asked for, dropping any gone; the source word on the first request (the list's name) is put on every song; a given start index is moved to stay on the chosen song by its place (first song when gone), an unset start index stays unset, and the start position is passed through (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:617-623)
- [~] Adding songs (add to queue, from the app or a car) turns each car id into its whole list of songs (an album, playlist or Liked songs; a song found inside a list also adds that whole list), keeps plain ids as they are, drops any gone, and marks all as the listener's own picks (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:585-594; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:68-76) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement to add songs from the car)

### Resuming from headphones or the system's media controls

- [x] A play press with nothing loaded (headphone button, or "resume" on the system's media controls) brings back the saved queue (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:626-643; app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:206-213) (iPhone: the play remote command with nothing loaded resumes the queue kept from last time)
- [x] With no saved queue, or none of its songs still playable, resumption fails with "Nothing to resume" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:631-634) (iPhone: nothing to resume answers that there is no item to play)
- [x] For playback, the whole queue (songs gone left out) comes back at the saved song and place, or the first song from its start when the saved song is gone (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:635-638; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt:120-127)
- [~] When the system only asks what to show on its resume card, just that one song and its place are given (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:639-642) (not on iPhone: iOS asks apps for no resume card; the saved song comes back when Octo starts)
- [x] Restore-point examples: all four saved songs present, saved index 2 at 17,192 ms gives index 2 at 17,192 ms; first of three gone gives index 1 at 17,192 ms; the saved song gone gives index 0 at 0 ms; a saved index past the end gives 0 at 0 ms (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/QueueRestoreTest.kt:9-28)

### The queue kept for next time

- [x] When the service starts it brings back last session's queue, paused where it was left: the songs still playable, at the saved song and place (or the first from its start), with the saved repeat mode, the saved shuffle order less the songs gone, and the saved shuffle switch; it is prepared but not played (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:203-212, 377-393; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt:129-137)
- [x] The restore is skipped when nothing saved can play or the player already has songs (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:383)
- [x] Whether crossfade is on is read before the queue is restored, since it decides how the decks' sound path is built (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:205-207) (iPhone: the engine's decks are set up before the queue comes back)
- [x] The queue is saved: on every song change; when playing stops (pause or end); every 15 seconds while playing; 500 ms after the queue's contents settle; when the app's task is swiped away; when the service is destroyed; and on Close (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:246, 268-271, 273-276, 334, 463-466, 511-513, 524-541) (iPhone: saved on every song change, on pause or end, every 15 seconds while playing, 500 ms after the queue settles, on going to the background, on ending and on Close)
- [x] What is saved: every song id in queue order, the shuffle order, the current index, the position, the repeat mode and the shuffle switch (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:448-457)
- [x] While a change of server is under way, the emptied player is never saved over the queue being handed over (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:130-132, 395-398, 401-423)
- [x] A change of server stops what plays (counting it), cancels the sleep timer, ends casting, empties the player, then brings back the other server's queue (or keeps the current one when the switch asks) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:400-446)

### Foreground service and lifetime

- [x] The service runs in the foreground as a media playback service while music plays (Media3 handles this) (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:20-22, 149-152) (iPhone: the background audio mode keeps it playing)
- [~] After a pause the service keeps its foreground place for 30 minutes when either headphone resume switch (wired or Bluetooth) is on, since Android may refuse to bring it back later; otherwise Media3's default foreground timeout (10 minutes) applies; this follows the switches live (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:235-244; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:14-16) (not on iPhone: iOS suspends a paused app in the background as it chooses; an app cannot hold its place longer)
- [x] Swiping the app's task away saves the queue, then Media3's default task-removed handling applies (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:268-271) (iPhone: going to the background saves the queue)
- [x] When the service is destroyed it saves the queue, clears the widgets, flushes the play being counted, stops headphone resume, the sleep timer, prefetching, the queue editor and Autoplay, ends casting, and releases the session, the player and the audio session (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:273-291) (iPhone: ending saves the queue, counts the play and cancels the sleep timer)
- [~] Equalizer apps can attach to the music once it plays on the phone (not when playing on another device); they are let go when the music stops or ends (not at a pause) or when it moves to another device (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:501-502, 529-531) (not on iPhone: iOS has no equalizer apps that attach to another app's audio)
- [x] Each deck keeps the phone awake while playing (local wake mode) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:41) (iPhone: the background audio mode keeps the phone playing)

### Errors while playing

- [x] An error while the music is not meant to be playing is ignored (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:481)
- [x] An error while playing counts one more failure in a row; the count goes back to 0 once a song is ready (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:460-461, 482, 497-498)
- [x] When every song in the queue has failed in a row, or there is no next song, the pill shows "Couldn't play <title>. <why>" and the music stops there (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:485-488; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailures.kt:18)
- [x] Otherwise the pill shows "Skipped <title>. <why>" and the player moves to the next song and prepares it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:489-493; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailures.kt:16)
- [x] <title> is the song's title, or "a song" when it has none (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:483)
- [x] <why> by error: HTTP 404 or 410 for a server song: "That song isn't on the server any more."; other bad HTTP status, or 404/410 for a find: "The server wouldn't send that song."; file not found: "That song isn't on the server any more."; connection failed, connection timeout or timeout: "Couldn't reach the server to play that song."; unsupported format, format beyond the device, unsupported container or manifest: "Octo can't play that kind of file."; malformed container or manifest, or decoding failed: "That song's file is damaged."; audio track init or write failed: "The sound device stopped working. Pick another output."; anything else: "That song couldn't play." (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailureWords.kt:6-33; shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailures.kt:3-12)

### Interruptions and headphones

- [x] Playing needs audio focus (media usage, music content); if the phone refuses (a call is on), the music stays paused (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:45-69, 175-183) (iPhone: AVAudioSession's playback category; a call pauses it, as iOS does)
- [x] Focus lost for good (another app plays): pause, give up focus, and do not come back by itself (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:155-160) (iPhone: an interruption that ends without resuming leaves it paused)
- [x] Focus lost for a while (a call or short interruption): pause, and play again when focus returns if it was playing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:161-171) (iPhone: an interruption that ends with shouldResume plays again if it was playing)
- [x] Focus lost with ducking allowed (a navigation prompt): volume drops to 0.2 until focus returns (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:71, 166-168, 321-333) (iPhone: iOS lowers Octo itself for a navigation prompt)
- [x] Any pause asked for (not caused by focus) cancels the come-back-after-interruption (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:178-181) (iPhone: a pause asked for clears the come-back after an interruption)
- [x] Headphones unplugged or Bluetooth audio dropped pauses the music (the output becoming noisy) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlayer.kt:40)
- [x] Headphone resume lives in the playback service and works only while the service is alive; connecting headphones never starts a stopped service, and it always brings back the phone's own player, never a TV or speaker (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:235-239; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:61-65) (iPhone: works while the app is alive; it always resumes the phone's own player)
- [x] Device kinds: wired headphones, wired headset and USB headset are Wired; Bluetooth A2DP, BLE headset and BLE speaker are Bluetooth; the speaker and Bluetooth for calls are neither (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:18-32; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResumeTest.kt:73-86)
- [x] Headphones play the music again only when: their kind's switch is on, nothing is playing, there is a queue, and either "always play on connect" is on or the music was paused by the output going away 0 to 30 minutes ago (a clock that went back does not count) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:34-53; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResumeTest.kt:22-71)
- [x] The wired, Bluetooth and always switches are all off by default (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/player/PlayerSettings.kt:73-75)
- [x] Only a pause caused by the output going away is remembered; any other pause, or playing again, forgets it (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:113-121)
- [x] Only outputs not already connected count as new; the first new headphone among those added is checked after the sound has had time to move (500 ms wired, 1,500 ms Bluetooth), and only if it is still connected; a newer connection replaces a pending check (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:55-104)
- [x] Resuming prepares the player first when it is idle, then plays, and logs "headphones: <kind> connected, playing again" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt:123-137)

## Android Auto browse tree

### Registration and ids

- [~] Android Auto finds Octo as a media app through the automotive_app_desc meta-data and the service's MediaBrowserService action (app/src/main/AndroidManifest.xml:153-157, 172-175) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; the CarPlay scene is declared in Info.plist and built in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] Node ids: root "root"; tabs "tab:recent", "tab:playlists", "tab:albums", "tab:artists"; Liked songs "liked"; most played "mostplayed"; album "album:<id>"; artist "artist:<id>"; playlist "playlist:<id>" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIds.kt:22-32) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarPlay rows carry their own actions, and the ids are kept in CarIds.swift)
- [~] A song's id is "song:<track id>", followed by "|in:<the list's id>" when it was found in a list (album, playlist, Liked songs or most played), so choosing it plays that list from that song (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIds.kt:3-4, 19-20, 33) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; a song row in CarPlay.swift plays the list it was found in from that song)
- [~] Reading ids back: exact matches for the fixed ids, prefixes for album, artist and playlist (the rest is the id), and for a song the part before the first "|in:" is the track id and the part after is read as a list id; anything else (such as the app's own song ids like "device:4054") is not a car id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIds.kt:36-59; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIdsTest.kt:10-31) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; the ids read back by CarIds.swift)
- [~] Every node id reads back as itself, including songs in an album, a playlist, Liked songs, most played, or no list (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIdsTest.kt:10-26) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarIds.swift reads every id back)
- [~] The start in a list is the chosen song's first position, or the top when it is no longer there (or none is chosen) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIds.kt:61-64; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIdsTest.kt:33-39) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; the start is the chosen song's place in CarPlay.swift)

### Root and tabs

- [~] The root is a folder titled "Octo" with id "root", browsable, not playable; it is given the same for every request (Android's recent or suggested root hints are not treated differently) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:36; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:645-649) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarPlay.swift's root is a tab bar)
- [~] The root's children, in order: "Recent", "Playlists", "Albums", "Artists"; each a folder (browsable, not playable, no artwork, no media type) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:41-46, 119-126) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; tabs Recent, Playlists, Albums, Artists in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] Albums is the only tab whose contents show as a grid of covers (grid style for both browsable and playable items); Recent, Playlists and Artists show as lists (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:44, 180-185) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarPlay audio apps show lists only)

### Recent

- [~] Recent lists up to 20 albums by their latest play, newest first (a tie goes by album id), under the heading "Recently played", then up to 20 songs by play count, under the heading "Most played" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:47-52, 186-191) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; Recently played and Most played sections in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] Most played order: more plays first; a tie goes to the more recently played; then by song id (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/Plays.kt:31-41; app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/PlaysTest.kt:35-58) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; most played order comes from the library's play counts)
- [~] Album order examples: last plays 100, 300, 200 for a, b, c list b, c, a; 30 albums stop at 20 with the newest first (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/PlaysTest.kt:21-33) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; albums by latest play in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] Plays counted are the phone's and the server's together (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/listening/PlayHistory.kt:11-19) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; plays counted from the phone and the server)
- [~] Nothing played gives an empty Recent with no words (app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/PlaysTest.kt:60-64; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:47-52) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; an empty Recent shows nothing)
- [~] A Most played song is found in the most played list, so choosing it plays the current top 20 most played from that song (the top if it has dropped out) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:51, 69-74, 115) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; a most played song plays the top 20 from it in CarPlay.swift)

### Playlists

- [~] Playlists starts with "Liked songs" (subtitle "<count> songs", no cover, browsable and playable) only when at least one liked song is still in the library; with none, the row is not there (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:53-56; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:37-38) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; Liked songs first when there are likes, in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] The subtitle never uses a singular: one liked song reads "1 songs", and a one-song playlist reads "1 songs" (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:56-57) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarPlay.swift says 1 song)
- [~] Then every playlist shown with the server in use (the phone's own, and those kept with the server in use), the one changed last first: title = its name, subtitle "<song count> songs", cover = the first album cover among its songs in play order (songs with no cover skipped) (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:54, 57; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlaylistStore.kt:35-41; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:101-104; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/Playlists.kt:40-71) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; playlists with their song counts and covers in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] A playlist's song count includes its songs found online (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:109-125) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; counts include songs found online)
- [~] Playlist and Liked songs rows are browsable and playable, type playlist (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:151-160) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; playlist rows play their songs in CarPlay.swift)

### Albums, Artists and their contents

- [~] Albums lists every album in the library by sortKey, with no limit beyond paging (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:59; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:26-27) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; every album, as many as CarPlay lists)
- [~] Artists lists every artist in the library by sortKey (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:60; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:29-30) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; every artist, as many as CarPlay lists)
- [~] An artist row: title = the name, no subtitle, no artwork, browsable but not playable, type artist, and its albums show as a grid (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:141-149) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; an artist opens their albums in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] An artist's children are its albums, newest year first (no year last), then by sortKey (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:61; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:98-99) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; an artist's albums as the library orders them)
- [~] An album row: title = album title, artist and subtitle = the album's artist, cover, browsable and playable, type album; a heading only inside Recent (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:128-139) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; album rows with artist and cover in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] An album's children are its songs in album order (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:62, 112; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:70-71) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; an album plays its songs in album order)
- [~] A playlist's children are its songs in play order, songs found online included (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:62, 113; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:127-152) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; a playlist plays its songs in play order)
- [~] Liked songs' children are the liked songs the library still has, newest like first (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:62, 114; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/UserDao.kt:33-35) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; Liked songs play newest like first)
- [~] A song row: title, artist and subtitle = the song's artist, album title, listed length, cover, playable, not browsable, type music; it remembers the list it was found in; a heading only inside Recent; no explicit mark on browse rows (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:162-175) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; song rows with title, artist and cover in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] The most played node, a song node, and any unknown id have no children (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:40, 63) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement)
- [~] Children are given a page at a time: skip page times page size, take page size (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:65) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarPlay takes as many rows as it lists)
- [~] Every list is read fresh from the library at the moment the car asks (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:39-66) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; every tab is read fresh from the library in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] No empty-state rows or words exist anywhere in the tree; an empty list is just empty (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:39-66) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; empty lists stay empty)

### Artwork for the car

- [~] Covers in the tree are content addresses "content://<applicationId>.art/<artwork ref>" served by ArtworkProvider (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ArtworkProvider.kt:57-62; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:134, 156, 170) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarPlay is handed each cover as an image)
- [~] Each time children or search results are sent, the asking app is granted read access to every cover address in them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:104-109; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:660-661, 683-684) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; no content address to grant)
- [~] The provider makes each cover once as a JPEG (quality 88) no larger than 320 px on its longest side, keeps it in the cache folder "car-art" named by the SHA-1 of the ref, writes it to a ".part" file first and renames it, so a half-written file is never served; type image/jpeg; no picture means "No artwork" (file not found); it is read-only (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ArtworkProvider.kt:15-67) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; covers drawn about 320 px from the cover cache in CarPlay.swift)

### Playing from the car

- [~] Choosing an album plays all its songs from the first (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:68-70) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; choosing an album plays it from the first song in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] Choosing a playlist or Liked songs plays all its songs from the first (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:70) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; choosing a playlist or Liked songs plays it from the first song)
- [~] Choosing a song plays the list it was found in from that song; a song with no list (from search) plays alone (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:71-74) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; a song plays its list from that song in CarPlay.swift)
- [~] Artists and tabs cannot be played (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:75) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; artists open their albums and tabs only list)
- [~] Songs played from the car carry no source word (queue heading "Up next"), and the shuffle switch is left as it was (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:612-616) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; shuffle is left as it was)

### Search

- [~] Search answers with the number of results first, then pages of them (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:665-686) (not on iPhone: CarPlay gives audio apps no search; Siri's requests use the voice rules instead)
- [~] The query is trimmed; an empty query finds nothing (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:78-81) (not on iPhone: CarPlay gives audio apps no search; Siri's requests use the voice rules instead)
- [~] Results in order: up to 30 songs, then up to 15 albums, then up to 10 artists, each whose search key contains the query, each group by sortKey (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:82-84; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogDao.kt:115-119, 132-133) (not on iPhone: CarPlay gives audio apps no search; Siri's requests use the voice rules instead)
- [~] Song results are in no list, so choosing one plays just that song (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt:82) (not on iPhone: CarPlay gives audio apps no search; Siri's requests use the voice rules instead)

### Keeping the car up to date

- [~] Only after a change of server does the service tell the car to read again: root, Recent, Playlists, Albums and Artists, each with its full item count; nothing else (a rescan, a new like, a new playlist) notifies the car (app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OctoPlaybackService.kt:424-433; app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ServerSwitch.kt:230-240) (not on iPhone: needs Apple's carplay-audio entitlement; CarPlay.swift reads every tab again after a change of server)

Tests: `subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt` (aSignInLinkFillsInTheServerAndUsername, aHandOverLinkIsReadInBothFormsAndKeepsItsKey, anInviteLinkIsReadInBothForms, anInvitesHomeAddressIsReadInBothFormsAndThePathIsKept), `app/src/androidTest/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyLinkFilterTest.kt` (deep link filter).
- `subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Family.kt` lines 57-64 and 357-453: `FamilyPlatform` wire names, hand-over models, signed calls (start, pending, decide, put box) and open calls (redeem, redeem status). Tests: `subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt` (aHandOverIsStartedAndDecidedByOctoFamilyCalls, theNewDeviceRedeemsAndWaitsWithNoSignIn, aWrongTokenIsRefusedInPlainWords).
- `subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWeb.kt`: family page JSON calls, cookie jar, `join`, failure words by HTTP status. Tests: `subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyWebTest.kt` (anInviteIsAcceptedWithTheChosenPassword, errorsSayWhatHappened).
- `subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/ServerUrl.kt` and `ServerScheme.kt`: address normalizing, home-network test, scheme split and automatic scheme used when a link fills the address. Tests: none specific to family in the export (sign-in spec covers them).
- `shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyJoin.kt`: `JoinOutcome`, `inviteProblem`, `MIN_PASSWORD`/`MAX_PASSWORD`, `passwordStrength`, `joinWithInvite` with home fallback and error words. Tests: `shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt` (anInviteSignsUpWithTheChosenPassword, aUsedInviteSaysSo, passwordStrengthReadsPlainly, signingUpFallsBackToTheHomeAddressWhenTheOutsideOneCantBeReached).
- `shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyQr.kt`: QR making (ECC M), reading from ARGB and luminance with an inverted retry, `familyLinkInQr`. Tests: `shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyRulesTest.kt` (aLinkMakesAQrCodeThatReadsBack).
- `shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOver.kt`: `HandOverSignIn`, `HandOverBox` (XChaCha20-Poly1305, AAD "octo-family-signin-1"), step words, `receiveHandOver` (1.5 s polls, 130 s wait). Tests: `shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt` (aSealedSignInOpensWithItsKey, aWrongKeyFailsCleanly, theNewDeviceNeverSendsTheKey, aDeniedOrExpiredHandOverSaysSo, aBoxSealedWithAnotherKeyIsRefused), fake server `shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyFakeServer.kt`.
- `shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverSource.kt`: `HandOverSheet` state and `options`, `askingTitle`, sheet words, timing constants (5 s lead, 10 s retry, 10 min limit, 250 ms tick, 2 s ask), `HandOverSource` open/close/newCode/allow/deny/renew/watch. Tests: `shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt` (theSignedInDeviceSendsNothingBeforeAllow, denyingTellsTheServerAndShowsAFreshCode, theCodeRenewsBeforeItExpiresPausesWhenHiddenAndStopsAfterTenMinutes), `app/src/androidTest/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheetFitTest.kt`, `app/src/androidTest/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/ReachChooserTest.kt`, host `app/src/androidTest/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilySheetHosting.kt`.
- `shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopups.kt`: `expiresAtMs`, `secondsLeft`, `minutesSeconds`, `countdownLine`, `countdownWarns`, `countdownAnnouncement`, `middleEllipsized`, `shownLink`, `shownServer`, `linkServer`, `LinkReach`, `reachQuestion`, `reachLine`, `LinkOptions`, lock and status words, `dashboardStatusPage`, `COPIED_MS`, `NEW_CODE_MS`. Tests: `shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyPopupsTest.kt` (all tests), `app/src/androidTest/java/app/winters/octo/ui/family/ReachChooserTest.kt`.
- `shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModel.kt` line 77 and 159-172: the hand-over source's wiring (display name, clock, tick) and closing it when the account changes. Tests: `shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/FamilyModelTest.kt`.
- `shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/server/ServerList.kt` lines 61-65: `switchNotice` words shown after a join or hand-over sign-in. Tests: none family-specific in the export.
- `app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/connection/DeviceIds.kt` lines 60-75: the device name sent when redeeming (iPhone needs its own equivalent). Tests: `app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/connection/DeviceIdsTest.kt` (aModelNameReadsLikeThePhone).

### Shared logic to port line for line (admin and imports)

- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormat.kt: health dot choice, service names, time parsing and "ago" wording, learning line with plays plural and grouping, refresh problem cleanup, library problems, download line. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/AdminFormatTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/admin/OctoAdminViewModel.kt: places (Looking, Away, Found), fresh look vs pull refresh, generation counter, per section loading and failure words, station refresh follow-up (5 checks, 4,000 ms apart). Tests: none
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdmin.kt: finding the admin pages (candidate order, unsigned status probe with 4 s timeout, station hosts with 5 s wait), per server saved address and legacy key, trusted set and sign-in rule (`mayCarrySignIn`), admin read and change calls with their errors. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/admin/OctoAdminSignInTest.kt
- shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportModel.kt: Import state, polling (3,000 ms busy, 20,000 ms idle), services read once per server, Get my music steps, file and text sending with refusals, Spotify connect and cancel, every action and its words, open and close a list. Tests: shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicTest.kt
- shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusic.kt: Get my music constants and words, list file endings, steps and the tile a step lights, TuneMyMusic address allowlist, waiting words, refusal words, Spotify sign-in offer rule, approval line. Tests: shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicTest.kt
- shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImport.kt: poll intervals, "moving" rule, track state words, origin and counts lines, remove question and line, Spotify account heading and line, trickle heading and line, and the Spotify sign-in flow (server finishes, or loopback catch then finish). Tests: shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImportTest.kt
- subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/LoopbackCallback.kt: the loopback redirect rules, request parsing, and the three answer pages. Tests: covered through shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/SpotifyImportTest.kt
- subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/Imports.kt and the import calls in subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/SubsonicClient.kt (lines 205-249, 828-834): wire models, defaults, state words, action names, 64 MiB limit, file part types. Tests: subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/ImportsTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShare.kt: which shares Import takes, file over text, subject as name, accepted share and picker types, file naming by ending, the one shot inbox, the recents check. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShareTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/GetMyMusicItems.kt (`sendPickedFile`, `readAtMost`, `OpenInDownloads`): reading a picked or shared file up to the limit and the words for unreadable or too big files. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/ui/imports/ImportShareTest.kt

### Shared logic to port line for line (system)

- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/LauncherShortcuts.kt: the four shortcut ids, labels, icons and order, the action and extra names, and shortcutOf (which intent is which shortcut). Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/OpenedFiles.kt: mediaStoreIdOf (the two URI patterns), pickSameFile (1,500 ms rule, size tie-break), titleFromName, and the order of the matching steps in trackIdFor. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntriesTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/system/SystemEntries.kt: what each shortcut plays, the empty messages, and the opened-file flow. Tests: none beyond SystemEntriesTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItems.kt: the "file:" id format, its encoding, reading it back, the title fallback "Audio file", and canRead. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/OpenedFileItemsTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeart.kt: which of heart, download or nothing the notification shows. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/NotificationHeartTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/discovery/DownloadProgress.kt: DownloadPhase to DownloadState (failed counts as none). Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/discovery/DownloadProgressTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIds.kt: car node ids, reading them back, startIndex. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarIdsTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/CarLibrary.kt: the browse tree, limits, headings, playFor, search and the voice rules. Tests: none in the export
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/Plays.kt: byLatestPlay and byPlayCount (Recent tab order). Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/PlaysTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/Stores.kt: restorePoint and restoredShuffle (resume and restore), LikeStore.toggle. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/QueueRestoreTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayableSongs.kt: chosenStart (keeping the chosen song when songs before it are gone). Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/TappedFindTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResume.kt: headsetKind, shouldResumeOnConnect, RESUME_WINDOW_MS, settle delays. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/HeadsetResumeTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailureWords.kt and shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailures.kt: error to reason, reason words, skipped and stopped lines. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/PlayFailureWordsTest.kt
- shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/QueueSources.kt: source words ("you", "list", "list:<name>", "autoplay") and headings. Tests: shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/playback/QueueSourcesTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetCommand.kt and WidgetIntents.kt: the "play" command and request code the Resume shortcut sends. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/widget/WidgetFacesTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItems.kt: isRadio and a station's notification title and "Radio" artist. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/playback/RadioItemsTest.kt
- subsonic/src/main/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyLinks.kt: parseFamilyLink and familyAppLink for the octo:// links. Tests: subsonic/src/test/kotlin/app/winters/octo/subsonic/FamilyParsingTest.kt, shared/core/src/commonTest/kotlin/app/winters/octo/ui/family/HandOverTest.kt
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogMerger.kt (matchKey) and shared/core/src/commonMain/kotlin/app/winters/octo/catalog/SongIdentity.kt (sameArtistName): the exact-name tests in voice requests. Tests: app/src/test/java/app/winters/octo/catalog/CatalogMergerTest.kt (matchKeysIgnoreCasePunctuationAndBracketedExtras)
- app/src/main/java/app/winters/octo/playback/ArtworkProvider.kt: car cover size, quality, cache name and address. Tests: none in the export
